dvitīyo'dhyāyaḥ
kāśyapaḥ
bhagavan pañcarākhyaṃ śāstramastīti yattvayā  |
ākhyātaṃ tasya śabdasya ko'rthastatkiṃparaṃ mune  | | 2.1  | |
tasya rūpaṃ katividhaṃ kīṭṭaśo granthavistaraḥ  |
etatsarvaṃ yathātattvaṃ saṃkṣevapāt kathyatāṃ mama  | | 2.2  | |
viśvāmitraḥ
śrūyatāmabhidhāsyāmi yatpṛṣṭo'smi tvayā mune  |
pañcendriyāṇi viṣayāḥ pañcabhūtāni tadguṇāḥ  | | 2.3  | |
pañcaśabdābhidheyāni vidvāṃso'pyācacakṣire  |
rā ityayamapi prokto dhāturādānavācakaḥ  | | 2.4  | |
viṣayendriyabhūtānādātāraśca pañcarāḥ  |
manupyāḥ pālanātteṣāṃ pañcarātramiti smṛtam  | | 2.5  | |
sāṃkhyayogādayaḥ pañca [rātrā(trī ?)] yante'sya saṃnidhau  |
tasmādvā pañcarātrārthaḥ procyate sūrisattamaiḥ  | | 2.6  | |
athavā rātrayo yadvat[ saṃnidhau pañcatāya vai](?)  |
nīyante tadvanyāni śastrāṇyetasya saṃnidhau  | | 2.7  | |
iti vā pañcarātrārtho vidvadibhaḥ kathyate dvija  |
sarvavedāntavedyasya sarvadeveśvarasya ca  | | 2.8  | |
viṣṇorārādhanaparaṃ śāstrametat prakīrtitam  |
bhavārṇavanimagrānāṃ puṃsāṃ siddhikaraṃ param  | | 2.9  | |
vāsudevasya devasya parasya paramatmanaḥ  |
parasya parato jyoteḥ paratattvakharūpiṇaḥ  | | 2.10  | |
śāstraṃ bhagavato viṣṇoḥ mukhāmbujavinirgatam  |
pañcarātrākhyamamṛtaṃ puṇyaṃ pāpavināśanam  | | 2.11  | |
yathā sarveṣu deveṣu paro nārāyaṇaḥ smṛtaḥ  |
tathā sarveṣu śāstreṣu pañcarātraṃ paraṃ smūtam  | | 2.12  | |
pañcarātroktavidhinā yo'rcayet puruṣottamam  |
tasya sāṃsārikaṃ duḥkhaṃ na bhavennātra saṃśayaḥ  | | 2.13  | |
narāyaṇamukhācchrutvā yathāsvamativaibhavam  |
[avadhārya pravaktṝṇāṃ bhedo'nantasya karhicit]  | | 2.14  | |
[vidyante tatra bhedānāṃ(?) loke kaśyapanandana]  |
tathāpi tantrāṇadhunā prādhānyena smṛtāni vai  | | 2.15  | |
kathyante kānicidvipra śrṛṇupvāvahito mune  |
viṣṇusiddhāntamātrayaṃ paramā viśvasaṃhitā  | | 2.16  | |
sanatkumāraṃ satyākhyaṃ viśvāmitrīyamityapi  |
sā?hmaṃ jayā sāttvataṃ ca pauṣkaraṃ puruṣottamam  | | 1.17  | |
śrīpraśraṃ ca mahīpraśraṃ māyāvaibhavikaṃ tathā  |
māhendrasaṃhitā pañcapraśrākhyaṃ śrīkaraṃ tathā  | | 2.18  | |
pahmodabhavaṃ nāradīyaṃ nalūbaramityati  |
trailokyamohanaṃ tantraṃ vāsiṣṭhaṃ sanakaṃ tathā  | | 2.19  | |
ahirbudhnyaṃ cāndramasaṃ tattvasāgarasaṃjñitam  |
viṣvaksenaṃ vihaṅgendrasaṃhitā bhārgavaṃ tathā  | | 2.20  | |
pārameśvaramaupendraṃ mārīcaṃ dakṣasaṃhitā  |
śaunakīyaṃ puṣṭitantraṃ kapiñjalamataḥ param  | | 2.21  | |
jaiminaṃ vāmanaṃ tantraṃ brahmasiddhāntameva ca  |
vaiyāsaṃ caiva hairaṇyaṃ pārāśaryaṃ ca śāmbaram  | | 2.22  | |
vīramaṅgalikaṃ caiva prahlādaṃ mūlasaṃhitā  |
mārkaṇḍeyaṃ mahālakṣmīsaṃhitā kāpilaṃ tathā  | | 2.23  | |
brāhmaṃ nārāyaṇākhyaṃ ca yājñavalvayaṃ nṛkesari  |
sāṃvartaṃ śrīkaraṃ sauraṃ saumyaṃ bhāgavataṃ tathā  | | 2.24  | |
bārhaspatyaṃ jāmadamnyaṃ yāmyasaṃjñātmakaṃ tathā  |
viṣṇusadabhāvasaṃjñaṃ ca tejodraviṇamityapi  | | 2.25  | |
dhanadīyaṃ yaca kaumāraṃ viṣṇuvaibhavikaṃ tathā  |
viṣṇupūrvahasyaṃ ca tathaivānandasaṃjñitam  | | 2.26  | |
hārītaṃ yogahṛdayaṃ tathaivośanasāhvayam  |
parapauruṣasaṃjñaṃ ca paulastyaṃ gautamaṃ tathā  | | 2.27  | |
śāklayaṃ cāpi jābālaṃ kātyāyanamataḥ param  |
aupagāyanasaṃjñaṃ ca tathā vālmīkisaṃhitā  | | 2.28  | |
bodhāyanamathāgastyaṃ kāṣṇaryaṃ śātātapaṃ tathā  |
bhāradvājaṃ paiṅgalaṃ ca trailokyavijayaṃ tathā  | | 2.29  | |
āgneyaṃ vāruṇaṃ caindraṃ brahmanāradasaṃhitā  |
umāmaheśvaraṃ śukraṃ rudrākhyaṃ pārṣasaṃhitā  | | 2.30  | |
saṃkarṣaṇaṃ ca pradyugnaṃ nandākhyaṃ śarvasaṃhitā  |
prācetasaṃ rāghavaṃ ca kalkisaṃjñaṃ tathaiva ca  | | 2.31  | |
mahāsanatkumārākhyaṃ rudrākhyaṃ skandasaṃhitā  |
vārāhaṃ mihirākhyaṃ ca tathaivāmṛtasaṃhitā  | | 2.32  | |
ityevamuktaṃ tantrāṇāṃ śatamaṣṭottaraṃ mune  |
etāni tantranāmāni yojānāti sa muktibhāk  | | 2.33  | |
eteṣāṃ granthasaṃkhyā ca śatakoṭipramāṇataḥ  |
yugakramavaśādvipra vṛddhiṃ hāsaṃ ca gacchati  | | 2.34  | |
[brahmaṇā pūrvamākhyātamayutagranthasaṃkhyayā] (?)  |
tatpunarmatimāndyane [mayā pañcasahastrakaiḥ (?)]  | | 2.35  | |
jñātavyaṃ tāta vakṣyāmi saṃkṣepāt kaśyapātmaja  |
sahastrayamānena sugrahatvāya suvrata  | | 2.36  | |
etat śrutvā gurumukhāt dīkṣāmāsādya yatnataḥ  |
karṣaṇādipratiṣṭhāntaṃ sarvaṃ karma samācaret  | | 2.37  | |
iha loke'khilān bhogān putrapautrādibhiḥ saha  |
bhuktvānte paramaṃ divyaṃ padaṃ vaiṣṇavamāpnuyāt  | | 2.38  | |
 | | iti pañcarātre mahopaniṣadi viśvāmitrasaṃhitāyāṃ
[pañcarātranirvacanaṃ nāma] dvitīyo'dhyāyaḥ  | |

<3>
tṛtīyo'dhyāyaḥ
kāśyayapaḥ
gururevaṃ tvayā proko prokto mune kiṃlakṣaṇaḥ smṛtaḥ  |
[kiṃ vā yoniḥsamācāra](kiṃyonirvā kimācāraḥ)satcchiṣyaścāpi
kīṭṭaśaḥ  | | 3.1  | |
viśvāmitraḥ
śrūyatāmabhidhāsyāmi gurorlekṣaṇamuttamam  |
brāhmaṇyāṃ brāhmaṇājjāto yathāvat sāṅgavedavit  | | 3.2  | |
kṛtaśramaḥ śabdaśāstre bahuśaḥ prayatātmavān
nyāyaśāstre ca niṣṇāto vaiśeṣikavicakṣaṇaḥ  | | 3.3  | |
mīmāṃsāpāraṭṭaśvā ca sāṃkhye yoge ca niṣṭhitaḥ  |
jyotiṣāmayane kalpe śīkṣāyāṃ ca viśāradaḥ  | | 3.4  | |
nirukte chandasāṃ śāstre vāstuśāstre tathauva ca  |
sarvaśāstrārthatattvajñaḥ pratibhānasamanvitaḥ  | | 3.5  | |
vedāntaviśrantamanāḥ karmaṇāṃ ca prayogavit  |
pañcarātrārthaniṣṇātaḥ pañcakālaparāyaṇaḥ  | | 3.6  | |
sṛṣṭisaṃhāratattvajño lokānāṃ karuṇākaraḥ  |
sthiraghīrmadhurābhāṣī vinītaḥ satyasaṃgaraḥ  | | 3.7  | |
vimatsaraḥ prasannātmā sādhurvāgmī jitendriyaḥ  |
[upendriyaḥ](?) paṇḍitaśca puṇyāpuṇyavivekabhāk  | | 3.8  | |
prāsādapratimādīnāṃ guṇadoṣaviśeṣavit  |
nityastrāyī śuddhavāsāḥ śuddhaveṣaḥ śubhāśrayaḥ  | | 3.9  | |
doṣābhipvaṅgarahito jñānī nityaṃ jitendriyaḥ  |
viṣṇorārādhanaparo ?hutāgrirmantravittamaḥ  | | 3.10  | |
sarvasaṃśayasaṃchettā śipyāṇāṃ śān?tamānasaḥ  |
vaiṣṇavānāgatān ṭṛṣṭvā hmabhyutthānābhivādanaiḥ  | | 3.11  | |
pratigrahītārcanābhirarghyādyābhiḥ kutūhalāt  |
ananyacetāḥ satataṃ viṣṇorbhajanatatparaḥ  | | 3.12  | |
samayācāranirataḥ saṃvidvāgmī guroḥ priyaḥ  |
preṣyavargāśanāt paścāt svayaṃ bhoktā satāṃ mataḥ  | | 3.13  | |
parāṅmukho niṣiddheṣu paramāstikyasaṃyutaḥ  |
dhṛtordhvapuṇṅgaḥ satataṃ śastayā śvetamṛtsrayā  | | 3.14  | |
śaṅkhacakādibhiśrihaiḥ sveṣu sthāneṣu lā?ñchataḥ  |
anyūnānatiriktaiśca sarvāṅaigaḥ samanaṅkṛtaḥ  | | 3.15  | |
ābhirūpyaguṇopeteḥ prajārañjanatatparaḥ  |
evaṃ lakṣaṇasaṃyukto yaḥ sa ācārya ucyate  | | 3.16  | |
śiṣyāṇāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ vacmi tacchṛṇupva sāhitaḥ  |
triṣu varṇeṣu saṃbhūtaḥ praśastakulasaṃbhavaḥ  | | 17  | |
adhatasāṅgavedaśca nityastrāyī jitendriyaḥ  |
śrutavṛttopasaṃpannaḥ satyavāk pāṭhatatparaḥ  | | 3.18  | |
ācaryabhaktiyuktaśca suśīlaḥ śāṭhyavarjitaḥ  |
alubdhaḥ saṃvibhāgī ca kṛtajñaḥ sajjanāṭṛtaḥ  | | 3.19  | |
khadāramātrasaṃtuṣṭaḥ śāstraśravaṇatatparaḥ  |
saṃvatsarai?stribhirvāpi dvābhyāṃ vaikena vā dvija  | | 3.20  | |
parīkṣitaḥ prasannātmā viṣṇubhaktisamanvitaḥ  |
aparyuṣitabhoktā ca jihvācāpalavarjitaḥ  | | 3.21  | |
saṃnidhau ca gurormaunī bhūmāvevopaveśakaḥ  |
mātāpitroṣca śuśrūṣuḥ śrautasmārtakriyāparaḥ  | | 3.22  | |
guroḥ patnayāṃ guroḥ putre guruvadvṛttisaṃyutaḥ  |
mitrāṇāmanabhidrehī dveṣyasaṃgavivarjitaḥ  | | 3.23  | |
niṣiddhācāravimukho vṛddhānāmabhivādakaḥ  |
agrikārye ca nirato guro rveśmanyatandritaḥ  | | 3.24  | |
ārādhako vaiṣṇavānāṃ vedyānāṃ śubhalakṣṇaḥ  |
dhṛtordhvapuṇḍratikalaḥ sadā dvādaśanāmabhiḥ  | | 3.25  | |
praśastojjavalaveṣaśca śaṅkhacakrādilāñchitaḥ  |
evamādiguṇaiyuṃktaḥ sthiradhīḥ śiṣya ucyate  | | 3.26  | |
striyaḥ śūdrāścānulomāḥ kalyāṇaguṇasaṃyutāḥ  |
yadi tānapi śiṣyatve gṛhṇīyāt kṛpayā guruḥ  | | 3.27  | |
bādhiryādibhirācāryo doṣaiḥ kaiścidyuto yadi  |
na sa tyājyo bhavedeṣa dīkṣitaścakramaṇḍale  | | 3.28  | |
tadgrataṃ jñānapauṣkalyaṃ dauṣāṃśca guṇatāṃ nayet  |

iti pañcarātre mahopaniṣadi viśvāmitrasaṃhitāyāṃ
[guruśiṣyalakṣaṇaṃ nāma] tṛtīyo'dhyāyaḥ  | |

<4>
caturtho'dhyāyaḥ
kāśyapaḥ
bhagavatparamityuktaṃ tantrametat tvayā guro  |
kimartho bhagavacchabdaḥ kīṭṛśo bhagavāṃśca saḥ  | | 4.1  | |
viśvāmitraḥ
vadāmi bhagavacchabdaṃ śrṛṇuṣva munisattama  |
jñānaṃ nissīmamaiśvaryamananyuruṣāśrayam  | | 4.2  | |
sarvātiśāyinī śaktiḥ balaṃ sarvottamaṃ tathā  |
anyairahāryaṃ vīryaṃ ca tejaḥ sarvottarottaram  | | 4.3  | |
etāni ṣaḍudīryante bhagaśabdena kāśyapa  |
yasminnime guṇāḥ santi sa ukto bhagavāniti  | | 4.4  | |
e?kavavatro dvihastaśca śuddhasphaṭikasaṃnibhaḥ  |
ayutātyivahīnduko?ṭikoṭisamaprabhaḥ  | | 4.5  | |
tejo'tirekād duṣprekṣaḥ śaṅkhacakradharaḥ svayam  |
kaustubhaṃ vakṣasā bimrat śrīvatsaśrīniketanaḥ  | | 4.6  | |
pītāmbaradharo nityaṃ vanamālāvibhūṣitaḥ  |
kirīṭahārakeyūrakuṇḍalojjvalavigrahaḥ  | | 4.7  | |
evaṃrūpo vāmudevo bhagavāniti śabditaḥ  |
hatyuḍarīkamadhyasthaṃ yaṃ dhyāyanti mumukṣavaḥ  | | 4.8  | |
guṇavaiṣamyato'bhūvan tasmāt saṃkarṣaṇāvadayaḥ  |
kāśyapaḥ
guṇavaiṣamyato jātāḥ saṃkarṣaṇamukhā iti  | | 4.9  | |
ākhyātaṃ bhavatā brahman tanme vistarato vada  |
viśvāmitraḥ
pūrvamuktaṃ bhagavato [vāsudevo dadat tridhā]  | | 4.10  | |
anyo'bhūdvāsudevākhyaḥ sṛṣṭisthityantakārakaḥ  |
ekavaktraścaturbāhuḥ śaṅkhacakragadābjabhṛt  | | 4.11  | |
śrīvatsaṃ vakṣasā bibhrat kaustubhaṃ ca prabhojjavalam  |
vanamālāṃ tathā bibhrat divyāmatimanoharam  | | 4.12  | |
pītāmbaradharaḥ saumyaḥ śikhikaṇṭhopamacchaviḥ  |
svātantryeṇa svakīyena dvidhātmānaṃ cakāra saḥ  | | 4.13  | |
tayorbhagavatoreko vimalasphaṭikacchaviḥ  |
anyaḥ kālāmbudābhāso nārāyaṇa itīritaḥ  | | 4.14  | |
vāsudevāt tato jajñe saṃkarṣaṇasamāhvayaḥ  |
tasmāt pradyumrasaṃbhūtiraniruddhastato'bhavat  | | 4.15  | |
ete sarve caikavavatrāścaturbāhusamanvitāḥ  |
sarṣibhirjātibhiḥ sarvairvāsudevasamairyutāḥ  | | 4.16  | |
jñānādhikyādabhūdevaṃ saṃkarṣaṇasamāhvayaḥ  |
pradyumrastu balādhikayādaiśvaryādhikyato'bhavat  | | 4.17  | |
aniruddha ime sarve svaiḥ svaiḥ ṣaḍbhirguṇairyutāḥ  | |
caturbhujāḥ sarva ete prāguktāyudhadhariṇaḥ  | | 4.18  | |
vāsudevāt prathamataḥ trayo devāstu jajñire  |
catvāraścātha tannāmrā kramādete dvijottama  | | 4.19  | |
catvāro'mī caturbhyaśca saṃbhūtāstu yathākramam  |
aṣokṣajo nṛsiṃhaśca vāmano bhārgavastathā  | | 4.20  | |
janārdana upendraśca hariḥ kṛṣṇaśca nāmataḥ  |
vāsudevāddevadevādaṣṭaitā mūrtayo'bhavan  | | 4.21  | |
brāhmī tu prathamā mūrtiḥ prājāpatyā ca vaiṣṇavī  |
divyā cārṣī mānuṣī ca saptamī kīrtitāsurī  | | 4.22  | |
paiśācī cāṣṭamī tāsāṃ mūtanīṃnāṃ daśamūrtayaḥ  | |
mīnamukhyā dvijaśreṣṭha vyūhe yathākramam  | | 4.23  | |
matyakūrmavarāhāstu vāsudevāt prajajñire  |
[acyutādacyuto bhūtvā vāsudevācca jajñire]  | | 4.24  | |
babhrūtrailokyamohāhvau dāśārhaḥ śaurisaṃjñakaḥ  |
annādhipatināmā ca pañcaitā mūrtayo dvija  | | 4.25  | |
saṃkarṣaṇāddhayagrīvaḥ śaṅkhodaranṛkesarī  |
vaikuṇṭhamūrtirādyastu mukundo'tha vṛṣā?kapiḥ  | | 4.26  | |
varāhaśca tathānando nāgarājaśca jajñire  |
sarvāsāmeva mūtīṃnāṃ muhūrtasamaye dvija  | | 4.27  | |
śaṅkacakragadāpahmādyāyudhāni sahaiva tu  |
kirīṭahārakeyūrakaṭakāni ca jajñire  | | 4.28  | |
[śriyādayo'thaṃ deva(vya?)stu śrīvatsā?(tsāt ?)divyālāñchanāḥ(nāta?)] |
ajāyantātha garuḍaḥ saṃjāto mūrtisaṃcayāt  | | 4.29  | |
vāsudevādikādvedamūrtastadvāhanātmanā  |
kumudaḥ kumudākṣaśca puṇḍarīko'tha vāmanaḥ  | | 4.30  | |
śaṅkukarṇaḥ sarpanetraḥ sumukhaḥ supratiṣṭhitaḥ  | |
praśrigarbho mānavaśvaca bhūtanāthā amī daśa  | | 4.31  | |
sarvaiḥ pāriṣadaiḥ sārdhamaniruddhāṅdhrito'bhavan  |
sarvataḥ pāṇipādācca sarvato'kṣiśiromukhāt  | | 4.32  | |
sarvataḥ śrutisaṃyuktādaniruddhātmanaḥ prabhoḥ  |
aṅgādāsīt jagatsarvaṃ sadevāsuramānuṣam  | | 4.33  | |
indravahnī ca catvāro vedāścāṅgāni ṣaṭ tathā  |
ajāyantāsya mukhato dyaurmūrdhraścakṣuṣo raviḥ  | | 4.34  | |
candramā manaso jātaḥ śrotrāt sarvā diśo'bhavan  |
nābherabhūnnabhaścāsya padabhayāṃ bhūmirajāyata  | | 4.35  | |
vāyuḥ prāṇāt tathā mṛtyurapānāt keśato'mbudaḥ  |
kṣaṇādayaḥ kālabhedāḥ śarīrādasya jajñire  | | 4.36  | |
oṣadhyo romakūpebhyo vanaspatibhiranvitāḥ  |
āsan yajñāśca bahavo dakṣiṇāsahitā dvija  | | 4.37  | |
brā?hmaṇā mukhato'bhūvan bāhvośca kṣatriyāstathā  |
ūvorvaiśyāstathā padabhyāṃ śūdrā jātāḥ pṛthak pṛthak  | | 4.38  | |
kṛtvaivaṃ jagataḥ sṛṣṭiṃ kṣīrārṇavagataḥ prabhuḥ  |
anantabhogiparyaṅke śrībhūmibhyāṃ samanvitaḥ  | | 4.39  | |
sanandanādyairyogīndraiścintyamāno hṛdambuje  |
brahmendraśarvapramukhairvandyamāno marudgraṇaiḥ  | | 4.40  | |
caṇḍādyairdvārapāleśca bhūteśaiḥ kumudādibhiḥ  |
viṣvaksenagarutmadabhyāṃ sevyamānaśca sarvadā  | | 4.41  | |
śete viṣṇuracintyātmā bhūtānāmanukamyayā  |
ityevaṃ guṇavaiṣamyāt mūrtibhedasamudabhavaḥ  | | 4.42  | |
jagadudabhūtisaṃyuktaḥ proktaste kaśyapātmaja  |
ataḥ paraṃ mahāprājña kimanyat kathayāmi te  | | 4.43  | |
iti pañcarātre mahopaniṣadi viśvāmitrasaṃhitāyāṃ vāsudevādimūtyuṃtpattikathanaṃ nāma caturtho'dhyāyaḥ

<5>
pañcamo'dhyāyaḥ
kāśyapaḥ
aniruddhājjagatsṛṣṭiḥ sākṣādabhagavatatstvayā  |
uktā yatsṛjati brahmā lokānityanuśuśruma  | | 5.1  | |
tatsatyaṃ kimutāsatyaṃ mahat kautūhalaṃ hi me  |
sartate jñātumadhunā kathyatāmakhilaṃ guro  | | 5.2  | |
[viśvāmitraḥ]
devo narāyaṇaḥ śrīmān kṣīrārṇavaniketanaḥ  |
ādau vinodamanvicchan svātantryeṇātmanaḥ prabhuḥ  | | 5.3  | |
apaḥ sṛṣṭā svalaṃ vīryaṃ nidadhe tāsu cojjavalam  |
hairaṇyamaṇḍamabhavad ravikoṭisamaprabham  | | 5.4  | |
tasmādabhūt svayaṃ brahmā sarvakopitāmahaḥ  |
taṃ ṭṭaṣṭavā bhagavān viṣṇurlokasṛṣṭyarthamādiśat  | | 5.5  | |
tathetyuktvā punarbrahmā sanakādīn tathāsṛjat  |
tānuvācātha sa brahmā prajāḥ sṛjata putrakāḥ  | | 5.6  | |
ityuktā api te sarve tathā svāyaṃbhuvā dvija  |
mano na pravaṇaṃ cakruḥ jagannirmāṇakarmaṇi  | | 5.7  | |
te tu sarve samabhavan yogino vijitendriyāḥ  |
nirmamā nirahaṃkārā rāgadveṣavivarjitāḥ  | | 8  | |
viśuddhasatvāḥ satataṃ tattvacintāparāyaṇāḥ  |
ityevaṃ teṣu sarveṣu [jagatsṛṣṭyarthameṣu ca]  | | 5.9  | |
cukopa bhagavān brahmā svatantrān svasutān prati  |
tataḥ kruddhasya devasya lalāṭāt kuṭilabhruvaḥ  | | 5.10  | |
prādurbabhūva puruṣo nīlalohitavarṇabhāk  |
nāma mahmaṃ dadasveti so'rodīt pituragrataḥ  | | 5.11  | |
rudantaṃ tamatho ṭṛṣṭvā vyayāhāra prajāpatiḥ  |
arodīriti yattena rudunāmā bhaviṣyasi  | | 5.12  | |
dvidhā kuru śarīraṃ te putraketyāha taṃ prabhuḥ  |
tato hṛṣṭamanā rudraḥ śarīraṃ sa dvidhākarot  | | 5.13  | |
ardhena so'bhavannārī naro'pyardhena cābhavat  |
tasmāttu mithunānnātā rudrāṇāṃ koṭayo'dbhutāḥ  | | 5.14  | |
caturbhujāḥ śūlahastāstrinetrāḥ krūramūrtayaḥ  |
pradhānāsteṣu ca mune rudrā ekādaśābhāvan  | | 5.15  | |
brahmātha rudrasṛṣṭiṃ tāṃ vyarthāṃ vīkṣya tataḥ prabhuḥ  |
marīcimaddhiṃ pulahaṃ pulastyaṃ ca bhuguṃ tathā  | | 5.16  | |
dakṣaṃ ca manasā devasṛṣṭyarthaṃ nirmame punaḥ  |
taireva sakalaṃ sṛṣṭaṃ jagat sthāvaraṅgamam  | | 5.17  | |
ityevaṃ jagataḥ sṛṣṭiṃ vidhāya kamalāsanaḥ  |
dhyānaṃ paraṃ samāsthāya paśyannāste paraṃ mahaḥ  | | 18  | |
iti te brahmaṇaḥ sṛṣṭiḥ kathitā dvijasattama  |
brahmā haro viṣṇuriti bhedo naivāsti karhicit  | | 5.19  |.
guṇatrayaṃ samāsthāya bhagavān puruṣottamaḥ  |
sṛṣṭiṃ sthiti saṃhṛtiṃ ca jagataḥ kurute svayam  | | 20  | |
mūtīrāsthāya bhagavān brahmaviṣṇuśivātmikāḥ  |
vinā tena tṛṇāgraṃ ca na kiṃciccalituṃ kṣamam  | | 5.21  | |
kāśyapaḥ
kiṃ guṇānāṃ tryaṃ proktamadhiṣṭhāya ca kaṃ guṇam  |
kiṃ ca rūpaṃ samāsthāya kiṃ karma kurute paraḥ  | | 5.22  | |
viśvāmitraḥ
sattvaṃ rajastama iti guṇatrayumudāhṛtam  |
rajoguṇadhiṣṭhāya brahnāmā sṛjatyayam  | | 5.23  | |
sattvamāsthā viṣṇvākhyaḥ pālatyakhilaṃ jagat  |
tamassaṃśrayaṇādrudrarūpī saṃharati prajāḥ  | | 5.24  | |
sṛṣṭisthitivināśānāmekameva hi kāraṇam  |
kāśyapaḥ
jānāmi kasmālloko'yamāvirbhavati suvrata | | 5.25  | |
ante ca kasmin vasati kathyatāṃ dvijapuṃgava  |
prasādapravaṇaṃ cittaṃ mayi cetad dvijottama  | | 5.26  | |
viśvāmitraḥ
nārāyaṇasya dve rūpe puruṣaḥ prakṛtiśca te  |
ātmecchayānupraviśya dvitayaṃ kṣobhayatyasau  | | 5.27  | |
anādireṣā prakṛtistriguṇā cāvināśinī  |
carācaramidaṃ sūte jagat puruṣasaṃśrayāt  | | 5.28  | |
guṇasamyayutāt tasmāt puruṣeṇāpyadhiṣṭhitāt  |
abhūt tattvaṃ mahannāma guṇaistaistrividhaṃ dvija  | | 5.29  | |
tasmādabhūdahaṃkāraḥ trividhaḥ saṃprakīrtitaḥ  |
vaikāristaijasaśca bhūtādiśceti nāmataḥ  | | 5.30  | |
vaikārikāt satt‌vayutādahaṃkārāt babhūva ha  |
śrotratvakcakṣujihvāgraghrāṇamindriyapañyakam  | | 5.31  | |
rajoyuktādahaṃkārāta taijasāt pañca jajñire  |
karmendriyāṇi vākpāṇipāyūpasthapadāni ca  | | 5.32  | |
tamoyuktādahaṃkārāt bhūtādeḥ samajāyata  |
śabdatanmātrametasmād ākāśaḥ saṃbabhūva ha  | | 5.33  | |
ākaśāt sparśatanmātraṃ vāyustasmādabhūd dvija  |
vāyośca rūpatanmātraṃ krameṇāgnimajījanat  | | 5.34  | |
agreśca rasatanmātraṃ tasmādāpaḥ prajajñire  |
adabhyaśca gandhatanmātramabhūt tasmādabhūnmahī  | | 5.35  | |
ākāśasya guṇaḥ śabda eka eva prakīrtitaḥ  |
vāyoḥ sparśaśca śabdaś?ca dvau guṇau kīrtitau  | | 5.36  | |
agreḥ sparśaśca śabdaśca rūpaṃ ceti guṇatrayam  |
catvāro'pāṃ guṇāḥ śabdarūparśarasāḥ smṛtāḥ  | | 5.37  | |
bhūmeḥ pañca guṇāḥ śabdasparśarūparasā api  |
gandhaśca pañca bhūtāni jātānyevaṃ dvijottama  | | 5.38  | |
nānāvīryāṇi cānyonyasaṃsargeṇa vinā svayam
nāśakan jagataḥ sṛṣṭau tasmāt saṃhatimāyayuḥ  | | 5.39  | |
mahadādyā viśeṣāntāḥ pradhāpanapuruṣeritāḥ  |
aṇḍamutpādayanti sma te sarve lokakāraṇam  | | 5.40  | |
devasya pahmanābhasya nābhau hemamayaṃ bṛhat  |
tasmāt svayaṃbhūrbhagavān tena sṛṣṭamidaṃ jagat  | | 5.41  | |
evaṃ yathā prakṛtito jagadāsīccarācaram  |
tathā tatraiva viprendra layameti punaḥ punaḥ  | | 5.42  | |
iti pañcarātre mahopaniṣadi viśvāmitrasaṃhitāyāṃ
[jagatsṛṣṭilayakathanaṃ nāma] pañcamo'dhyāyaḥ  | |

<6>
ṣaṣṭho'dhyāyaḥ
kāśyapaḥ
bhagavan kathyatāṃ mahmaṃ guruṇānena kiṃ khalu  |
śiṣyebhya upadeṣṭavyaṃ kathaṃ vā tena gṛhmate  | | 6.1  | |
viśvāmitraḥ
ācaryeṇopadeṣṭavyā mantrāḥ praṇavapūrvakāḥ  |
praṇavasya samuddhāraḥ śrūyatāṃ prathamaṃ dvija  | | 6.2  | |
prathamaṃ viṣṇumuddhṛtya śrīdharaṃ tadanantaram  |
ekīkṛtyācaredetau mandaraṃ tadanantaram  | | 6.3  | |
eṣa praṇava ityuktaḥ parabrahmapravaktṛkaḥ  |
ekākṣaro manurayaṃ plutodāttamimaṃ paṭhet  | | 6.4  | |
ṛṣirbrahmā bhavedasya devīgāyatrasaṃjñitam  |
chando'sya pratipādyaṃ tu paramātmā hariḥ svayam  | | 6.5  | |
buddhistattvaṃ smṛtaṃ kṣetraṃ paramaṃ padamucyate  |
sito varṇaḥ samuddiṣṭaḥ ṣaḍaṅgānyapi me śṛṇu  | | 6.6  | |
jñānaiśvarye'tha(?)śaktiśca balavīrye satejasī  |
namaḥ svāhā vaṣaṭ huṃphaṭ vauṣaḍantāni tānyapi  | | 5.7  | |
caturthyantāni hṛdaye śirasaḥ śikhayā saha  |
kavacāstre sanayane krameṇaitena vinyaset  | | 5.8  | |
ādyameṣāṃ trayāṇāṃ syāt bījaṃ binduvibhūvibhūṣitam  |
śaktiṃ sa tadviduḥ sarvamenaṃ tattvaṃ japenmanum  | | 69  | |
avijñātaṛṣicchandodevatābījaśaktayaḥ  |
mantrāḥ sarve cāṅgahīnā niṣphalā japakarmaṇi  | | 6.10  | |
tasmāt sarvaṃ viditvaiva japakarma samācaret  |
praṇamya svaguraṃ pūrvaṃ tadgurūṃścānvagasya tu  | | 6.11  | |
mantramūrtyādikān paścāt natvaitajjapamācaret  |
prathamaṃ vyāpakanyāsaṃ varṇanyāsamataḥ param  | | 6.12  | |
aṅganyāsaṃ ca tadanu karayorvapuṣi kramāt  |
kṛtvā japavidhiṃ samyagācarenmunipuṃgava  | | 6.13  | |
mātṛkānyāsapūrvastu sarvamantrajapaḥ smṛtaḥ  |
śiromadhye'tha vadane ṭṛṣṭhiśrotranasāyuge  | | 6.14  | |
kapoloṣṭhodaradvandve ūrdhvāścāsyamadhyage  |
acaḥ krameṇa vinyasya karayoḥ pādayorapi  | | 6.15  | |
saṃdhyagreṣu krāmānyasya ilāṃ vargacatuṣṭayam  |
pañkamaṃ pārśvayoḥ pṛṣṭhe nābho ca jaṭhare tathā  | | 6.16  | |
vakṣaḥkakṣāparagalakaṇṭheṣvapi catuṣṭayam  |
yakārādi tataḥ paścācchakārādicatuṣṭayam  | | 6.17  | |
hṛtpāṇipādayugale kakṣe hṛdi mukhāntare  |
vinyasya mātṛkāmeva mūrtiśaktiyutāṃ tu vā  | | 6.18  | |
japaḥpuṃsāṃ samuddiṣṭo vāgdevīsaṃprasādakaḥ  |
bhāṣyaṃ praṇavamantrasya śruṇuṣvaikamanā dvija  | | 6.19  | |
akārārtho bhavedviṣṇurmakāro jīvavācakaḥ  |
?B avadhāraṇavācī syādukāreṇa trayeṇa tu  | | 20  | |
jīvo nārāyaṇasyaivetyayamartho nirūpitaḥ  |
lakṣatrayaṃ japenmantraṃ tadardhaṃ tarpayedapi  | | 6.21  | |
homaścāpi tadardhaṃ syādevaṃ kṛtvā tato manuḥ  |
siddhaḥ syāt siddhimātreṇa sarvakarma samācaret  | | 6.22  | |
śākayāvakabhaikṣāśī trisandhyaṃ snānamācaret  |
kṛtordhvapuṇḍraḥ satataṃ devatālayagoṣṭhaḥ  | | 6.23  | |
parvatāgre nadītīre samudrataṭasaṃśritaḥ  |
athavā bilvamūlastho japakarma samācaret  | | 6.24  | |
evaṃ [praṇavasiddhasya] haihikāmuṣmikaṃ phalam  |
karasaṃpuṭasaṃsthaṃ syāt tasmādevaṃ tu sādhayet  | | 6.25  | |
etassanna paro mantraḥ sarvasiddhipradāyakaḥ  |
sarve vedāḥ samutpannaḥ praṇavānmantranāyakāt  | | 6.26  | |
antyakāle hariṃ dhyāyan vācā praṇavamuccaran  |
yogaśāstroktamārgeṇa yaḥ śarīraṃ parityajet  | | 6.27  | |
sa yāti paramaṃ dhāma nārāyaṇasamāhvayam  |
athāṣṭākṣaramantrasya samuddhāraṃ vadāmyaham  | | 6.28  | |
praṇavaṃ pūrvamudtya bhadrapāṇiṃ saviṣṇukam  |
otadevayutaṃ dakṣaṃ vighraśaṃ ca sagopanam  | | 6.29  | |
viśvātmānaṃ sādidevaṃ śaṅkhamādisamanvitam  |
dvitīyasvarasaṃyuktaṃ vanamālīṃ (?) samuddharet  | | 6.30  | |
aprameyena saṃyuktaṃ caturgatimathoddharet  |
evamaṣṭākṣaro mantra uddhṛtaḥ sarvasādhakaḥ  | | 6.31  | |
okārādiyutaṃ bījamardhendukṛtaśekharam  |
śaktirāyeti mantrānto'syāntaryāmī muniḥ smṛtaḥ  | | 6.32  | |
chando gāyatramuddiṣṭaṃ devīpūrvaṃ ca devātā  |
paramātmā samuddiṣṭaḥ kṣetraṃ vyoma paraṃ smṛtam  | | 6.33  | |
[varṇā(rṇa ?)ḥ śukla(klo ?) bhavedasma] pratyakṣaramṛṣin śṛṇu  |
gautamaśca bharadvājo viśvāmitra anantaram  | | 6.34  | |
jamadagrivasiṣṭhau ca kāśyapā[gri(trī ?)sakumbhajau ]  |
ṛṣayo'ṣṭau kramādete varṇanāṃ samudāhṛtāḥ  | | 6.35  | |
gāyatryādivirāḍantacchando'ṣṭakamudāhṛtāḥ  |
dharā dhruvaśca somaśca tathāpo'gnirjaleśvaraḥ  | | 6.36  |
pratyūṣaḥ saṃprabhāsaśca devān pratyakṣaraṃ viduḥ  |
kṣetrāṇi paramākāśaṃ satyādyaṃ lokasaptakam  | | 6.37  | |
[pṛthivyantarvyu(ntaṃ vyu ?tkrameṇa] varṇānāṃ kramavartinām  |
tattvānyagnirvāyuviyat sūryo dyauraśca candramāḥ  | | 6.38  | |
nakṣatrasaṃghāḥ kramaśo? vidyudvarṇāṣṭakasya tu  |
nārā narāṇāṃ saṃghātā narāṇāṃ sūnavo'thavā  | | 6.39  | |
āpo'thavā samuddiṣṭāḥ teṣāmayanamityayam  |
āsyante vāyanamiti tenaḥ nārāyaṇaḥ smṛtaḥ  | | 6.40  | |
tadīyo'haṃ madīyo na bhaveyamiti cārthanā  |
asyārthaḥ kathito vipra narastasmai karoti vā  | | 6.41  | |
dīkṣāpūrvaṃ gurorlabdhavā mantramenaṃ śubhe'hani  |
mārgaśīrṣe site pakṣe dvādaśyāṃ bhaktipūrvakam  | | 6.42  | |
nārāyaṇaṃ samārādhya cakrābje maṇḍalottame  |
nīlāmbudapratīkāśaṃ puṇḍarīkāyatekṣaṇam  | | 6.43  | |
caturbhirbāḍukamalaiḥ śaṅkhacakragadāmbujam  |
dadhānaṃ saumyavapuṣaṃ nīlaku?ñcitamūrghajam  | | 6.44  | |
vasānaṃ pītavasane pūrṇacandranibhānanam  |
tāmroṣṭhaṃ cāruhasitaṃ lasanmakarakuṇḍalam  | | 6.45  | |

dhyāyannevaṃ śucirbhūtvā japenmantravaraṃ sudhīḥ  |

siddhisaṃsūcakāṃścaiva susvaprāpi paśyati  |

dharmārthakāmamokṣaṇāṃ yadvāñchati tadeva tat  |
nityaṃ jāpadau kṛtvā trīn prāṇāyāmānananyadhīḥ  | | 6.52  | |
karayośca śarīre ca vyāpakaṃ prathamaṃ nyaset  |
nyāsaśca trividhaḥ proktaḥ sa sṛṣṭisthitisaṃhṛtiḥ  | | 6.53  | |
?dakṣiṇāṅgumārabhya vāmāṅguṣṭhāntameva yaḥ  |
nyāso'yaṃ sṛṣṭisaṃjñaḥ syāt vyutkrameṇa ca saṃhṛtiḥ  | | 6.54  | |
aṅguṣṭhādikaniṣṭhāntamubhayoḥ karayostu yaḥ  |
sthitinyāsaśca saṃprokto gṛhasthastaṃ samācaret  | | 6.55  | |
sṛṣṭinyāsaṃ brahmācārī saṃnyāsī saṃhṛti nyaset  |
dehe ca śira ārabhya pādāntaḥ sṛṣṭisaṃjñakaḥ  | | 6.56  | |
saṃhāro viparītaḥ syāt nābhyādihṛdayāntakaḥ  |
sthitinyāsaḥ samuditaḥ triṣvekaṃ nyāsamācaret  | | 6.57  | |
tarjanīṃ dakṣiṇakarādārabhyārṇaṃcatuṣṭayam  |
praṇavādyantasaṃyuktaṃ tatkaniṣṭhāvadhi nyaset  | | 6.58  | |
sabījakaṃ vāmakare'pyevameva catuṣṭayam  |
aṅguṣṭhābhyāmaṅgulīnāṃ madhyaparvasu vinyaset  | | 6.59  | |
vinyasyāṅgāni ca tathā karayorubhayorapi  |
aṅguṣṭhādikaniṣṭhāntaṃ vinyasyāṅgāni vai daśa  | | 6.60  | |
śeṣamaṅgadvayaṃ nyasyedaṅgulyagreṣu vinyaset  |
dehe'pi vyāpakanyāsaṃ kṛtvā varṇāṣṭakaṃ punaḥ  | | 6.61  | |
nābhyādhyoguhmajānvagrapādapūṣṭheṣvapi kramāt  |
śiro'kṣiyuganāsāgrahṛdaṣvapi vinyaset  | | 6.62  | |
kaniṣṭhāṅguṣṭhaśirasi saṃyojya prathamaṃ nyaset  |
aṅguṣṭhābhiścatasṛbhirdvitīyaṃ nyāsamācaret  | | 6.53  | |
jānuno naitadagre syāt pādayoḥ sasamūrubhiḥ  |
[mūdhnaryākayā (?)] madhyamayā tarjanyā sā ca cakṣuṣoḥ  | | 6.64  | |
nāmāgrānāmi?kāṅguṣṭhaśirobhyāṃ hṛdaye punaḥ  |

varṇaṣaṭkaṃ pañcaśikhaṃ praṇavādi sajātikam  |

padadvaye namo'ntaṃ ca vinyaset sacaturthakam  | | 6.67  | |
saptamāṣṭamavarṇo dvau vinyasettvaṅdhripṛṣṭhayoḥ  |
astramantreṇa digbandhaṃ kṛtvā paścācca tarjanīm  | | 6.68  | |
agriprākāramantreṇa triḥ parikramyā cordhvagam  |
japādīni ca karmāṇi kuryādevamatantidrataḥ  | | 6.69  | |

iti pañcarātre mahopaniṣadi viśvāmitrasaṃhitāyāṃ [aṣṭākṣaramantroddhāravidhirnāma] ṣaṣṭho'dhyāyaḥ  | |

<7>
saptamo'dhyāyaḥ
viśvāmitraḥ
śrūyatāṃ dvādaśārṇasya mantrarājasya saṃvidhiḥ  |
yasya śravaṇamātreṇa mucyate sarvapātakaiḥ  | | 7.1  | |
abhyarcya mātṛkācakrāduddhared dvādaśākṣaram  |
udgrīthamuddharet pūrvaṃ vidhreśaṃ viṣṇupūrvakam  | | 7.2  | |
mandaraṃ caudanayutaṃ subhadraṃ cāprameyakam  |
gadadhvaṃsaṃ vipṇuśīrṣaṃ varuṇaṃ cādisaṃyutam  | | 7.3  | |
saṃprasāraṇasaṃyuktamekanetramataḥ param  |
ādidevayutaṃ kumbhamanantarasudīrayet  | | 7.4  | |
saśrīdharaṃ pūrṇacandramatriṃ mādhavasaṃyutam  |
madhusūdanasaṃyuktaṃ pīyūṣātmānama???ñcitam  | | 7.5  | |
aprameyayutaṃ śaṅkhamuddharenmunisattama  |
eṃva dvādaśavarṇasya mantravaryasya suvrata  | | 7.6  | |
viṣṇurdaṇḍasamāyukto bījamasya prakīrtitaḥ  |
kruddholkāya maholkāya vīrolkāya tathaiva ca  | | 7.7  | |
vyulkāya ca sahastrādi ulkāyeti ca pañcamaḥ  |
tejolkāya ca ṣaṣṭhaḥ syādigrajāyāntimā ime  | | 7.8  | |
hṛdayādyaṅgamantrāḥ syuḥ ṣaḍete mantrasattmāḥ  |
athavā dvādaśārṇasya sthāneṣu dvādaśakhapi  | | 7.9  | |
iyaṃ klṛptiḥ parasyaiva vāsudevasya kīrtitā  |
vibhavādeḥ punabīṃjaṃ bhāskaraḥ sa guṇālayaḥ  | | 7.10  | |
ardhacandrasamāyuktaṃ punaraṅgaprakalpanam  |
dvādaśātmānamuddhṛtya varuṇena samanvitam  | | 7.11  | |
ādidevaṃ pañcabinduṃ hṛṣīkeśaṃ ca godhanam  |
aniruddhamanuṃ caurvaṃ ṣaḍimān daṇḍinaḥ paṭhet  | | 7.12  | |
ete krameṇa saṃyojyā natyantāścāṅgakalpane  |
vyūhādau viṣṇurasya syāt bījadaṇḍena saṃyutam  | | 7.13  | |
kruddholkādīni vā bījaṃ varṇadvādaśakaṃ tu vā  |
bījapakṣe dvādaśāṅgaḥ ṣaḍaṅgo'nyasya mantrarāṭ  | | 7.14  | |
triṣu pakṣeṣu brahmārṣirvāsudevaḥ svayaṃ smṛtaḥ  |
chandaśca devīgāyatraṃ devatāpi svayaṃ bhavet  | | 7.15  | |
sito varṇaśca tattvaṃ syāt buddhiḥ kṣetraṃ paraṃ viyat  |
bījapakṣe dvādaśāṅega bījāni dvādaśacchaviḥ  | | 7.16  | |
praṇavādyaṃ namo'ntaṃ ca bījānte nāmasaṃyutam  |
nyasya paścājjapenmantraṃ pūrvavaddhyānasaṃyutam  | | 7.17  | |
karayorvyāpakaṃ nyasya daśavṅguliṣu kramāt  |
vinyasya daśavarṇāṃśca śiṣṭaṃ taṃ mūdhriṃ vinyaset  | | 7.18  | |
[deva](he ?)pi pūrvavat jañca varṇān vinyasya deśikaḥ  |
śirasaśca caturdikṣu pūrvādiṣu catuṣṭayam  | | 7.19  | |
vinyasya pūrvavaccheṣaṃ nyasedvarṇatrayaṃ dvija  |
pūrvasmāttu viśeṣo'sya devaḥ sphaṭikasaṃnibhaḥ  | | 7.20  | |
śeṣāhibhoge vipūle sukhāsīno bhavet svayam  |
ubhābhyāṃ pārśvasaṃsthābhyāṃ devībhyāṃ sahito vibhuḥ  | | 7.21  | |
japed dvādaśalakṣaṃ tu tadardhaṃ tarpaṇaṃ bhavet  |
tadardhaṃ ca bhaveddhoma evaṃ sidhyati mantrarāṭ  | | 7.22  | |
dvādaśārṇasamo mantro vidyate na kadācana  |
saṃkarṣaṇamanuṃ vakṣye śrṛṇu tvamavadhānataḥ  | | 7.23  | |
dvādaśārṇāt samuddhṛtya prathamaṃ saptavacarṇakam  |
caturthyāntaṃ svanāmaiva paṭhedvinyasya vai dvija  | | 7.24  | |
pradyumrasyāniruddhasyāpyevameva vidhiḥ smṛtaḥ  |
dviṣaṭkākṣaravat sarve ṛṣyādikamudīritam  | | 7.25  | |
agriprākāramantrasya vidhānaṃ śrṛṇu sarvataḥ  |
vedīn (diṃ ?) pūrvamuddhṛtya vahniṃ gope(pa?)nasaṃyutam  | | 7.26  | |
sārdhacandre samuddhṛtya bhadrapāṇiṃ samādhavam  |
uddhṛtya dvādaśārṇo'yamāgriprākārasaṃjñitaḥ  | | 7.27  | |
sarvamantrasamārambhe kṛtordhvastāṃ satarjanīm  |
mūrdhraḥ parisamuddhṛtya bhrāmayet triḥ pradakṣiṇam  | | 7.28  | |
gāyatryā viṣṇupūrvāyā varṇoddhārakramaṃ śrṛṇu  |
udagrīthaṃ pūrvamuddhṛtya mantrasyāṣṭākṣarasya tu  | | 7.29  | |
[ādyaḥ](dyāt ?) paraṃ pañcavarṇāstataḥ kumbhaṃ saviṣṇukam(?)  |
vairājaṃ mandarayutaṃ sāprameyamudīrayet  | | 7.30  | |
saṃprasāraṇasaṃyuktaṃ dīptimantaṃ samuddharet  |
dvādaśākṣaramantrasya pañca parṇānudāharet  | | 7.31  | |
dhīmahīti padaṃ coktvā vijayaṃ viṣṇusaṃyutam  |
bhadrapāṇiyutaṃ paścād vīrasenayutaṃ paṭhet  | | 7.32  | |
kalaśaṃ bandhusahitaṃ śāntaṃ mumukhasaṃyutam  |
saśrīdharaṃ sargayutaṃ piṭhītvā tadanantaram  | | 7.33  | |
?pavitramagriviṣṇubhyāmuddhṛtya pretanāyakam  |
vāsudevasamākrāntamatriṃ prathamasaṃyutam  | | 7.34  | |
caturgati[sāti(di ?)deśa](va ?)muktvā vairājamuddharet  |
kevalaṃ (lo ?) viṣṇugāyatrīmanūrevaṃ samuddhṛtaḥ  | | 7.35  | |
ṛṣirbrahmāsya gāyatraṃ chando viṣṇuśca devatā  |
krūddholkādīni cāsya syuḥ ṣaḍaṅgāni mahāmate  | | 7.36  | |
śirolalāṭanayanadvayaśravaṇayugmake  |
mukhe kaṇṭhe ca hṛdaye bāhumūlyuge'pi ca  | | 7.37  | |
kūrparadvitaye caiva maṇibandhayute'pi ca  |
karayorudare guhme corujaṅghāpadadvaye  | | 7.38  | |
?B vinyaset kramaśo varṇān varṇavarṇānataḥ śṛṇu  |
hemābharaktadhūmābhasitāsitasupāalāḥ  | | 7.39  | |

aṣṭamo'dhyāyaḥ
kāśyapaḥ
bhagavan sarvamantrāṇāmuddhārastu svayeritaḥ  |
varṇānāṃ nāmabhedā ye tvayoktā munipuṃgava  | | 8.1  | |
na vehmi tānahaṃ sarvānupadeṣṭuṃ tvamarhasi  |
mayi prasādapravaṇaṃ mānasaṃ vidadhāsi cet  | | 8.2  | |
viśvāmitraḥ
vakṣyāmi varṇanāni kramaśo munisattama  |
yeṣu jñāteṣu mantrāṇāmuddhāraḥ sukaro mavet  | | 8.3  | |
susame bhūmibhāge tu śuddhe virahite janaiḥ  |
praśaste vātarahite prokṣite pañcagavyakaiḥ  | | 8.4  | |
darpaṇodarasaṃkāśaṃ catuṅgulamucchritam  |
hastadvayapramāṇaṃ caturaśraṃ vidhāya ca  | | 8.5  | |
sthaṇḍilaṃ śuddhadeśotthamṛdibhargomayavāriṇā  |
upaṇḍilaṃ ca saṃprokṣya praṇavena vilipya ca  | | 8.6  | |
dvādaśākṣaramantreṇa daśakṛtvo'bhijapya ca  |
tanmadhye mātṛkācakramaraiḥ ṣoḍaśabhiryutam  | | 8.7  | |
saṃyuktaṃ nābhinemibhyāṃ kṛtvā candanavāriṇā  |
śubhanakṣatrayoge tu sumuhūrte dvijottama  | | 8.8  | |
strataḥ śuklāmbarayugaṃ paridhāya subhūṣitaḥ  |
śubhacandanaliptāṅga śuklamālyavibhūṣitaḥ  | | 8.9  | |
svadehe mātṛkāṃ devīṃ vinyasya gurucoditaḥ  |
bahavo maṇayaḥ sūtre yathā syurdvijasattama  | | 8.10  | |
tathaiva tasminnākāre kartate varṇamālikā  |
sūteti(?) kṛtvā manasi varṇacakraṃ samālikhet  | | 8.11  | |
madhye praṇavamālikhya svarāpyarabhūmiṣu  |
nābhimadhye tathā sparśānādinemau samālikhet  | | 8.12  | |
āvirṇapraklṛptāṅgāṃ varṇābharaṇabhūṣitām  |
svaraklṛptamukhāmbhojāṃ kavargakakaradvayām  | | 8.13  | |
ṭavargapādayugalāṃ pavargavihitodarām  |

dhyātvaivaṃ mātṛkāṃ devīmarcayedardhyapūrvakaiḥ  | | 8.15  | |
upacāraiḥṣoḍaśabhistathā vai mūlavidyayā  |
varṇātmānaṃ ca puruṣaṃ pūjayeccakramadhyame  | | 8.16  | |
mantrān samuddharet paścād yatheṣṭaṃ bhaktiyūrvakam  |
varṇānāṃ nāmadheyāni śṛṇuṣvāvahito dvija  | | 8.17  | |
praṇavo brahmakośaśca brahma codgrītha ityapi  |
tārakaśchandasāmādiśoṃkāsyābhidhā imāḥ  | | 8.18  | |
akāraścāprameyaśca prathamo viṣṇurucyate  |
ākāraścādidevaśca gopano madhusūdanaḥ  | | 8.19  | |
ikāra iṣṭa [indha](ddha ?) rāmo [bandha](ndhu ?)śca kalpyate  |
īkāraḥ pañcabinduśca mahāmāyā ca vāmanaḥ  | | 8.20  | |
[ukārādaya ..va] (ukāra udayoddāmau ? ) śrīdharo bhuvanāhvayaḥ  |
ukāraścordhvalokeśo hṛṣīkeśo guhālayaḥ  | | 8.21  | |
ṛkāra ṛta[yā māyā] (dhāmā ca ?) satyaścaiva tathāṅkuśaḥ  |
ṝkāro viṣṭaro jvālā yo[(gī?)] dāmodaro'pi ca  | | 8.22  | |
laṛkāro goptṛnāmā syāt keśavaśca maheśvaraḥ  |
lṝkāro dīrdhaghoṇastu nārāyaṇasamāhvayaḥ  | | 8.23  | |

aṃkāro'nu[(sva ?)]raścaiva pradyumraḥ kapilākṣakaḥ  |
aḥkāraśca kalāntopasargaścaivāniruddhakaḥ  | | 8.26  | |

ghakāro gajavāhaśca pañcātmā pañca(pahmapā ?)ṇikaḥ  | | 8.28  | |

<9>
navamo'dhyāyaḥ
kāśyapaḥ
dīkṣāyā lakṣaṇaṃ brūhi bhagavan madanugrahān (?)  |
tsarvaṃ vistareṇaiva brūhi me bhaktavatsala  | | 9.1  | |
viśvāmitraḥ
dīkṣāṃ vakṣye tu saṃkṣepāt tacchṛṇu dijapuṃgava  |
āditye mīnameṣavṛṣanakeṣusaṃsthite  | | 9.2  | |
pūrvapakṣe śubhe ṛkṣe ekādayāṃ tathā niśi  |
śucau deśe manojñe ca kārayitvā tu maṇḍapam  | | 9.3  | |
gomayena samālipya veṣṭhayeddarbhamālayā  |
vedīṃ kuryācca tanmadhye caturhastapramāṇataḥ  | | 9.4  | |
tālotse[dhāparā cānye vistārāyāmatatsamā]ḥ  | | (?)  |
vedyāḥ paścimadigbhāge kuṇḍaṃ kuryādvidhānataḥ  | | 9.5  | |
krameṇa brāhmaṇādīnāmevaṃlakṣaṇakaṃ mune  |
catuṣkoṇaṃ tālayugmamanukūlaṃ dvijasya tu  | | 9.6  | |
vṛttaṃ kuryāt kṣatriyasya gokarṇayutasaṃyutam  |
cāpākāraṃ ca vaiśyasya vitastidvayasaṃmitam  | | 9.7  | |
trikoṇaṃ śūdrayoneḥ syāt prādeśadvayasaṃmitam  |
vistārāyāmasadṛśaṃ tatpramāṇamitāṃ tathā  | | 9.8  | |
trimekhalāṃ ca kurvīta paścime yonibhūṣitām  |
daśamyāṃ saṃbharet sarvān samidādīn samāhitaḥ  | | 9.9  | |
madhyaṃdine śuciḥ snāta ekabhuktena vartaṃyet  |
ekādaśyāṃ tithau s?nātaḥ śuddhavāsoyugaṃ dadhat  | | 9.10  | |
śvetagandhānuliptāṅgaḥ śvetamālyavibhūṣitaḥ  |
bhūṣitaśca yathālabdhairbhūṣaṇaiḥ samalaṃkṛtaḥ  | | 9.11  | |
sāyaṃkāle tu saṃprāpte kuryāccaivādhivāsanam  |
caturṣu dvāradeśeṣu pārśvayoḥ kalādvayam  | | 9.12  | |
nidhāya vedavikāmadhye kuryāccakrābjamaṇḍalam  |
hemādinirmitaṃ kumbhaṃ gālitodakapūritam  | | 9.13  | |
vinyastaratnakanakaṃ sakūrcaṃ sūtraveṣṭitam  |
vāsoyugalasaṃvītaṃ candanastragalaṃkṛtam  | | 9.14  | |
akṣataiḥ sumanobhiśca vikīrṇaṃ ca samantataḥ  |
yaddavyanirmitaḥ kumbhastaddravyāt karkarī bhavet  | | 9.15  | |
śatakṛtvo'stramantreṇa karkarīṃ cābhimantrayet  |
vedikāṃ paritaḥ siñcet dhārayācchinnayā guruḥ  | | 9.16  | |
cakrabjasyeśadigbhāge kumbhasthāpanamāvaret  |
vrīhin nidhāya suśubhān kumbhaṃ tasyopira nyaset  | | 9.17  | |
kumbhasyāntaḥ phalaṃ tasya phalani parito nyaset  |
kalaśaṃ dakṣiṇe pārśve vāme ca karakaṃ nyaset  | | 9.18  | |
kumbhe ca harimāvāhma pūjayenmūlavidyayā  |
maṇḍale ca yajed devamupacāraiśca ṣoḍaśauḥ  | | 9.9  | |
saha sarvaiḥ parīvāraiḥ śāstracoditavartsanā  |
paścime ca hariṃ devaṃ maṇḍalāduktvartmanā  | | 9.20  | |
hariṃ ca lakṣmībhūmibhyāṃ śayānaṃ parikalpya ca  |
bhogairardhyādibhiḥ sarvairnaivedyāntaiḥ sabharcayet  | | 9.21  | |
mathitvāgriṃ ca mūlena kuṇḍe taṃ vinyasedgruruḥ  |
tatra siddhaṃ caruṃ kṛtvā taṇḍulaiḥ susitaiḥ śubhaiḥ  | | 9.22  | |
caturbhāgaikabhāgaṃ tu maṇḍalasthāya viṣṇave  |
nivedya caikaṃ bhāgaṃ tu kumbhasthāya nivedayet  | | 9.23  | |
juhuyādagraye bhāgaṃ bhāgaṃ bhuñjīta tatsvayam  | |
samidho'ṣṭhottaraśataṃ juhuyānmūlavidyayā  | | 9.24  | |
saruṃ puruṣasūktena tāvatsaṃkhyaṃ hunedbudhaḥ  |
pūrṇāhutiṃ vidhāyānte srātaṃ śiṣyaṃ jitendriyam  | | 9.25  | |
vasānaṃ cāhate śubhre vāsasī bhūṣaṇottamaiḥ  |
bhūṣitaṃ cānuliptāṅgaṃ śuddhamālyavibhūṣitam  | | 9.26  | |
āhūya navavastr?eṇa nege badhrīta vāgyataḥ  |
guruḥ svadakṣiṇe bhāge prāṅmukhaṃ copaveśya tam  | | 9.27  | |
anvārabdhastena caiva ghṛtena caruṇāpi ca  |
āhutīrdvādaśa hunet pṛthak pṛthaganukramāt  | | 9.28  | |
hutvā tilaiḥ sakusumaistridhā mūdhn?ryasya cālabhet  |
hutabhasma samādāya sitamaṣṭākṣareṇa tu  | | 9.29  | |
ūrdhyapuṇḍraṃ ca vinyasya badhvā pratisaraṃ kare  |
dakṣiṇe cāsmantreṇa prāśayet pañcagavyakam  | | 9.30  | |
praṇavena caruṃ paścād bhojayedātmaśuddhaye  |
dantakāṣṭhena dantāṃśca śo dhayitvā kṣipecca tat  | | 9.31  | |
tadagrapatansthānaṃ samyagālokayedgruruḥ  |
yāmyanaiṛrta[vāyavyaṃ](vye?) yadagramaśubhaṃ hi tat  | | 9.32  | |
anyadikṣu śubhaṃ brūyāt śantihomena cāśubham  |
śamayennārasiṃhena manunāṣṭaśatāhutīḥ  | | 9.33  | |
tilānācamayecchiṣyaṃ devasyālokya pādayoḥ  |
guruśca praṇato mūrdhrā devaṃ vijñāpayet tataḥ  | | 9.34  | |
saṃsārapāśabaddhānāṃ paśūnāṃ pāśa[mokṣaṇe](cane?)  |
tvameva śaraṇaṃ deva gatiranyā na vidyate  | | 9.35  | |
pāśa[mokṣaṇa](cana?)heturyastvatsamārādhanātmakaḥ  |
tenemān janmapāśena pāśitān paśujanmanaḥ  | | 9.36  | |
vipāśayāmi deveśa tadanujñātumarhasi  |
iti vijñāpya deveśa śiṣyaṃ sūtreṇa veṣṭayet  | | 9.37  | |
kṛtvā ṭṛḍhaṃ kṛṣṇavarṇaustrisūtraistriguṇīkṛtam  |
punastat triguṇīkṛtya taṃ pādādi śiro'vadhi  | | 9.38  | |
pañcaviṃśativārāṃstu veṣṭayecchiṣyadehake  |
tatsaṃspṛṣṭo'tha juhuyāt sarpiṣāṣṭottaraṃ śatam  | | 9.39  | |
pūrṇāhutiṃ vidhāyātha svapradarśanasiddhaye  |
svaprādhipatimantneṇa tāvatsaṃkhyaṃ hunedgruruḥ  | | 9.40  | |
māṣaudanabaliṃ dadyādūtebhyaśca samāhitaḥ  |
śiṣyasya netrabandhaṃ ca vistraya ca śarīragam  | | 9.41  | |
ādāya māyāsūtraṃ taccharāve nikṣipedatha  |
pighāya tatkumbhapārśve sthāpayitvā dvijottama  | | 9.42  | |
darbhān saṃstīrya teṣyavenaṃ sthāpayet prāvicharo yathā  |
kumbhamaṇḍalagaṃ devaṃ tadā nodvāsayedgruruḥ  | | 9.43  | |
prātaḥ pūcchecca śiṣyaṃ taṃ svaprasya ca śubhāśubham  |
śubhaṃ ceddīkṣayedenamaśubhaṃ cenna dīkṣayet  | | 9.44  | |
śāntihomena tacchāntiṃ kṛtvā strātvā śucistataḥ  |
saśiṣyaḥ praviśettatra maṇḍape yāgasaṃbhṛte  | | 9.45  | |
dhvajaṃ ca toraṇaṃ caiva pūjayitvā vidhānataḥ  |
kumbhagaṇḍalasaṃsthaṃ taṃ devamabhyarcya bhaktitaḥ  | | 9.46  | |
pāyasādi nivedyānnaṃ praṇamyaiva pradakṣiṇam  |
netrabandhaṃ ca śiṣyasya kṛtvā pārśve niveśya ca  | | 9.47  | |
deśiko mūlamantneṇa samidājyatilān pṛthak  |
aṣṭottaraśataṃ hutvā pūrṇāntamavadhānataḥ  | | 9.48  | |
ādāya māyāsūtraṃ tat chitvā vai tattvasaṃkhyayā  |
saṃhārakramayogena [sāmeṣaḥ (?)]pañcaviṃśatiḥ  | | 9.49  | |
prakṛtyādīni saṃsmṛtya tattvāni juhuyādguruḥ  |
pṛthagaṣṭaśataṃ vārānaṣṭakṛtvo'thavā hunet  | | 9.50  | |
guruḥ svahṛdayāmbhojakarṇikāyāṃ susaṃsthitam  |
dhyātvā nārāyaṇaṃ devaṃ paramātmānamacyutam  | | 9.51  | |
śiṣyasya jīvaṃ saṃsmṛtya tasminneva praveśayet  |
śoṣaṇādi vidhāyātha śiṣyadehasya deśikaḥ  | | 9.52  | |
punaḥ svahṛdayābjaṃ ca sajīvaṃ saṃkramayya ca  |
sṛṣṭhikrameṇa tattvāni prakṛtyā viniyojayet  | | 9.53  | |
sṛṣṭikāle ca tattvānāṃ juhuyācca yathākramam  |
spṛṣṭamā śṣyiśarīraṃ tu tanmadhye śiṣyasaṃyutam  | | 9.54  | |
dhyātvā tatra sthitaṃ devaṃ vastreṇāmūlavidyayā  |
netrabandhaṃ ca [vinya](sra?)sya paridhāyāmbarāntaram  | | 9.55  | |
pādau prakṣālayitvā tamācamayyātha cakṣuṣī  |
vāso'ntareṇa badhvātha svapārśce saṃniveśya ca  | | 9.56  | |
āsīnasya prāṅyukhasya śodhayedadhvapaddhatim  |
kālādhvānaṃ padādhvānaṃ bhuvanādhvānamanvataḥ  | | 9.57  | |
tattvādhvānaṃ ca tadanu mantrādhvānamanantaram  |
varṇādhvānaṃ ca matimāt śodhayitvā ?svavidyayā  | | 9.58  | |
mahāvyāhṛtibhiḥ pūrvamājyahomaṃ vidhāya tu  |
hutvāśiṣṭenājyena śiṣyapādau spṛśedgruruḥ  | | 9.59  | |
hutvā tatastilairnābhiṃ spṛśettadaviśeṣataḥ  |
pahmahomaṃ vidhāyāsya hṛdayaṃ ca samālabhet  | | 9.60  | |
tatra hutvā ca caruṇā saṃpṛśedasya mastakam  |
pṛthagaṣṭhaśataṃ homo mahāvyāhṛtibhirbhavet  | | 9.61  | |
tato gṛhītvā śiṣyāsya karaṃ dakṣiṇamātmanaḥ  |
dakṣiṇena kareṇaiva kṛtvā caiva pradakṣiṇam  | | 9.62  | |
kumbhamaṇḍalagaṃ devaṃ nārāyaṇamananyadhīḥ  |
sthitvaiva tasmin dvārbhāge hemaratnādikaistataḥ  | | 9.63  | |
kusumaiḥ kevalairvāsya pūrayedañjaliṃ budhaḥ  |
prakṣepayenmaṇḍale taṃ yatra puṣpāñjaliśca saḥ  | | 9.64  | |
bhūbhāge patitastattanmūrtilanāsya nirdiśet  |
nāmno'nte tasya śiṣyasya keśavādipadaṃ bhavet  | | 9.65  | |
yadvā bhāgavato vāpi tathā bhaṭṭārako'pi vā  |
viprasya kṣatriyasyāpi nāmno'nte devaśabdakaḥ  | | 9.66  | |
pāladāsāntamevaṃ syānnāma vaiśyacaturthayoḥ  |
digbandhamo[kṣaṇaṃ](canaṃ ?) kṛtvā maṇḍalaṃ darśayetattaḥ  | | 9.67  | |
sthāpitaiḥ kalaśairetaṃ navabhistryadhikaistu vā  |
snāpayitvā navaṃ vāsaḥ paridhāpya padadvayam  | | 9.68  | |
kṣālayitvā tataścainamācamayya dvijottama  |
guruḥ śiṣyeṇa sahito devaṃ dhyāyet samāhitaḥ  | | 9.69  | |
śiṣyasya dakṣiṇe karṇe praṇavādyaṅgasaṃyutam  |
ṛṣ?yādisahitaṃ mantraṃ dadyādaṣṭākṣarāhṛyam  | | 9.70  | |
dvādaśārṇaṃ ca tadanu mūrtimantrān yathākramam  |
ekaṃ yathā gururdadyāt tanmantrasya vaśena ca (?)  | | 9.71  | |
śūdraṇārṇaṃ ca tathā strīṇāmanulomabhuvāmapi  |
namaḥsvāhāḍuṃphaḍādirahitaṃ praṇavena ca  | | 9.72  | |
varjitaṃ viṣṇunāmaiva caturthyantaṃ ca pāṭhayet  |
gāyatrīṃ japahomaṃ ca ādadyād brāhmaṇo manum  | | 9.73  | |
tataḥ śiṣyaḥ svayaṃ devaṃ kumbhamaṇḍalagaṃ harim  |
ārādhayedruruṃ paścāt praṇatasyotthitasya ca  | | 9.74  | |
kṛtāñkalipuṭasyāsya śipyasya śirasi svayam  |
cakrabjamaṇḍalaṃ dhyātvā svahaste viṣṇuhastakam  | | 9.75  | |
nikṣipya deśikaḥ paścādācārānatha śikṣayet  |
mantro nānyasya vaktavyo na pradarśyākṣamālikā  | | 9.75  | |
mudrāṃ na darśayet khasya mantrasiddhiṃ vadenna ca  |
gurornāma na vaktvayamasyādeśaṃ na laṅghayet  | | 9.77  | |
sadāsannaṃ ca śaiṣṭyaṃ ca pādukāṃ nāpi laṅghayet  |
dūṣaṇaṃ na gurorvrūyāt nācarettasya cāpriyam  | | 9.78  | |
gurutrakalatrādau guruvadabhaktimāvaret  |
mantraṃ guruṃ devatāṃ ca samabuddhayābhipūjayet  | | 9.79  | |
nāstikān na spūśeccaiva nābhibhāṣeta taiḥ samam  |
gurudevāgripurataḥ pādau naiva prasārayet  | | 9.80  | |
devārcanamakṛtvā tu na kiṃcidapi bhakṣayet  |
sandhyāsu tajjapastotrapraṇāmādīn samācaret  | | 9.81  | |
vaiṣṇavāṃśca yatīn ṭṛṣṭvā praṇameddaṇḍavat kṣitau  |
gaccham̐stiṣṭham̐stathāsīno [bhuñjan] (?) svapnaparāyaṇaḥ  | | 9.82  | |
devaṃ nārāyaṇaṃ dhyāyet tadgratenāntarātmanā  |
evamādisamā(dā ? )cāraṃ śiṣyāyopadiśedgraruḥ  | | 9.83  | |
śāntihomaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā pūrṇāhutimathācaret  |
agrervisarjanaṃ kṛtvā kumbhamaṇḍalagaṃ harim  | | 9.84  | |
udvāsya praṇataṃ śiṣyamāśīrbhirvardhayedgruruḥ  |
śiṣyaśca dadyādgrurave gāṃ bhūbhiṃ bhūṣaṇāni ca  | | 9.85  | |
gajamaśvaṃ ca śibikāṃ dāsīdāsagaṇaṃ bahu  |
vittaśaṭhyamakurvāṇaḥ svātmānaṃ ca nivedayet  | | 9.86  | |
svaśaktyā bhojayedviprān vaiṣṇavān pratitarpya ca  |
bhuktyāvāśeṣaṃ tadanu tatraivāsīta vāgyataḥ  | | 9.87  | |
ādityāstamayāt paścāt svanuliptaḥ svalaṃkṛtaḥ  |
guroranujñayā gehaṃ gacched bandhusamanvitaḥ  | | 9.88  | |
dīkṣāmevaṃ tu yaḥ kuryāccakramaṇḍalasaṃnidhau  |
sa sava brāhmaṇo cidvān sa evāśramiṇāṃ varaḥ  | | 9.89  | |
sa etairnābhirvācya ekāntī pāñjarātrikaḥ  |
sūrirbhāgavataścaiva sāttvataḥ pāñcakālikaḥ  | | 9.90  | |
anyākhyābhirna vācyo'yaṃ yathaiva prākṛto janaḥ  |
samayo'tha samā(dā?)cāraḥ svādhyāyadravyasaṃgrahau  | | 9.91  | |
śuddhiḥ pūjā stutirdhyānaṃ ?vidhirityaṣṭadhā mataḥ  |
yo'nena vidhinā tiṣṭhet sa bhāgavata iṣyate  | | 9.92  | |
jñānādiṣaṅguṇagaṇo bhaga ityucyate dvija  |
sa yassinnasti bhagavān sa eva bhagavān hariḥ  | | 9.93  | |
tadīye yaḥ sunirataḥ sa bhāgavatanāmabhāk  | |

iti pañcarātre mahopaniṣadi viśvāmitrasaṃhitāyāṃ dīkṣāvidhirnāma
navamo'dhyāyaḥ  | |

<10>
daśamo'dhyāyaḥ
kāśyapaḥ
ārādhanavidhiṃ brahman mama vaktuṃ tvamarhasi  |
yena samyak kṛtenāhaṃ tarāmi bhavasāgaram  | | 10.1  | |
viśvāmitraḥ
ārāghanavidhiṃ vakṣye śṛṇu kaśyapanandana  |
saṃsārapāśabaddhānāṃ vimukterhetumañjasā  | | 10.2  | |
śuddhenaiva hi kartavyā viṣṇorārādhanakriyā  |
strānenāpi bhavecchuddhiḥ śarīrasya vicakṣaṇa  | | 10.3  | |
tasmāt strānaṃ pravakṣyāmi śṛṇuṣvaikāgramānasaḥ  |
strānaṃ saptavidhaṃ proktaṃ vāvyaṃ pārthivaṃ tathā  | | 10.4  | |
divyaṃ vāruṇamāgrayaṃ mānasaṃ mātramityapi  |
gacchantīnāṃ gavāṃ pādairudyatāṃ vāyunā hṛtām  | | 10.5  | |
dhūliṃ saṃdhārayen mūrdhrā vāvyaṃ tat pracakṣate  |
praśastavaiṣṇavakṣetratātayā śvetamṛtsrayā  | | 10.6  | |
mū?tīnāṃ keśavādīnāṃ nāmoccāraṇapūrpakam  |
lalāṭādiṣu yadgrātre ūrdhvapuṇḍrasya dhāraṇam  | | 10.7  | |
tatparthivaṃ bhavet strānaṃ yogivaryairniṣevitam  |
nirabhrapaṭalavyomri varṣe jāte ca sātape  | | 10.8  | |
tena saṃplāvanaṃ yatsyāt taddivyaṃ strānamucyate  |
nadyādiṣu taṭākeṣu sarvairyadavagāhanam  | | 10.9  | |
strānaṃ tu vidhivadyattadvāruṇaṃ paricakṣate  |
samidibhargomayaiḥ svasya havane vihite sati  | | 10.10  | |
tadabhasma saṃgṛhma kare mantreṇaivābhimantrya ca  |
yadūrdhvapuṇḍrakaraṇamāgreyaṃ strānameva tat  | | 10.11  | |
pādau prakṣālya cācamya dhautavāseyugaṃ dadhat  |
āsīnaścāsane śuddhe prāṇānāyanya vāgyataḥ  | | 10.12  | |
hṛtpuḍarīkasaṃsthasya hareryat smaraṇaṃ punaḥ  |
karoti mānasaṃ tatsyāt strānaṃ strāneṣu pūjitam  | | 10.13  | |
āpo hi ṣṭheti mantrāṇāmuccāraṇapurasmaram  |
kuśagraiḥ śucibhistoyaiḥ prokṣaṇaṃ yadvidhīyate  | | 10.14  | |
tanmāntraṃ strānamityuktaṃ saptaitānyuditāni te  |
kṛtvāpi vāruṇaṃ pūrvamitareṣāṃ vidhirmavet  | | 10.15  | |
tasmānyocyate strānaṃ vāruṇaṃ tacchṛṇu dvija  |
nadīsarittaṭākādi gatvā dakṣiṇatīragaḥ  | | 10.16  | |
udakāñjalibhiḥ pūrvaṃ tīraṃ saṃśodhya tatra ca  |
nidhāya tatra strānīyaṃ dhautaṃ vastrayugaṃ tataḥ  | | 10.17  | |
śucerdeśāt samānīya mṛttikāṃ vibhajed dvidhā  |
kuryādekena bhāgena śarīrapariśodhanam  | | 10.18  | |
strānīyādibhiranyaiśca śiraḥprabhṛti śodhayet  |
strātvācanya mṛdo bhāgaṃ sthāpitaṃ vinyaset tridhā  | | 10.19  | |
ekaṃ vāmakare kṛtvā ekaṃ digbandhamācaret  | |
bhāgenānyareṇaiva limpedgrātraṃ svakaṃ punaḥ  | | 10.20  | |
avaśiṣṭhena vai pīṭhaṃ tena tīrthasya kalpayet  |
tatra gaṅgāṃ smaredgriṣṇorvāmapādābjaniḥsṛtām  | | 10.21  | |
ardhyādikaṃ nivedyātha samāhṛtya jalāñjalim  |
trīn vārān sapta vā mantrānuccārya śirasi kṣipet  | | 10.22  | |
hṛdi nārāyaṇaṃ devaṃ dhtyātvā tatpādapahmayoḥ  |
adho niveśya svaśiro yathāśakti japenmanum  | | 10.23  | |
uttīrya paścādācamya śuddhavāseyugaṃ dadhat  |
kṛtordhvapuṇḍrakaraṇo devarṣipitṛrūpiṇam  | | 10.24  | |
dhyāyeddevaṃ tarpayitvā svasūtrokttena vartmanā  |
vidhāyāhnikaśeṣaṃ ca kṛtvopasthānakaṃ raveḥ  | | 10.25  | |
āgamya yāgasadanaṃ prakṣālya padayoryugam  |
ā jānu hastugmaṃ ca prakṣālyā maṇibandhanāt  | | 10.26  | |
ācamya dvāramāśritya uṅganyāsapurassaram  |
pāṇibhyāṃ tāḍanaṃ caiva kṛtvā dvārasya saṃmukham  | | 10.27  | |
vidhāya cāgriprākāraṃ tarjanyā cordhvasaṃsthayā  |
kavāṭaṃ vāyumantreṇa samuddhāṭhyāntaraṃ vrajet  | | 10.28  | |
devavrataṃ sāma japan ?dakṣiṇāṅghripurassaram  |
gandhādisaṃbhārayutaṃ japtvā varuṇaman?trakam  | | 10.29  | |
dvāḥsthānāmarcanaṃ kṛtvā sarveṣāṃ maṇḍapaṃ vrajet  |
sarvamaṅgalasaṃyuktaṃ ratnakuṭṭimisaṃyutam  | | 10.30  | |
sauvīratoṇaiścāpi dhvajaiśca samalaṃkṛtam  |
maṇikiṅkiṇimālāḍhyaṃ maṇipīṭhavicivrakam  | | 10.31  | |
māṇikyamālikā[bhiśca] (yuktaṃ?)lambamālāvirājitam  |
evaṃ kartumaśaktaścet manasaivaṃ smaredbudhaḥ  | | 10.32  | |
gatvā ca devapurataḥ puṇḍarīkākṣavidyayā  |
praṇanya daṇḍavad bhūmāvutthāya racitāñjaliḥ  | | 10.33  | |
āropya dīpanikaraṃ mālādyapi nirasya ca  |
pātraprakṣālanaṃ kṛtvā nirgate paricārake  | | 10.34  | |
śuklāmbarayugaṃ bibhrat sarvālaṃkārabhūṣitaḥ  |
stragbhirvilepanaiḥ śvetairalaṃkṛtaśarīkaḥ  | | 10.35  | |
ūrdhvapuṇḍrāṇi pinyasya dvādaśa śvetamṛtsrayā  |
devasya dakṣiṇe pārśve vṛsyādāvāsane śubhe  | | 10.36  | |
ustramantreṇa saṃśuddhe svayamāsīta vāgyataḥ  |
badhvā pahmāsanaṃ vātha svastikāsanameva vā  | | 10.47  | |
śaṅkhakāhalasaṃśriṃ tūryaghoṣaṃ pravartya ca  |
tiraskariṇyā dvāraṃ ca nicchidramapidhāya ca  | | 10.38  | |
saṃkalpya vahniprākāraṃ tanmantreṇātmano bahiḥ  |
cakramantreṇa cābdhya cakramudāṃ svarakṣaṇam  | | 10.39  | |
vidhāyetthaṃ tataḥ kuryāt prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ kramāt  |
itthaṃ svarakṣaṇaṃ kṛtvā yogamudrāṃ vidhāya ca  | | 10.40  | |
nābhikande catuṣkoṇe maṇḍale vāyusaṃjñite  |
tadbījaṃ manasā dhyātvā tatsamutthena vāyunā  | | 10.41  | |
dhūmravarṇena dehasathaṃ pāpmānamapi śoṣya ca  |
hṛdabjāntastrikoṇasthaṃ vahnibījaṃ vicintya ca  | | 10.42  | |
raktavarṇaṃ tadutthāgrijvālābhiḥ kalmaṣaṃ dahet  |
pītābhaṃ kaṇṭhadeśe ca māhendraṃ caturaśrakam  | | 10.43  | |
bījaṃ vidhāya jvalanamutthitaṃ vāyunā saha  |
stambhayet kumabhakenaiva mūrdhri sphaṭikasaṃnibham  | | 10.44  | |
vartulaṃ maṇḍalaṃ dhyātvā vāruṇaṃ tasya madhyabhāk  |
tadbījaniḥsṛtenaiva kṣālayedamṛtāmbhasā  | | 10.45  | |
ā pādādā ca mūrdhraśca śarīraṃ sarvatastataḥ  |
bhūmiprabhṛtitattvāni?ni sāvadhānena cetasā  | | 10.46  | |
saṃhārakramayogena kāraṇeṣūpasaṃharet  |
ā pādādā ca jānvantaṃ pṛthivīsthānamucyate  | | 10.47  | |
tatrasthaṃ pañcaguṇitaṃ pītābhaṃ caturaśrakam  |
ghrāṇopasthasanāyuktaṃ tanmātrālakṣyate tataḥ  | | 10.48  | |
gandhe saṃhṛtya paścāttamapsu caivopasaṃharet  |
ā jānoḥ [pāyu](nābhi?)paryantaṃ [vāyu](apāṃ ?)sthānaṃ vidurbudhāḥ  | | 10.49  | |
tasmāccturguṇā āpaḥ śvetāścandrārdhasaṃsthitāḥ  |
rasanāṃ pāyusaṃyuktāṃ gandhatanmātrayā saha  | | 10.50  | |
rase saṃhṛtya paścāt taṃ saṃharet tejasi dvija  |
nābherhṛdayaparyantaṃ vahnisthānaṃ prakīrtyate  | | 10.51  | |
tatsaṃsthaṃ triguṇaṃ tryaśraṃ pāṭalaṃ vahnimujjavalam  |
sarasaṃ ṭṛṣṭipādābhyāṃ rūpe saṃhṛtya tatpunaḥ  | | 10.52  | |

dhenuṃ savatsāṃ sauvarṇakhuraśṛṅgavibhūṣitām  | | 10.170  | |
pātrasthairaṣṭabhirdhānyaiḥ saha mātrāṃ pradarśayet  |
ārcāya ca tāṃ dadyāt pūjāsaṃpūrāṇāya ca  | | 10.171  | |
annaṃ nivedayet siddhaṃ pāyasadyaṃ catuṣṭhayam  |
apakvairatipakvaiśca yathāyogaṃ prakalpitaiḥ  | | 10.172  | |
phalaiḥ sarvartusaṃbhūtairmadhurādisamanvitaiḥ  |
saṃprokṣya cāstramantreṇa parivicya ca varmaṇā  | | 10.173  | |
praṇavenārhaṇaṃ datvā dahanāpyanādikam  |
iviṣo doṣāśāntyarthaṃ kārayitvā pradakṣiṇam  | | 10.174  | |
mudrāṃ ca saurabhīṃ kṛtvā darśayitvā ca bhaktitaḥ  |
ardhyapātrasthitaṃ puṣpaṃ vinyasya ca yathākramam  | | 10.175  | |
viṣṇuhastamākhyaṃ ca kṛtvā vāmakareṇa ca  |
anvārabdhaṃ dakṣiṇena grāsamudrāyutena ca  | | 10.176  | |
devasya tvetimantreṇa kare devasya dakṣiṇe  |
nirvapet sāvadhānena bhaktiyuktena cetasā  | | 10.177  | |
ardhye dhūpe ca naivedye triṣu ghaṇṭāṃ ninādayet  |
datvā ca pāvanaṃ bhaktaṃ pānīyaṃ ca susaṃskṛtam  | | 10.178  | |
dadyādacamanīyaṃ ca mṛjet pāṇyośca candanam  |
nivedayecca tatāmbūlaṃ karpūreṇa samanvitam  | | 10.179  | |
saratnatilamātraṃ ca hmācaryāya pradāpayet  |
karmārcāyāṃ duditaṃ kriyājātaṃ dvijottama  | | 10.180  | |
tatsarvaṃ dhruvaberādau vidadhīta vicakṣaṇaḥ  |
kṣamāpayitvā deveśaṃ bhaktinamreṇa cetasā  | | 10.181  | |
parivārāṃśca gandhādyaiścaturbhiḥ pūjayed dvija  |
stotrāṇi vijñāpya punarmūlamantraṃ japed budhaḥ  | | 10.182  | |
aṣṭottaraśataṃ vāpi tadardhaṃ vā tadardhakam  |
ardhyapātrakaraḥ paścāt vinirgatyāgrimaṇḍapam  | | 10.183  | |
tathā homaṃ vidhāyātha gatvā devasya saṃnidhau  |
nivedya [tatkarma….na] (jātāṃ ?) devāpāmbujadvaye  | | 10.184  | |
mahotsavavidhānena parivārabaliṃ caret  |
devabhuktojjñitairbhogairviṣvaksenaṃ ca pūjayet  | | 10.185  | |
vijñapya devadevāya praṇameddaṇḍavat kṣitau  |
ādāya cāvaśiṣṭāni pūjāvastūni kṛtsnaśaḥ  | | 10.186  | |
gṛhaṃ gatvā ca tairdravyairhṛdayāmbujasaṃsthitam  |
arcayitvā jagannāthaṃ kṛtakṛtyaḥ sadā bhavet  | | 10.187  | |

iti pañcarātre mahopaniṣadi viśvāmitrasaṃhitāyāṃ
[nityārādhanakramavidhirnāma] daśamo'dhyāyaḥ  | |

ekādaśo'dhyāyaḥ
kāśyapaḥ
homaṃ kṛtveti bhagavān bhavatā vihitaṃ mama  |
tatprakāro na saṃdiṣṭaḥ saṃkṣepeṇāpi taṃ vada  | | 11.1  | |
viśvāmitraḥ
prakāramagrikāryasya śṛṇuṣvāvahito mune  |
dīkṣāvidhau prokṣaṇe ca pratiṣṭhotsavayorapi  | | 11.2  | |
prāyaścitteṣu sarveṣu snapaneṣu viśeṣataḥ  |
evamādiṣu cānyeṣu sarveṣvapi ca karmasu  | | 11.3  | |
sāmānyena vadānyadya havanaṃ śṛṇu suvrata  |
pacanālayamāsadya prakṣālya caraṇāvubhau  | | 11.4  | |
ācāntastasya yāgyāṃśe kuṇḍaṃ kuryāt salakṣaṇam  |
ekahastasamāyāmaṃ caturaśraṃ trimekhalam  | | 11.5  | |
oṣṭhanābhisamāyuktaṃ vistārasaṭṛśāvaṭam  |
mūrdhā tu pūrvadigbhāge tvīśānāgneyakoṇagau  | | 11.6  | |
bāhu naiṛrtavāyavyakoṇagaṃ padayoryugam  |
jaṭharaṃ kuṇḍamadhyaṃ syādyoniryonirvidhīyate  | | 11.7  | |
kuṇḍapaścimadiksaṃsthamadhyāsyāsanamuttamam  |
sottarīyaḥ sordhvapuṇṅ prāṅmukho homamācaret  | | 11.8  | |
kuṇḍaṃ saṃmārjya cālipya gomayena sahāmbhasā  |
kuśaiḥ saṃmārjya taireva prokṣayenmūlavidyayā  | | 11.9  | |
kevale sthaṇḍile vāpi havanaṃ vidhivaccaret  |
ātmāno dakṣiṇe pārśve vinyaset puṣpabhājanam  | | 11.10  | |
homadravyāṇi cānyāni vāmapārśve nidhāpayet  |
sakuśāḥ samidhaḥ sthālīṃ caroḥ pātraṃ ghṛtasya ca  | | 11 .11  | |
strukastruvau parirdhīstoyamakṣatāni ca mekṣaṇam  |
vyajanāni ca darvīṃ ca śuṣkakāṣṭhasamuccayam  | | 11.12  | |
agrerviharaṇaṃ caiva gandhadravyaṃ sacūrṇakam  |
nyasya dvandvāni caitāni prayuñjayāt sati saṃbhave  | | 11.13  | |
likhet prāgāyatāstistro rekhāḥ kuṇḍasya madhyame  |
udīcyaśca tathā tistro likhitvā mūlavidyayā  | | 11.14  | |
darbhādvayaṃ tadvisṛjya pārṇi prakṣālya cāmbhasā  |
mathitaṃ laukikaṃ vāpi pāvakaṃ tatra nikṣipet  | | 11.15  | |
praṇavena vidhṛtyāgriṃ samūhma ca paristaret  |
tribhistribhirdarbhakhaṇḍaiḥ prāgudahmūrdhabhistataḥ  | | 11.16  | |
dravyāgrimadhye vinyatya praṇītāmadvakṣatārcitām  |
apāṃ pūrṇāṃ tataḥ kūrcaṃ darbhābhyo prapighāya ca  | | 11.17  | |
prādeśamātranirmeyaṃ chedanaṃ tasya ca tribhiḥ  |
darbhaiḥ pavitraistaireva praṇītāñjalimarpayet  | | 11.18  | |
prokṣaṇīmātmanaścāgre sakūrcāṃ tāṃ ca nikṣipet  |
kūrcaṃ tat karayordhṛtvā sāṅguṣṭhānāmikāgrataḥ  | | 11.19  | |
tristrapūya ca kūrcena punarāghārasaṃyutam  |
homadravyāṇi sarvāṇi prokṣayet tajjalena ca  | | 11.20  | |
spṛśecca tāni dravyāṇi svasvanāmrābhidhāya ca  |
pradakṣiṇaṃ strāvayecca prokṣaṇīvāri sarvataḥ  | | 11.21  | |
praṇītāmambhasāpūrya devaṃ tatra sabhājayet  |
agrerdakṣiṇadigbhāge darbhairāstīrya cāsanam  | | 11.22  | |
kūrce brahmāṇabhāvāhma tadagre vinivedye ca  |
iṣṭvā gandhādibhirdravyairviṣṇugāyatriyā punaḥ  | | 11.23  | |
taṇḍulānāḍhakamitān sthālyāṃ nikṣipya śodhya ca  |
agrāvadhiśritya ca tāṃ pācayeccarumañjasā  | | 11.24  | |
gālitaṃ drāvitaṃ tvājyamājyasthālyāṃ nirupya ca  |
mūlamantreṇa darbhāgrayugaṃ vinyasya tatra ca  | | 11.25  | |
uttareṇāgrimaṅgāreṣvāropyātha savahinā  |
darbheṇa kṛtvā paryagriṃ trīn vārānavatārya ca  | | 11.26  | |
darbhamagrau vinikṣipya ghṛtaṃ svapurato nyaset  |
trirutpūyātha kūrcaṃ tamadibhaḥ spṛṣṭvā ca pāvake  | | 11.27  | |
praharedamṛtīkuryādājyaṃ surabhimudrayā  |
sabhājayedindramukhyān dikpālān kramaśastataḥ  | | 11.28  | |
strukstruvāvuṣṇapayasā saṃkṣālya kuśakūrcakam  |
tena tau strukstruvau mūle madhye mūrdhri ca saṃmṛjet  | | 11.29  | |
kūrcasya mūlamadhyāgraiḥ krameṇa munipuṃgava  |
amrau niṣṭapya codīcyāmātmanastau nidhāya ca  | | 11.30  | |
ghṛtaṃ struveṇa cādāya secayeddhomavastuṣu  |
saṃsicya sarumājyena pātre cāpyavaropya tam  | | 11.31  | |
pāyasannaṃ guḍānnaṃ ca maudgraṃ kevalameva ca  |
caruṃ caturvidhaṃ kuryāt culyāṃ vāpi paceccarum  | | 11.32  | |
bimbe nivedyate yadyat tatsarvaṃ juhuyādapi  |
pratyagārabhya paridhīn udaksaṃsthān nidhāpeyat  | | 11.33  | |
pariṣicyātha paritaḥ śikhīśānadiśi nyaset  |
samidhaṃ punāpyekāṃ praṇītopari vinyaset  | | 11.34  | |
nidhāya manasā dhyāyan sākṣāllakṣmīpatiṃ prabhum  |
ājyāktānekasaṃsthāgrān juhuyāt sakṛdeva ca  | | 11.35  | |
kuśakūrcāstṛte vāme pāṇāvājyaṃ nidhāya ca  |
prājāpatyamathaindraṃ cāpyādhārahutimācerat  | | 11.36  | |
ājyabhāgaṃ ca juhuyādāgneyyāṃ dakṣiṇārdhake  |
saumyamuttarabhāge ca madhye vyāhṛtibhirhunet  | | 11.37  | |
homakāle smaredagniṃ saptahastaṃ dviśīrṣakam  |
pādatrayeṇa saṃyuktaṃ jihvāsaptakasaṃyutam  | | 11.38  | |
varadaṃ śaktihastaṃ ca dadhānaṃ strukstruvāvapi  |
abhītidaṃ carmadharaṃ vāme cājyadharaṃ kare  | | 11.39  | |
kālyākhyā[yāṃ tu] (yastu ?)(jahvā[yāṃ] (yā ?)madhyamaṃ sthānamucyate  |
kṛṣṇavarṇā ca sā jñeyā karā?lī raktatavarṇikā  | | 11.40  | |
tatasathānaṃ pūrvadigbhāge pītavarṇā manojavā  |
sā dakṣiṇadiśi jñeyā lohitā vahnivarṇikā  | | 11.41  | |
sā syāt ?paścimadiksaṃsthā dhūmrā cānvarthanāmikā  |
somadiksaṃsthitā sā syāt śvetavarṇā sphuliṅginī  | | 11.42  | |
vahnikoṇagatā sā syāt viśvarūpātha saptamī  |
bimbīphalaruciḥ sā syādīśānanilayā ca sā  | | 11.43  | |
kālyāṃ śāntikahomaḥ syāt karālyāṃ vijayo bhavet  |
manojavā puṣṭhikarī lohitāyāṃ caśīkṛtiḥ  | | 11.44  | |
dhūmrāyāṃ māraṇaṃ kuryāt sphuliṅginyāṃ samṛddhayaḥ  |
viśvarūpāhvayāyāṃ syādaṇimādimahāphalam  | | 11.45  | |
tilānāghārasaṃmiśrān juhuyājjalaśāntaye  |
dviṣaḍarṇena mantreṇa hunedaṣṭaśatāhutīḥ  | | 11.46  | |
pūrṇāhutyavasāne tu vaiṣṇavāgriṃ prakalpayet  |
garbhādhānādi kartavyamagneḥ karma ca vaidikam  | | 11.47  | |
kuṇḍe lakṣmīmṛtusnātāṃ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitām  |
devaṃ nārāyaṇamapi dhyātvā sarvāṅgasundaram  | | 11.48  | |
prathamena tu bījena garbhādhānaṃ vidhāya ca  |
dvitīyena tu bījena kuryāt puṃsavanaṃ tataḥ  | | 11.49  | |
sīmantonnayanaṃ kuryāt tṛtīyenaiva sādhakaḥ  |
jātakarma caturthena nāmakarma pareṇa tu  | | 11.50  | |
ṣaṣṭhenānnāśanaṃ caulaṃ saptamenopanāyanam  |
aṣṭamena vidhātavyamaṣṭākṣaramanorayam  | | 11.51  | |
vidhiḥ kramādvidhātavyo dviṣaḍarṇavidhiṃ śṛṇu  |
caturbhirnavamādyaistu bījeḥ kuryādyathāvidhi  | | 11.52  | |
prājāpatyaṃ ca saumyaṃ ca tathāgneyamanantaram  |
vaiśvadevakrame tāni catvāri ca yathākramam  | | 11.53  | |
bṛtibījaṃ ca tuhuyād dvādaśākṣaravidyayā |
sāṅgena sakalenātha mantreṇāṣṭottaraṃ śatam  | | 11.54  | |

tanmadhye devadevasya yogapīṭhaṃ prakalpayet  | | 11.55  | |

tanmadhye hṛdayāmbhojādavatārya śriyaḥ patim  | | 11.56  | |
mantranyāsādi sakalaṃ yathāvidhi vidhāya ca  |
ardhyādīnāṃ ṣoḍaśānāṃ bhogānāṃ ṣoḍaśāhutīḥ  | | 11.57  | |
krameṇa parivārāṇāmevaṃ hutvā sakṛt sakṛt  |
evaṃ kuryādabhagavataḥ saptārciṣi sabhājanam  | | 11.58  | |
anantaraṃ ca bimbādāvārādhanavidhiryadi  |
vakṣyamāṇena juhuyāt pālāśaiḥ samidhādikaiḥ  | | 11.59  | |
samidhastālamānāḥ syuḥ kaniṣṭhānahanā api  |
sthūlodvegakarī hnasvā vṛṣṭyabhāvāya vai samit  | | 11.60  | |
ativṛṣṭikārī dīrghā carmahīnārthanāśakṛt  |
bhinnā kulakṣayakarī kṣatā bhāryāvināśanī  | | 11.61  | |
kṣobhakṛcca saśākhā syāt purāṇā putranāśanī  |
bandhunāśakarī cārdrā sajantuḥ sarvanāśakṛt  | | 11.62  | |
nirdoṣāśca dhṛtāktāśca hotavyāḥ samidho mune  |
caruṃ saddhtṛsaṃmiśraṃ grāsamudrāpramāṇataḥ  | | 11.63  | |
ājyaṃ struveṇa juhuyāt kareṇa samidho hunet  |
palaśaparṇena caruṃ kevalena kareṇa vā  | | 11.64  | |
akhaṇḍitāni puṣpāṇi phalānyapi huned dvija  |
dhānyānāṃ ca tilānāṃ ca taṇḍulānāṃ ca kāśyapa  | | 11.65  | |
pramāṇamaṅgulyagraistu yāvat sūddhāramañjasā  |
tāvatpramāṇaṃ juhuyād dhṛtenābhyajya mantravit  | | 11.66  | |
evaṃ yathoktavidhinā hutvātho deśikāttamaḥ  |
pañjopaniṣadā kuryāt prāyaścittaṃ ghṛtena tu  | | 11.67  | |
sadarbhasamidhaṃ caiva strucamāj?yābhipūritam (tām ?)  |
struveṇa pihitāṃ tāvannāsikāgrāntamuddharet  | | 11.68  | |
mūlūntreṇa juhuyāt pūrṇāhutiriyaṃ bhavet  |
darbhān sarvān samādāya viniyuktān yathākramam  | | 11.69  | |
[adyaprabhṛti(?)sakalaṃ] dagdhavā niravaśeṣataḥ  |
anuyājaṃ tathādhārasamidhau paridhīnapi  | | 11.70  | |
ūrdhvaṃ hutvā praṇītāṃ tu sthāpayedātmanaḥ punaḥ(raḥ?)  |
prokṣaṇījalamādāya praṇītāmavasicya ca  | | 11.71  | |
pūrvādi somaparyantaṃ jalaṃ saṃsecayet kṣitau  |
paścimena tu nikṣipya tanuṃ saṃprokṣya cātmanaḥ  | | 11.72  | |
brahmaṇamapi codvāsya pariṣekaṃ vidhāya ca  |
agrimadhyagataṃ devaṃ svahṛdabje samarpyaṃ ca  | | 11.73 | |

secayitvātha śeṣeṇa prokṣya cātmānameva ca  | | 11.74  | |
bhasmanā tilakaṃ kṛtvā gṛhītārdhyaḥ praviśya ca  |

ārādhanaṃ tadyathāvadgrṛhāṇa parameśvara  | | 11.76  | |

kṛtakṛtyo bhaveduktamagrikāryaṃ mayā tava  | | 11.77  | |

<12>
dvādaśo'dhyāyaḥ  | |
viśvāmitraḥ
nityotsavavirdhi brahman pravakṣyāmi śṛṇuṣva(?)tam  |
utsavaṃ(vaḥ?) trividhaṃ ( dhaḥ?) proktaṃ (ktaḥ?) tantre'smin brahmaṇā purā  | | 12.1  | |
pātrasthe taṇḍule prātarāvāhma parameśvaram  |
parikramed dvādaśabhiḥ śaktibhiḥ sahito mune  | | 12.2  | |
ahme (nne?)madhyāhasamaye sāyaṃ kusumasaṃcaye  |
athāvā triṣu kāleṣu balyarthaṃ cārayed dvija  | | 12.3  | |
balipradānasya kṛte sarveṣvāvaraṇeṣvapi  |
dhruvādāvahma deveśaṃ balibimbe niveśya ca  | | 12.4  | |
arcayedardhyapūrvābhiḥ saparyābhirdvijottama  |
tataḥ śuciṃ dvijavaraṃ śvetavastropadhāriṇam  | | 12.5  | |
sottarīyaṃ ca soṣṇīṣaṃ dhṛtadvādaśapuṇḍrakam  |
vaiṣṇavaṃ sumukhaṃ dāntaṃ yuvānaṃ japatatparam  | | 12.6  | |
garutmantaṃ bhāvayitvā [tanmūrdhri viniveśayet] (baliṃ tanmūrdhri vinyaset ?)  |
śaṅkhakāhalanādāḍhyaṃ tūryaghoṣapuraḥsaram  | | 12.7  | |
anyena svarṇabhṛṅgāraṃ dhārayedvaṇṭayā saha  |
aparo dhārayet pātraṃ balidravyeṇa saṃyutam  | | 12.8  | |
garbhagehadvārapāśvamārśvasādyātha udumbare  |
dakṣiṇe vāstunāthastu dvibhujo raktapātradhṛk  | | 12.9  | |
śvetavastradharo haste dakṣiṇe surasaṃśrayaḥ  |
gadodyatakaro vāstunātho dhyeyo dvijottama  | | 12.10  | |
kṣetranāthaṃ vaṇḍahastaṃ nīlanīmūtasaṃnibham  |
samuṣṭikaṃ vāmahastaṃ dadhānaṃ vāmapārśvagam  | | 12.11  | |
vīkṣamāṇaṃ vibhorvaktraṃ dhyātvā dvau pūjayed dvija  |
ūrdhvodumbaramadhyasthāṃ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitām  | | 12.12  | |
taptakārtasvaranibhāṃ vidrumapratimāgbarām  |
pahmakumbhakarāṃ pahmasaṃsathāṃ dvāraśriyaṃ yajet  | | 12.13  | |
bāhme garbhagṛhadvāraśākhānūle tu dakṣiṇe  |
baddhoṣṇīṣaṃ sordhvapuṇḍraṃ śuklāmbaradharaṃ dvija  | | 12.14  | |
hārakeyūrakaṭakakuṇḍalādivibhūṣitam  |
taruṇādityavarṇābhaṃ caturbāhuṃ ca dakṣiṇe  | | 12.15  | |
bhuja(hasta ?)dvaye cakradharamūrdhvahaste(?)gadāmapi  |
śroṇītaṭaniṣaṇṇāṃ ca viśrāntāgrāṃ bhukstale  | | 12.16  | |
jvalantaṃ dadhataṃ vāme śaṅkhamūrdhve'kṣasūtrakam  |
adhaśca caṇḍaṃ dhyātvaivaṃ pūjayed dvijasatama  | | 12.17  | |
vyatyastabhujayugmaṃ ca pracaṇḍamapi pūjayet  |
garuḍaṃ kāñcananibhaṃ kuṭikabhrūyugojjavalam  | | 12.18  | |
lambodaraṃ ca vṛttākṣaṃ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitam  |
nīlāmbaraṃ pakṣayugmamaṇḍalena virājitam  | | 12.19  | |
kṛtāñjaliṃ devadevasumukhaṃ(saṃmukhe ?) samyagarcayet  |
athāgramaṇḍapadvāraśākhādvayasusaṃsthitau  | | 12.20  | |
pahmagarbhanibhau caṇḍapracaṇḍasaṭṛśau bhuje  |
raktāmbarādisahitau pūjayed gaṇanāyakau  | | 12.21  | |
ubhau dhātṛvidhātārau prathamāvaraṇe tathā  |
kumudaṃ pūrvadigbhāge nānāvasrādibhūṣitam  | | 12.22  | |
caturbhujaṃ dakṣiṇakaradvandve śvetaṃ ca cāmaram  |
mudrāmabhayasaṃjñāṃ ca savye hārastrajaṃ tathā  | | 12.23  | |
tūṣṇī [mānama]saṃjñāṃ ca dadhānaṃ ca sabhājayet  |
kumudākṣaṃ vahnikoṇa vyatyastubhujayugmakam  | | 12.24  | |
dakṣiṇe puḍarīkaṃ ca kahnacābhaṃ garuḍadhvajam  |
dadhānaṃ naiṛrte caiva vāmanaṃtadvadarcayet  | | 12.25  | |
paścime śaṅkukarṇaṃ ca pañcarāgaśarīrakam  |
māyūrapiñchavyajanadhāriṇaṃ vāyukoṇagam  | | 12.26  | |
sarpanetraṃ ca vidhivadarcayed dvijapuṃgava  |
some sumukhasaṃjñaṃ ca śyāmaṃ chatradharaṃ bhajet  | | 12.27  | |
supratiṣṭhaṃ tatheśāne dhyātvaivaṃ ca sabhājayet  |
anuktaṃ śeṣameteṣāṃ kumudasyeva kāśyapa  | | 12.28  | |
ūrdhvapuṇḍreṇa sarve'pi lalāṭasthena śobhitāḥ  |
tadbāhmadvārapārśvasthaṃ jayaṃ savijayeṃ yajet  | | 12.29  | |
caṇḍapracaṇsaṭṛśāvubhāvetau caturbhujau  |
pītamālyāmbaradharau [pītoṣṇīṣaśirī] (dharau?) mune  | | 12.30  | |
paścādupendraṃ prāgbhāge vahnikoṇe ca sattama  |
tejodharaṃ dakṣiṇe ca duratikramasaṃjñitam  | | 12.31  | |
naiṛrte paścime caiva mahākarmamahāhnadau  |
agṛhmaṃ vāyukoṇeca vasuretasamuttare  | | 12.32  | |
vardhamānaṃ tatheśāne sākṣiṇaṃ gaganopari  |
adhobhāge ca sarvasya ādhāranilyaṃ kramāt  | | 12.33  | |
etān sphaṭikavarṇābhān varābhayakarān yajet  |
athavākṣastrajaṃ cakraṃ śaṅkhaṃ cagadayā saha  | | 12.34  | |
karaiścaturbhirdadhato makuṭādivibhūṣitān  |
tatastṛ?tīyāvaraṇadvārapārśvasthitāvubhau  | | 12.35  | |
yajedabhadraṃ subhadraṃ ca yathā caṇḍapracaṇḍakau  |
indraṃ yajeta tadanu śyāmarṇaṃ caturbhujam  | | 12.36  | |
sidvipasthaṃ kuliśaṃ bibhrataṃ tīkṣṇadhārakam  |
[ajārusaṃ] (ajārūḍhaṃ ?) raktavarṇaṃ hutāśaṃ śaktihastakam  | | 12.37  | |
yamaṃ mahiṣasaṃsthaṃ tu kṛṣṇaṃ daṇḍakaraṃ yajet  |
pretāsanaṃ khaṅgadharaṃ niṛrtiṃ cāsitadyutim  | | 12.38  | |
varuṇaṃ makarārūḍhaṃ muktābhaṃ pāśadhāriṇam  |
vāyuṃ hariṇamārūḍhaṃ kṛṣṇadhvajapadāṅkitam  | | 12.39  | |
śaśinaṃ śaśakārūḍhaṃ śvetaṃ śiśiraparvatam  |
vṛṣārūḍhaṃ pravālābhaṃ śūlinaṃ tryambalaṃ yajet  | | 12.40  | |
kūrmārūḍhaṃ haladharamadhastānnāgarājakam  |
brahmāṇaṃ haṃsasaṃsthaṃ ca caturvavaktraṃ smaran yajet  | | 12.41  | |
caturbhujāḥ sarva etesākṣamālāḥ paraṃ vibhum  |
dhyāyanta iti saṃsmṛtya varābhayakarān yajet  | | 12.42  | |
amareśavirūpākṣau tathā gopurapārśvagau  |
iṣṭvā caturthāvaraṇe cakrādyāyudhsaṃhatim  | | 12.43  |
vajraṃ śaktiṃ ca daṇḍaṃca khaṅgaṃ pāśaṃ dhvajaṃ tathā  |
śiśirāstraṃ triśūlaṃ ca krameṇaitāni pūjayet  | | 12.44  | |
mahāgopurapārśvasthau śūṅkhapahmanidhī ubhau  |
śaṅkhapahmasamānābhau śuklamālyāmbarāvṛtau  | | 12.45  | |
nidhibhāṇḍasamārūḍhau hasvapāṇipadau yajet  |
śaṅkhapahmadharāvetau nidhihastāvathāpi vā  | | 12.46  | |
iṣṭhvā bhagavato hetīraṣṭadikṣavadikṣvabhipūjayet  |
śaṅkhaṃ samusalaṃ caiva cakraṃ nandakameva ca  | | 12.47  | |
gadāṃ śārṅgaṃ ca pahmaṃ ca vanamālāṃ kramādyajet  |
devamāropya śibikāṃ balidānāya nāthayet  | | 12.48  | |
balipradānasamaye tattadrāgāṃśca gāyakaiḥ  |
gāpayitvā nartakībhistattannṛttaṃ ca darśayet  | | 12.49  | |
śaṅkhakāhalabherībhistūryaṣoṣeṇa sarvataḥ  |
badhirīkṛtya kakubho balidānavidhiṃ caret  | | 12.50 | |
dhvajaiśachatraiśca vividhaistālavṛntagaṇairapi  |
cāmarairdhāryamāṇaiśca saṃchāditadigantaram  | | 12.51  | |
kanyakābhirvicitrābhirbhūṣitābhiśca sarvataḥ  |
vasānābhiśca vāsāṃti paṭṭāṃśukamayānyapi  | | 12.52  | |
gandharvarājasaṃkāśairgāyakānāṃ gaṇairapi  |
purataḥ pṭaṣṭhataścaiva sevyamāno dvijottamaiḥ  | | 12.53  | |
paṭhadibhaścaturo vedān saṃghaśaḥ sevitaḥ pṛthak  |
sthāne sthāne tu tāmbūlaṃ bhakṣyāṇi vividhāni ca  | | 12.54  | |
phalākāni ca supakvāni sāpūpāni nivedya ca  |
vedavidabhayo gāyakebhyo nartakībhayaḥ prasādyaca  | | 12.55  | |
evaṃ sarvatra saṃcārya gopapuradvāranirgame  |
nivārya tūryaghoṣādi śaṅkhameva ninādayet  | | 12.56  | |
nirgatya ca mahāpīṭhasamīpe dhāmasaṃmukhaḥ  |
vādyavādiviśeṣeṇa ghoṣayitvā samantataḥ  | | 12.57  | |
saṃkṣālya pīṭhamūrdhānaṃ śucinā gandhavāriṇā  |
ārādhayitvā tatrāpi viṣṇupāriṣadeśvarān  | | 12.58  | |
kṣiptvā ca pīṭhaśirasi baliśeṣamaśeṣayet  |
gāyakairgāpayitvā ca rāgān bahuvidhānapi  | | 12.59  | |
upanṛtya samantācca nartakībhiḥ pradakṣiṇam  |
parītya vāratritayaṃ dhāmānta praviśet punaḥ  | | 12.60  | |
mukhamaṇḍapamāsādya śibikāmavaropya ca  |
datvā ca pāduke devamāsane copaveśya tam  | | 12.61  | |
ardhyādibhiśca sakalairbhorgairārādhayet punaḥ  |
upahārān bahuvidhān nivedyāvasarocitam  | | 12.62  | |
dhāmāntardevadevaṃ taṃ śākunaṃ sūktamuccaran  |
praveśya ca yathāpūrvamāsena viniveṣya ca  | | 12.63  | |
ardhyādi saṃpradāyātha bahirdevasya saṃmukham ( khe ?)  |
geyaṃ nṛttaṃ bahuvidhaṃ darśayet pūjakottamaḥ  | | 12.64  | |
ativṛṣṭimahātaprabhṛtikṣobhaṃsaṃbhave  |
nityotsavo na kartavyo balimātraṃ pradāyet  | | 65  | |
caṇḍapracaṇḍayorvāpi kevalaṃ nikṣipedbalim  |
tayordvayorvā purato mūlamantrapurassaram  | | 12.66  | |
namo bhūtebhya ityukatvā sodakaṃ prakṣipedbalim  |
balipradānakāle ca saṃjñabhiḥ svābhireva ca  | | 12.67  | |
caturthyantanamo'ntābhiḥ praṇavādyābhirutsṛjet  |
svasvamudrāṃ pradarśyaiva nānyathā vihito vidhiḥ  | | 12.68  | |
iti pañcarātre mahopaniṣadi viśvāmitrasaṃhitāyāṃ
nityotsavavidhirnāma dvādaśo'dhyāyaḥ  | |

<13>
trayodarśo'dhyāyaḥ
kāśyapaḥ
bhagavan mantramudrāṇāṃ prabhedaṃ [vinibodha me] (viniyojanam?)  |
vaktumarhasi sarvajña dayā cenmayi vartate  | | 13.1  | |
viśvāmitraḥ
aṅgalīnāṃ samāveśabhedo mudreti kathyate  |
mudaṃ drāvayate yastmād duṣṭanāṃ darśanāt kṣaṇāt  | | 13.2  | |
tato mudreti kathitā mantratantranviśāradaiḥ  |
hiṃsakānāṃ nirasanaṃ devānāṃ prīṇanaṃ bhavet  | | 13.3  | |
karmādau darśanādāsāṃ tasmādetāḥ pradarśayet  |
mantrajātas?ya saṃkhyānaṃ yathāgraptaṃ vidhīyate  | | 13.4  | |
tathaiva mudrāṃ rahasi darśayenna janāmrataḥ  |
karāvubhau ca saṃkṣālya pañcagavyairviśodhya ca  | | 13.5  | |
vilipya ca śubhairgandhai mudrāṃ paścāt pradarśayet  |
madhyamānābhikāṅgulyormadhye'ṅgaṣṭhaṃ samāyatam  | | 13.6  | |
praveśya hṛdaye muṣṭiṃ vidhāya sthāpayecca tām  |
hṛnmudraiṣā vidhātavyā mantrāṅyāsakarmaṇi  | | 13.7  | |
adhomukhīṃ vidhāyaināṃ mastake viniveśya ca  |
hanyādaṅgaṣṭhaśirasā tarjanīṃ śīrṣamudrikā  | | 13.8  | |
muṣṭiṃ kṛtvordhvagāṅgaṣṭhāmaṅguṣṭhāgraṃ śikhopari  |
sparśayediyamākhyātā śikhāmudreti sūribhiḥ  | | 13.9  | |
aṅgaṣṭhamud kṛtvā badhvā muṣṭiṃ samunnatām  |
pradeśinīṃ kiṃcidagraṃ natāṃ muṣṭyānayā punaḥ  | | 13.10  | |
varmabandhaṃ hṛdā dhyāyenmudraiṣā kavacātmikā  |
aṅguṣṭhaṃ tarjanī [paṅkti(?)tisṛbhirmuṣṭibandhanam]  | | 13.11  | |
vighāya tarjanyagaṅṣṭhe mūrdhrā (rdhro ?) maṇḍalavadbahiḥ  |
sphoṭayeddikṣu sarvāsu saiṣā mudrāstrasaṃjñitā  | | 13.12  | |
adhomukhīṃ śikhāmudrāṃ bhruvodhye niveśayet  |
cakṣurmudreyamuddiṣṭā kuryādaṅgeṣvimāḥ kramāt  | | 13.13  | |
dakṣiṇena kareṇaitā vidadhyāt kramaśo mune  |
tarjanīmunnatāṃ kṛtvā tisṛṇāṃ madhyasaṃgatam  | | 13.14  | |
vidhāyāṅguṣṭhametena svadehaṃ bhrāmayedbahiḥ  |
pradakṣiṇaṃ samākhyātā sagriprākaramudikā  | | 13.15  | |
svadeharakṣaṇaṃ kuryādanayā mudrayā budhaḥ  |
kṛtvā savyakaraṃ nābheradhastāt prasṛtaṃ punaḥ  | | 13.16  | |
uttānīkṛtya vinyasyet tasyopari ca dakṣiṇam  |
yogamudreti vikhyātā savyaṃ karatalaṃ punaḥ  | | 13.17  | |
nābherathordhvavidhibhiśrantaraṃ ca karaṃ punaḥ  |
samāṅgaṣṭhaṃ tadupari sthāpayeddvijapuṃgava  | | 13.18  | |
yogasaṃpuṭamudrā syādiyaṃ mudrāyugaṃ tvidam  |
yogināṃ tattvasaṃhāre samutpādanakarmaṇi  | | 13.19  | |
yogābhyāsavidhau śastaṃ kuryāttatraiva karmaṇi  |
kumbhamudreti kathitā yogasaṃpuṭamudrikā  | | 13.20  | |
chidritā ghaṭasaṃsthānā tathaivordhvamukhasthitā  |
tayā samācaret strānaṃ tarjanyānābhikāśiraḥ  | | 13.21  | |
saṃveśya kramaśaḥ kuryāt tathāṅṣṭhakaniṣṭhike  |
iyaṃ saṃhāramudrā syāt tattvasaṃhṛtikarmaṇi  | | 13.22  | |
udvāsane devatānāmetāṃ mudrāṃ pradarśayet  |
aṅgulībhiścatasṛbhiḥsaṃhṛtābhiśca saṃhṛti ḥ  | | 13.23  | |
tarjanyāṃ tu vimuktāyāṃ sṛṣṭimudreti tāṃ bidaḥ  |
āvāhane devānāṃ tattvasṛṣṭau ca sā matā  | | 13.24  | |
jñānamudrā samākhyātā tarjanyaṅguṣṭhasaṃhatiḥ  |
tattvamudreti ca vidurimāṃ pūrve tu sūrayaḥ  | | 13.25  | |
[uttānayordhva(?)gatayo]rhastayorubhayorapi  |
madhyamābhyāṃ spṛśenmadhyameṣā sā nyāsamudrikā  | | 13.26  | |
mantranyāse tu mudreyaṃ khyātā devātmikā yayoḥ  |
japamudrāṃ śṛṇu brahṛman aṅguṣṭhabhyāṃ karadvaye  | | 13.27  | |
tarjanyoḥ saṃspṛśenmadhyaṃ vistṛtāḥ syutathetarāḥ  |
japakāleṣu sarveṣu japamudrāṃ pradarśayet  | | 13.28  | |
karadvaye samastābhiraṅgalībhirdvijottama  |
seyaṃ puṭākṛtiḥ proktā mudrā brāhmī dvijottamaiḥ  | | 13.29  | |
karadvaye'ṅgaṣṭhayugaṃ kaniṣṭhātarjanīyugam  |
saṃyojya śleṣayedagraṃ viṣṇumudrāmimāṃ viduḥ  | | 13.30  | |
rudramudreti kathitāpyaṅgaṣṭhānyo'nyasaṃyutā  |
ubhayāṅguṣṭhamudrā sā kathitā tava suvrata  | | 13.31  | |
karadvayāṅgabhyāṃ(yena ?) śravasī pidhāyaikaṃ prasārayet  |
dakṣiṇaṃ sā bhavenmudrā pitṝṇāmabhivādane  | | 13.32  | |
vinyasya vāmaśravaṇe vāmahastāṅgulīstathā  |
prasārayet karaṃ cānyaṃ vidhrarājasya mudrikā  | | 13.33  | |
aṅguṣṭhayugalaṃ madhye tarjanyoḥ saṃhared dvayoḥ  |
eṣā bhāgavatī mudrā kathitā dvijapuṃgava  | | 13.34  | |
vārāhamudrā kathitā karayoḥ saṃpuṭe kṛte  |
spṛṣṭavā (smṛtvā ?) tu dakṣiṇakare raktaṃ sarasijaṃ punaḥ  | | 13.35  | |
trikoṇaṃ madhyatastasya bījamagreḥ samujjvalam  |
avācitaṃ nidhāyaitaṃ nikṣiped dravayamūrdhani  | | 13.36  | |
agnereṣā bhavenmudrā sarvadravyaviśodhinī  |
kare vāme sitaṃ pahmaṃ smṛtvā ṣoḍaśapaṅkajam  | | 13.37  | |
tanmadhye śaśinaṃ dhyāyet kalāṣoḍaśakānvitam  |
tasmāt stravantamamṛtaṃ smṛtvā [tadabhasma secayet ](?)  | | 13.38  | |
punastadutthitaṃ dhyonmudraiṣāpyānī smṛtā  |
[jaṅguṣṭharjanīyugme kaniṣṭhāyugalaṃ tathā]  | | 13.39  | |
śliṣṭaṃ prasāryaṃ saṃhṛtya madhyamaṃ dakṣiṇe kare  |
anāmikāṃ cetarasmin śeṣaṃ caiva prasārayet  | | 13.40  | |
mudraiṣā saurabheyī syācchodhanī sarvavastunaḥ  |
añjaliṃ karayugmena vidhāyeṣadvikāsayet  | | 13.41  | |
mudraiṣāvāhanakarī ṭṭaṣṭvaitāṃ devatāgaṇaḥ  |
saṃnidhiṃ yāti viprendra tasmādetāṃ pradarśayet  | | 13.42  | |
vikāsasahitā saiṣā hṛdaye śirasi sthitā  |
khyātā praṇāmamudreti kṣipraṃ devaprasādinī  | | 13.43  | |
vāsadudevādimūtīnāṃ viṣṇvādīnāṃ ca kāśyapa  |
satyādīnāṃ tathā mīnaprabhṛtīnāṃ ca sattama  | | 13.44  | |
prāsudanakarī mudrā sarveṣāmiyameva hi |
maṇibandhau saṃgamayya saṃprasārya tathāṅgalīḥ  | | 13.45  | |
madhye tu ku??ñjitaṃ kṛtvā tathā cāṅguṣṭhayugmakam  |
pā?tayitvā tataḥ pṛṣṭhaṃ sparśayitvā prāsārayet  | | 13.46  | |
pahmamudrā bhavedeṣā lakṣyādīn saṃnidhāpeyat  |
savyahastasya [muṣṭistama](muṣṭau tu a?)ṅguṣṭhamitarasya ca  | | 13.47  | |
nidhāyāṅgaṣṭharjanyoḥ(nyau?) saṃyojya prasṛte same  |
apasavyasya tisṛbhiranyābhirmuṣṭibandhanāt (nam?)  | | 13.48  | |
kārayecchaṅkhamudraiṣā jñānamokṣapradāyinī  |
tiryak karadvayaṃ kṛtvā maṇibandhāvasānakam  | | 13.49  | |
anyonyaṃ syādabhimukhaṃ maṇḍalīkṛtya kāśyapa  |
bhrāmayeccakramudraiṣā sarvaduṣṭanibarhiṇī  | | 13.50  | |
karadvayena muṣṭiṃ tu kṛtvāṅgaṣṭhau vināmya ca  |
prasārayedūrdhvamṛjuṃ madhyamāyugamañjasā  | | 13.51  | |
anyo'nyabandhanaṃ kuryāditarāṅgulayorapi  |
śleṣayet kūrparayugaṃ gādamudrā prakīrtitā  | | 13.52  | |
spṛśenmadhyamayā tarjanyagraṃ sā dhanumudrikā(dhanuṣo matā ?)  |
vāmāṅguṣṭhamṛjūkṛtyā muṣṭiṃ badhvetarāṅgulīḥ  | | 13.53  | |
bandhayecca tadaṅguṣṭhamitareṇa krameṇa ca  |
prasārya ca tadaṅguṣṭhaṃ mudrā sā musalāhvayā  | | 13.54  | |
aṅguṣṭhatarjanīvarjaṃ tisṛbhirmuṣṭibandhanaḥ(nam ?)  |
natāgrāṃ tarjanīṃ bhūyastadaṅguṣṭhena bandhayet  | | 13.55  | |
khaṅgumudrā bhavet sā tu duṣṭavarganibarhiṇī  |
lambayitvā karayugamanyo'nyasyāmalātmakaiḥ  | | 13.56  | |
nibadhyāṅguṣṭhayugmaṃ tu saṃharedvanamālikā  |
apasavyakaraṃ spṛ(spa ?)ṣṭamātmanastu parāṅmukham  | | 13.57  | |
parāṅmukhaṃ savyakaraṃ lambamānaṃ prakalpayet  |
varadābhayākhyaṃ tu mudrādvayamidaṃ dvija  | | 13.48  | |
indrādīśānaparyantametanmudrādvayaṃ bhavet  |
karadvaye pṛṣṭhayugaṃ saṃśliṣya ca kaniṣṭhike  | | 13.59  | |
badhvā prasārayedyugmaṃ tarjanyo ( nayau?) stuṇḍasaṃnibham  |
aṅguṣṭhayugalaṃ pādayugavallambayedadhaḥ  | | 13.60  | |
itarāṅguliyugmaṃ tu cālayet pakṣasaṃnibham  |
eṣā garuḍamudrā syādviṣasaṃhārakarmaṇi  | | 13.61  | |
aṅgaṣṭhena kaniṣṭhāditrayaṃ saṃhṛtya tarjanīm  |
darśayanmudrikā hmeṣā vipyakasenaprasādinī  | | 13.62  | |
sarvāṅgalīḥ saṃprasārya natāgrā viralāśca tāḥ  |
kṛtvordhvagābhinīḥ kuryādeṣānantasya mudrikā  | | 13.63  | |
vāmena tarjanīmūrdhvaṃ saṃsthāpyāṅguṣṭhakaṃ punaḥ  |
kṛtvoṣṭhāditrayasyāgre sthāpayeccaṇḍamudrikā  | | 13.64  | |
tathetarakare kuryāt pracaṇḍasya ca mudrikā  |
utthāpayedvāmahastamadhyamāṃ jayamudrikā  | | 13.65  | |
tathetarakare kuryānmudrikā vijayāhṛyā  |
anāmikāṃ tathā vāmātkuryādgrāṅgasya mudrikā  | | 13.66  | |
itarasmānmunistasya(dyāmunasya?) mudraiṣā parikīrtitā  |
śaṅkhasya nidhanāthasya vāmahastakaniṣṭhikā  | | 13.67  | |
[pahmaṃ svena rahasvastho kaniṣṭhordhvīkṛtā bhavet]  |
tathaivānāmikāṅguṣṭhe kuryādvastrasya mudrikā  | | 13.68  | |
muṣṭimudrā bhave nmuṣṭirveṣṭitāṅguṣṭhikā bhavet  |
snānamudrā vinirmuktatāniṣṭhāntā ( ktakaniṣṭhaḥ?)ṅgumūrdhani  | | 13.69  | |
vimuktānāmakāṃ tāṃ ca gandhamudrāṃ vidurbudhāḥ  |
vimuktamadhyamā saiva puṣpamudreti kīrtitā  | | 13.70  | |
yajñopavītamudrā syānmadhyamāṅguṣṭhamūrdhani  |
agraparvasamāyogaḥ kaniṣṭhāṅguṣṭhamūrdhani  | | 13.71  | |
vidhānaṃ pūrvavinyāsāt saiṣākalpasya mudrikā  |
karāvūdhvīkṛtāṅguṣṭhau vidhāyānyāṅgulīgaṇam  | | 13.72  | |
kṛtavyatikarāgraṃ tu kuryāt sā dhūpamudrikā  |
saṃhṛtya vāmahastasya kaniṣṭhāmūrdhvasaṃsthitām  | | 13.73  | |
vidhāya madhyamāṃ paścādaṅguṣṭhaṃ saṃharettathā  |
anāmikārjanībhyāṃ saṃharecca kaniṣṭhikām  | | 13.74  | |
kareṇa dakṣiṇenāpi vihitāyāṃ tathaiva ca  |
dīpamudrāṃ viduḥ prājñā devasya priyakāriṇīm  | | 12.75  | |
upacārantarāṇāṃ tu mudraiṣā syātkṛtāñjaliḥ  |
aṅguṣṭhamūrdhvagaṃ sthāpya śoṣābhiḥ kṛtamuṣṭikā  | | 12.76  | |
pratimāmudrikā caiva jñeyā devapriyāvahā  |
hastumuttānitaṃ kṛtvā kramādvati(kra? )kṛtāñjaliḥ  | | 13.77  | |
pṛthak kuryāt tathāṅguṣṭhaṃ saiṣā svāgatamudrikā  |
prārthanāmudrikā seyaṃ dhyānamudrāpi sā bhavet  | | 13.78  | |
apasavyakarasyāgramaṅgulīpañcakasya ca  |
anvārabdhenetareṇa paramānnanivedanam  | | 13.79  | |
grāsamudrāṃ viduḥ prājñāḥ sadā devaprasādanī(nīm ?) !
dīghīkṛtya kaniṣṭhāṃ ca tarjanīṃ cāppubhe hame  | | 13.80  | |
aṅgaṣṭhaṃ madhyamānāmamūrdhri saṃyojayedapi  |
mudraiṣā vrīhimukhsyānaṃ dhānyānāṃ havane budhaiḥ  | | 13.81  | |
ādhāraśaktipūrvāṇāṃ mudrāgaṇamathocyate  |
karadvaye muṣṭiyugaṃ vidhāyāṅguṣṭhamadhyataḥ  | | 13.82  | |
kṣepayedvāmamuṣṭeḥ syāditarā dakṣiṇopari  |
aspṛṣṭā syādiyaṃ mudrā saṃproktādhāraśaktijā  | | 13.83  | |
vyatyastā kūrmakālāgrermudraiṣāt syā priyaṃkarī  |
vidhāyādhemukhaṃ vāmamadhyamāpṛṣṭage ubhe  | | 13.84  | |
tarjanyanāmike kuryāt kuñcito(te ?)madhyamāmṛjūm  |
vidhāya prasṛtaṃ kuryāt kaniṣṭhāṅguṣṭhike api  | | 13.85  | |
anantāsanamudraiṣā nāsyāṃ yojayate yataḥ  |
karābhyāṃ muṣṭiyugmaṃ tu lagraṃ kṛtvāṅgulitrayaiḥ  | | 13.86  | |
tarjanyaṅguṣṭhayugalaṃ prāptalagraṃ ca mīlayet  |
pṛthivīmudrikaiṣā syācchṛṇu kṣīrābdhimudrikām  | | 13.87  | |
saṃślaṃṣya maṇibandhau ca hmaṅguṣṭhaṃ cāṅgulīgaṇam  |
anyonyasaṃmukhaṃ kuryānnakhāgraiḥ suṣiraṃ mune  | | 13.88  | |
tanmadhye'ṅguṣṭhayugalaṃ nirādhāraṃ pracālayet  |
eṣā sā mudrikā proktā paṅkajasya puroditā  | | 13.89  | |
saṃyojya hastayugalaṃ tarjanībhyāṃ mukhe mukhe  |
[amadhyake mastakaṃ cedbaddhaṃ kuryāditīdṛśam]  | | 13.90  | |
[tattyavatānāmikāyugmaṃ tatastaṃ copasaṃharet]  |
niyojayet kaniṣṭhe dve baddhe caivaikaśastathā  | | 13.91  | |
aṅguṣṭhayugalaṃ lagraṃ kuyāddharmādimudrikā  |
krameṇaitā vijānīhi nirvyāsiṅgena cetasā  | | 13.92  | |
tarjanyaṅguṣṭhayugalaṃ melayeddakṣiṇe kare  |
paścācchanaiḥ śanaiḥ kuryāt tadbandhasya vikāsanam  | | 13.93  | |
śākhāśaṅkha(saṃgha?)sya ca punaḥ samutthānāt pṛthagbhavet  |
dhāmnāṃ trayāṇāṃ mudrāḥ spuretā haṃsatya kathyate  | | 13.94  | |
hastau prasāritau spṛṣṭau vidadhyādañjaliryathā  |
eṣā bhāvāsanasya syānmudrā śuddhā paratātmanaḥ  | | 13.95  | |
[sarvā hastādayo vaktrādgṛhītvā madhyamāṅguliḥ] |
dakṣiṇasyaya kaniṣṭhasya śeṣābhistisṛbhirmune  | | 13.96  | |
muṣṭiṃ vidhāya cāṅguṣṭhamunnataṃ kārayedbudhaḥ  |
eṣā mudrā jayānāmrā niruktā mantravittamaiḥ  | | 13.97  | |
adhomukhe sthite vāme kare tatpṛṣṭhasaṃsthite  |
dakṣiṇe baddhamuṣṭiste (ṣṭau tu ?) viṣṇuṃ dhyāyecchriyaḥ patim  | | 13.98  | |
sarvārthasādhanī mudrā jayetyeṣāpi kīrtitā  |
garuḍoparisaṃsthasya devasyaiṣā priyaṃkarī  | | 13.99  | |
śaktiyuktasya devasya mudrāṃ śaktyātmikāṃ śṛṇu  |
uttānavāmahastaṃ tu prasārya viralāṅgulīḥ  | | 13.100  | |
kuryādāku?ñcitaprāntāḥ tacchākhāḥ setuvadabhavet  |
aṅguṣṭhasaṃmukhaṃ tāsu lagrāścāṅgulidhyataḥ  | | 13.101  | |
hṛdayābhimukhīmenāṃ badhrīyādiyameva sā |
anyāsāmapyanuktānāṃ mudrāṇāṃ dvijapuṃgava  | | 13.102  | |
pahmamudrāṃ vidadhyādvā yadvā kuryādathāñjalim  |

iti pañcarātre mahopaniṣadi viśvāmamitrasaṃhitāyāṃ [mudrālakṣaṇavidhirnāma]trayodaśo'dhyāyaḥ | |

caturdaśo'dhyāyaḥ
viśvāmitraḥ
pratiṣṭhāṃ śṛṇu viprarṣe devadevasya śarṅgiṇaḥ  |
sarvalokeṣu yadvastu sthāvaraṃ jaṅgamaṃ tathā  | | 14.1  | |
draṣṭuṃ yacchakyate sarvaiḥ śrotuṃ vā dvijasattama  |
teṣāmantarbahirapi(?) vyāpakatvena saṃ(yaḥ?)sthitaḥ  | | 14.2  | |
tasya viṣṇorbhagavataḥ pratiṣṭhā kīṭṭaśī bhavet  |
tathā hi mandabuddhīnāṃ viśvāsāyābhidhīyate  | | 14.3  | |
dhāmrastu pūrvadigabhāge dakṣiṇe cottare'thavā  |
paścime vāpi kartavyaṃ maṇḍapaṃ(?) koṇadikṣvapi  | | 14.4  | |
varjadantarāleṣu maṇḍapasya vinirmitim  |
ṣaṭtriṃśadibharyutaṃ stambhairyadvā ṣoḍaśamibhardvija  | | 14.5  | |
dvāraiścaturbhiḥ sahitaṃ darbhamālābhiveṣṭitam
kṣaimapaṭṭavitānāḍhyaṃ lambamuktāphalastrajam  | | 14.6  | |
dvārapārśvayuganyastatoyapūrṇaghaṭai(ṭaṃ?) rdvija  |
lepitaṃ gomayāmbhobhiḥ sudhārekhāvicitritam  | | 14.7  | |
dīpamālāparivṛtaṃ puṣpamālābhilambitam  |
pibhājayecca tanmadhyamekāśītipadaiḥ samaiḥ  | | 14.8  | |
tanmadhye navabhiḥ kuryāt padairvediṃ vidhānataḥ  |
vediṃ ca paritaḥ kuryāt kuṇḍāni dvijasattama  | | 14.9  | |
caturaśraṃ tathā cāpaṃ vṛttaṃ caturdiśam  |
maṅgalānyaṣṭa yājñīyairdramaiḥ kṛtvā ca vinyaset  | | 14.10  | |
mairī ca vṛṣabho nāgaścāmaraṃ kesarī tathā  |
patākā dhvajadīpāṃśca (dīpau ca?) maṅgalānyaṣṭa kāśyapa  | | 14.11  | |
pratiṣṭhādivasāt pūrvaṃ navame saptame'pi vā  |
pañcame vāpi divase maṅgalāṅkuraropaṇam  | | 14.12  | |
