adhivāsadināt pūrve dine munivarottama  | | 14.13  | |
bimbasya śuddhaye kuryāt jalādhivasanaṃ dvija  |
ācāryo yajamānaśca mūtirpapaiḥ sahitastadā  | | 14.14  | |
snātaḥ śuklāmbaradharaḥ svanuliptaḥ svalaṃkṛtaḥ  |
yajamānānukūlarkṣe muharte śobhane sati | | 14.15  | |
puṇyahavācanaṃ kuryāt brahmaṇairvedapāragaiḥ  |
prokṣaṇaṃ pañcagavyena kṛtvā sthānasya śuddhaye  | | 14.16  | |
praviśya karmaśālāyāṃ pratimāvalokya ca  |
astrodakena saṃkṣālya pīṭhaṃ ratnaśilāmapi  | | 14.17  | |
rathamāropya bimbaṃ tat sarvamaṅgalasaṃyatum  |
tīrthadeśaṃ samāsādya nadīṃ vā pārśvavartinīm  | | 14.18  | |
pavitramantraṃ prajapet trīn vārām̐stasya śuddhaye  |
kṣīrārṇavaṃ jale tasmin dhyātvā dārumayaṃ tataḥ  | | 14.19  | |
paryaṅkaṃ navavastreṇa saṃchannaṃ vinyasettataḥ  |
tasmin prāṅmastakaṃ devaṃ śāyayenmūrtipairdvijaiḥ  | | 14.20  | |
prāgādau śayanādbāhme kalaśānaṣṭa vinyaset  |
teṣvindrādīn samabhyarcya baliṃ ca vikarettataḥ  | | 14.21  | |
kārayitvā prapāṃ tatra tīre vedādi pāṭhayet  |
gītanṛttādibhiścaiva dinaśeṣaṃ nayeddvivaja  | | 14.22  | |
rātrau ca dīpān prajvālya rakṣāṃ tatra vidhāya ca  |
gatvā ca maṇḍalaṃ tatra vinyaseddvijapuṃgava  | | 14.23  | |
cakrābjaṃ svastikaṃ vāpi tasya tasya prāguttare dvija  |

tilaistadardhasaṃkhyaiśca madhye madhye paṭāstṛtaiḥ  |
vidhāya rāśiṃ tasyordhve vinyaset kumbhamuttatamam  | | 14.25  | |

kumbhamaṇḍalage deve pāyasādi nivedya ca  | | 14.28  | |
kuṇḍe vahiṃ pratiṣṭhāpya hutvā cāṣṭasahastrakam  |
samiccarudhṛtaiścai pūrṇāmapi vidhāya ca  | | 14.29  | |

prāpte'parāhṇe tadbimbaṃ rathamāropya deśikam  |
alaṃkṛtya ca dviksthairvedānadhyāpya ca dvijaiḥ  | | 14.37  | |
ekāyanīṃ puraḥ(naḥ?)śākhāṃ pāṭhayitvā ca tāṃ dvijaiḥ  |
śrīsūktaṃ bhadrasūktaṃ ca śākunaṃ sūktamapyatha  | | 14.38  | |
puṃsūktaṃ viṣṇusūktaṃ ca bhagavatpratipādakān  |
mantrānapi paṭhadibhaśca vaiṣṇavairbhagavanmayaiḥ  | | 14.39  | |
śaṅkhakāhalasaṃmiśratūryaghoṣapurasmaram  |
siddhārthānastnasaṃjaptānācāryo mūrtipaiḥ saha  | | 14.40  | |
vikirannagrato yātāt sarvavighropaśāntaye  |
grāmaṃ pradakṣiṇaṃ kurvannālayaṃ saṃpraveśayet  | | 14.41  | |
avatārya śanairbimbaṃ rathamaṇḍalasaṃnidhim  |
japan nṛsiṃhasya manuṃ sthāpayitvā tadagrataḥ  | | 14.42  | |

tvatsamārādhanārthāya devadeva śriyaḥ pate  | | 14.43  | |
prakalpitamidaṃ bimbaṃ tvadabhaktahitakāmyayā  |
pratiṣṭhāṃ kartumicchāmi prasadī parameśvara  | | 14.44  | |

pañcadaśo'dhyāyaḥ
kāśyavaḥ
maṇḍalasya vidhānaṃ me vaktumarhasi saṃpratam  |
sarvakarmasu devasya pūjanaṃ yatra kathyate  | | 15.1  | |
viśvāmitraḥ
yajñavṛkṣotthitai kāṣṭhaiḥ ṣaḍaṅgulaghanānvitaiḥ  |
dvādaśāṅgulavistāraiścaturbhiścaturaśrakam  | | 15.2  | |
kalpayenmaṇḍalāyāmaṃ vistāradviguṇaṃ mune  |
koṇeṣvetasya caturo vṛttāṃstālāṣṭakāyatān(?)  | | 15.3  | |
saṃsthāpayettadūrdhve tu teṣāṃ dārucatuṣṭayam  |
saṃniveśya catuṣkoṇaṃ tacchubhe bhūtale nyaset  | | 15.4  | |
tanmadhye(dhyaṃ ?)pūrayecchuddhamṛdā madhyaṃ yathonnatam  |
susamaṃ kārayettacca darpaṇodaravanmune  | | 15.5  | |
gomayāmbhobhirālipya maṇḍalaṃ tatra vinyaset  |
sūtrāṇi saptadaśa ca prañcayudacci nipātayet  | | 15.6  | |
meṭpañcāśatsamadhikamevaṃ koṣṭhaśatadvayam  |
ṣaṭtriṃśatsveva koṣṭheṣu madhye śaṅkuṃ nidhāya ca  | | 15.7  | |
sūtreṇa pañcavṛttāni bhrāmayetteṣu madhyataḥ  |
kṣetraṃ tu karṇikāyāḥ syādbahirbimbaṃ ca sūtrataḥ  | | 15.8  | |
vibhājayet tridhā teṣāṃ prathamā kesarāvanī  |
dalakṣetraṃ dvitīyaṃ syānnābhikṣetraṃ tṛtīyakam  | | 15.9  | |
tatsṛtravartmanā kuryād dvividhaṃ trividhaṃ tu vā  |
barmiṇḍalayugmaṃ syānnābhikṣetrādbahirdvija  | | 15.10  | |
arasthānaṃ bahirnebhibhūrekaṃ maṇḍalaṃ bhavet  |
aṣṭhāviṃśatikoṣṭhaiḥ syādāsanaṃ parito bahiḥ  | | 15.11  | |
saṃbhaveccaturaśraṃ tu vīthiḥ koṣṭairaśītibhiḥ  |
bahiḥ paṅktiyugena syāccaturaśraṃ ca tadbahiḥ  | | 15.12  | |
koṣṭhadvādaśakaṃ cordhvaṃ śataṃ paṅktayordvayorbhavet  |
pratidikkeṣu(?)koṣṭheṣu kuryād dvāracatuṣṭayam  | | 15.13  | |
pṛthak koṣṭhacatuṣkeṇa paṅktiyugmena kāśyapa  |
pratyekaṃ śaṅvidhyarthaṃ punaḥ koṇacatuṣṭhaye  | | 15.14  | |
mārjayet koṣṭhayugmaṃ tu paṅktiyugme ca kāśyapa  |
pratidvāraṃ tatsamīpe pāśveyugme mahāmate  | | 15.15  | |
urdhaśobhāvidhānāya bāhmānantararathyayoḥ  |
koṣṭhadvayaṃ bahiḥpaṅktayomekaikaṃ tadanantare  | | 15.16  | |
mārjanīyaṃ tu śobhārthamekaikaṃ cānyato bahiḥ  |
koṣṭhatrayaṃ mārjanīyaṃ pratyekaṃ dvijasattama  | | 15.17  | |
vyatyāsenopaśobhārthamantarekaṃ bahirdva(sra ?)yam  |
saṃmārjanīyaṃ koṣṭhānāṃ viniyogaḥ pradarśitaḥ  | | 15.18  | |
yadvā samantā[(tpīṭhādi ?)]karaṇaṃ ca vinā mṛjet  |
viṃśādhikena koṣṭhānāṃ śatayugmena kāśayapa  | | 15.9  | |
ādipaṅkajamāne vidhāyāvaraṇatrayam  |
caturdikṣu caturdvāraṃ kalpayet kaśyapātmaja  | | 15.20  | |
yadvā yatheṣṭakakubhi dvāramekaṃ prakalpayet  |
pratyāvaraṇabhevaṃ ca kurśad dvādaśapaṅkajam  | | 15.21  | |
ekaikaṃ koṇadeśeṣu dvārāṇāṃ pārśvayordvayam  |
daladvādaśakopetaṃ karṇikākesarānvitam  | | 15.22  | |
ardhenādyasya pahmasya pahmānyanyāni kalpayet  |
pahme tu madhyame kuryādbindūn dvādaśa kāśyapa  | | 15.23  | |
karṇikāyāṃ kesarorvyāṃ śataṃ navatisaṃyutam (?) |
rekhāṇaṃ ca vidhāyātha dalabhūmau catuṣṭaya(rdiśa?)m  | | 15.24  | |
koṇeṣu ca caturvidrekhāyugmaṃ dale bhavet  |
tatsūcyāgraṃ citaṃ(?) madhye kuryādupadalānyapi  | | 15.25  | |
arakṣetre pratidiśamare dve dve vidikṣu ca  |
tasyaikaimarāṇāṃ syād dvādaśaiva dvijottama  | | 15.26  | |
pahmapatrasamānāni tulyānīndīvaracchadaiḥ  |
yadvā yavodarābhāni mātuluṅgākṛtīni vā  | | 15.27  | |
pipīlikodarabhāni yathānyo'nyāsadṝṃśi ca  |
eteṣāmapi madhyāni tulyāni syuḥ parasparam  | | 15.28  | |
evaṃvidhaṃ praśastaṃ syādvāsudevasamarcane  |
nārāyaṇārcane pūrvaṃ bimbādyāḥ kathitā mayā  | | 15.29  | |
[aṣṭāvaṣṭau tu kāryāṇāṃ sabījanāvanirbhavet] (?)  |
ṣaṭtriṃśattantukoṣṭhāni mārjanīyāni kāśyapa  | | 15.30  | |
ṛte nābhyādinā kāryaṃ pahkajaṃ dvijasattama  |
nidhāya śaṅkuṃ sūtreṇa samaṃ dvedhā prakalpayet  | | 15.31  | |
ādyaṃ syāt karṇikābhūmirdvitīyaṃ kalpayet tridhā  |
tepvādyaṃ kesarakṣetraṃ dalabhūmirdvitīyakam | | 15.32  | |
antarālaṃ tṛtīyaṃ syād dvayośca dalapīṭhayoḥ  |
bindvādīn dvādaśāṣṭau vā kuryāt kaśyapanandana  | | 15.33  | |
etat syānmaṇḍalaṃ nāmrā bhadrakaṃ sarvakāmadam  |
svastikasya cikīrṣā cenmaṇḍalasya dvijottam  | | 15.34  | |
ṣaṭtriṃśatkoṇa(ṣṭa?)kān madhye mārjayitvā nipātayet  |
ekādaśa ca sūtrāṇi prācyu(ñcayu?)da?ñci ca kāśpa  | | 15.35  | |
teṣu dve dve ca saṃmṛjya madhye vīrthi prakalpayet  |
prācyāmudīcyāmapi ca paścātkuryādvinirgamam  | | 15.36  | |
prācyāṃ yaḥ(?) koṣṭhayugmena vāhmapaṅktāvudaṅmukham  |
prācyāṃ paśrimataścāpi kalpayeddakṣiṇāmukham  | | 15.37  | |
udīcyāyāṃ (cyāṃ ca?) tathodak syātpratyaṅmukhamapi dvija  |
prācyāṃ ca dakṣiṇāgraṃ syāditthaṃ svastikamaṇḍalam  | | 15.38  | |
ṣaṭtriṃ[śaḥkoṣṭhakaḥ](śatkośṭhakaṃ?) kṣetraṃ pūrvoktaṃ kāśyapa dvidham  | | (dvidhā ?) |
madhyataḥ paṅkajaṃ kuryācchiṣṭaṃ kuryāt tridhā samam  | | 15.39  | |
teṣu dvābhyāṃ tu bhāgābhyāṃ vṛtaṃ rsūryasya maṇḍalam  |
śiṣṭhenaikena bhāgena bahiḥ syādantarālakam  | | 15.40  | |
etatyānmaṇḍalaṃ sauraṃ saumyameva tadevaṃ hi  |
maṇḍalānāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ bāhmapīṭhādyathāpuram  | | 15.41  | |
yadvā vidadhyāt pīṭhādīn vinā cakrāmbujaṃ dvija  |
athavā cakrarahitaṃ pahmaṃ syāt kevalaṃ dvija  | | 15.42  | |
śobhayenmaṇḍalaṃ pañcavarṇai ratnaiḥ(kta?)sitādibhiḥ  |
athavā cūrṇitai ratnairlohairvā dhātubhistathā  | | 15.43  | |
yadvā candanakāśmīroṣṭhakādibhireva vā  |
athavā puṭakairgandhaiḥ kṣuṇṇairyajñatanūdabhavaiḥ  | | 15.44  | |
kṛṣṇaiḥ śuklairapi śyāmaiḥ kṣuṇṇaiḥ kausugbhapāṭalaiḥ  |
cūrṇitābhirharidrābhiḥ puṣpairvā pañcavarṇakaiḥ  | | 15.45  | |
śālyādīnāṃ taṇḍulairvā piṣṭhairvīhibhireva vā  |
yadvarṇā karṇikā vipra tadvastūdabhavavarṇakaiḥ  | | 15.46  | |
sarvatrākāraśobhāṃ ca kuryānmunivarātmaja  |
kṛṣṇaiḥ śyāmairatha bhavedantarālābhipūraṇam  | | 15.47  | |
karṇikāṃ śobhayetpītaiḥ sandhīḥ śuklaiśca śobhayet  |
raktaiśca karṇikārekhāmasitaiḥ kesarasthalam  | | 15.48  | |
pūrvabhāgaṃ dvidhā kṛtvā bhāgaṃ śvetairathottaram  |
virāmaṃ pītaraktaiśca śvetaiśca pratikesaram  | | 15.49  | |
kuryādbindūn dalāgrāṇi raktaireva vibhūṣayet  |
sitaraktairdalādyāntānyapi saṃbhūṣayed dvija  | | 15.50 | |
sitaiśca sarvavalayaṃ raktairvā paritaiśca pāṭakaiḥ  | | 15.51  | |
nābhidvandve śyāmapītaiḥ pāṭalairevaikanābhike  |
kṛṣṇairarāṇāmagrāṇi pāṭalairasaṃhatim  | | 15.52  | |
valayaṃ cāpyarāntaḥsthamasitaiśritrayed dvija  |
nebhibhāgaṃ dvidhā kṛtvā pūrvabhāgaṃ sitetaraiḥ  | | 14.53  | |
sitairvicitrasayeccheṣaṃ nomibhāgaṃ vicakṣaṇaḥ  |
pītaiḥ pīṭhaṃ pāṭalairvā śvetairvā paribhūṣayet  | | 14.54  | |
latāvitānapatrādiyuktāṃ vīthīṃ ca pāṭalaiḥ  |
raktaiḥ śvetaistathā pītairupaśobhāṃ vibhūṣayet  | | 14.55  | |
śuklai raktaistathā pītaḥ kṛṣṇerdvārāṇi śobhayet  |
yathāsaṃkhyān (khyaṃ ?) tataḥ koṇānuttarādīn sitetaraiḥ  | | 14.56  | |
śvetaiḥ śaṅkhān vidadhyācca svastikaṃ pāṭalaṃ bhavet  |
athā pītameva syādraktamādityamaṇḍalam  | | 14.57  | |
sitaṃ maṇḍalamindoḥ syātsarvataścāntarālakam  |
kuryādyatheṣṭavarṇābhyāṃ hastamātre ca maṇale  | | 14.58  | |
rekhāṇāmucchrayaṃ kuryātkaniṣṭhāṅgulisaṃmitam  |
dviguṇe'nābhikāmātraṃ triguṇe madhyamāsamam  | | 15.59  | |
caturguṇe pañcaguṇe tarjanyaṅguṣṭhasaṃmitam  |
vidhāyetthaṃ maṇḍalaṃ tu yatheṣṭaṃ pūjayettataḥ  | | 15.60  | |
mantrādhvānaṃ karṇikāyāṃ tattvādhvānaṃ ca kesare  |
daleṣu varṇādhvānaṃ ca nābhau ca padapūrpakam  | | 15.61  | |
adhvānaṃ ca kalādhvānamareṣu ca vibhāvayet  |
nemyāṃ ca bhuvanādhvānaṃ karṇikābinduṣu dvija  | | 15.62  | |
dvādaśākṣaravarṇāni(rṇāṃstu?)pratyekaṃ ca sabhājayet  |
parātmānamāvāhma sakalīkṛtya ca dvija  | | 15.63  | |
kesareṣu ca lakṣmyādīn (dyā?) dale dvādaśamūrtayaḥ  |
śrīvatsādyāḥ pūjanīyāḥ vyāptyādyāśca samantataḥ  | | 15.64  | |
pūjyā dalāntavalaye viṣṇuḥ syātpūrvanābhigaḥ  |
dvitīyanābhigo brahma tṛtīye ca triyambakaḥ  | | 15.65  | |
māsapā dvādaśābje(re ?)ṣu matsyādyāstadanantare  |
valaye nebhivalaye prathame śaṅkhapūrvakāḥ  | | 15.66  | |
hetayaḥ pūjatīyāḥ syuḥ pīṭhakoṇacatuṣṭhaye  |
vārāhanārasiṃhau ca tathānantahayānanau  | | 15.67  | |
āgreyādi sabhājyāḥ syurvīthyābhindrādayaḥ kramāt  |
caturṣu dvārakoṇeṣu(?) caṇḍādyā dvārapālakāḥ  | | 15.68  | |
rathyāyāmīśakoṇeṣu (tu?) viṣvavasenaṃ sabhājayet  |
pūrvadvārādbahirdeśe garutmān samupasthitaḥ  | | 15.69  | |
sudarśano dakṣiṇe ca gadā dvāre ca vāruṇe  |
uttare pāñcajanyaśca prathamāyāmathāvṛtau  | | 15.70  | |
māseśa viṣṇupūrvāśca pahmeṣu dvādaśasvapi  |
anantarāvṛtisthe ca pahmadvādaśake dvija  | | 15.71  | |
vāsudevādikāḥ kṛṣṇāvasānā mūrtayo dvija  |
antyāvaraṇapahmeṣu dvādaśādityapūjanam  | | 15.72  | |
ārādhanaṃ vidheyaṃ syādvāsudevasya kāśyapa  |
maṇḍale'smin mahābhāga paścānnārāyaṇe yajet  | | 15.73  | |
bindvaṣṭake'ṣṭākṣarāṇi kesareṣu ca śaktayaḥ  |
vyāptyādyāḥ pūjanīyāḥ syurlakṣmyādyāśca daleṣu ca  | | 15.74  | |
areṣu pāñcajanyaḥ syātpūjanīyaḥ pṛthakpṛthak  |
pāhmāṣṭakeṣu triṣu ca caturviśatimūrtayaḥ  | | 15.75  | |
triṣvāvaraṇadeśeṣu samārādhyāḥ pṛthakpṛthak  |
itthabhūte maṇḍale'smin nārāyaṇamananyadhīḥ  | | 15.76  | |
āvāhmārādhayeccainaṃ sarvadeveśvaraṃ harim  |
itthaṃ maṇḍalayāgaste mayoktaḥ kāśyapātmaja  | | 15.77  | |
ataḥ paraṃ ca vaktavyamasti cedvaktumarhasi  |
iti pañcarātre mahopaniṣadi viśvāmitrasaṃhitāyāṃ
[maṇḍalayāgavidhirnāma] pañcadaśo'dhyāyaḥ  | |

<16>
ṣoḍaśo'dhyāyaḥ
kāśayapaḥ
bhagavan gādhitanaya devasya harimedhasaḥ  |
viśeṣadivase pūjā vihitā yadi me guro  | | 16.1  | |
vaktumarhasi sarvajña karuṇākara sāṃpratam  |
viśvāmitraḥ
devadevasya viṣṇostu viśeṣadivasārcanam  | | 16.2  | |
śṛṇu sānugrahaṃ vittaṃ madīyaṃ tvayi vartate  |
māse śucau site pakṣe daśamyāṃ dvijapuṃgava  | | 16.3  | |
kṛtvā prātastanaṃ kāryaṃ strātaḥ śuklāmbarāvṛtaḥ  |
dhṛtordhpuṇḍro bhagavaddhāma gatvā praṇamya ca  | | 16.4  | |
vijñāpayejjagannāthaṃ devadevaṃ śriyaḥ patim  |
bhagavan sarvajagatāmāvāsa karuṇānidhe  | | 16.5  | |
vāsudeva śriyaḥ kānta sarvādhāra jaganmaya  |
cāturmāsye viśeṣeṇa pūjā yā bhavatoditā  | | 16.6  | |
tayā deva samāraddhumicchāmi tvāṃ janārdana  |
juṣasva tāṃ prasīda tvaṃ śaraṇāgatavatsala  | | 16.7  | |
iti vijñāpya deveśaṃ samārādhya yathāpuram  |
praṇamya daṇḍavad bhūmau stutvā stotrairapi dvija  | | 16.8  | |
prāpte madhyāhnasamaye gatvā ca saritaṃ śubhāg  |
tatra madhyāhnakīṃ sarvā tatkriyāṃ samavāpya ca  | | 16.9  | |
saṃprāśya pañcagavyaṃ ca devasya nikaṭaṃ vrajet  |
tatrāpi ca yathāpūrvaṃ vidhāya yajanaṃ hareḥ  | | 16.10  | |
nirgatya maṇḍape tatra yathāpūrvaṃ svalaṃkṛtam  |
vitānadhvajamuktāstraksumastragbhirvibhūṣite  | | 16.11  | |
yajñavṛkṣodabhavaiścaiva caturbhistoraṇairvṛte  |
kumbhāṣṭakaiśca paritaḥ pārśvayoḥ saṃsthitairvṛte  | | 16.12  | |
caturdvāre tathā lipte gomayādibhaḥ samantataḥ  |
citrite ca sudhācūrṇermadhye cakrābjamaṇḍale  | | 16.13  | |
maṇḍaleśānabhāge ca śālibhistaṇṅulaistilaiḥ  |
madhye madhye ca vistīrṇaiḥ paṭaiḥ śuddhaiḥ samāvṛte  | | 16.14  | |
māhendramaṇḍale lipte kumbhaṃ ca śubhalakṣaṇam  |
tantubhirveṣṭhitaṃ caiva kālamaṇḍalavarjitam  | | 16.15  | |
veṣṭhitaṃ kṣaumayugmena suśubhena navena ca  |
saratnaṃ ca sahemābjaṃ sakūrcaṃ ca sapallavam  | | 16.16  | |
saphalaṃ sāpidhānaṃ ca sthāpayitvā samantataḥ  |
evaṃvidhaṃ ca karakaṃ tasya dakṣiṇato nyaset  | | 16.17  | |
kumbhāṣṭakaṃ ca ?paritaḥ s?thāpayitvāṣṭadikṣvatha  |
maṇḍalasyottare bhāge kuṇḍaṃ kṛtvā vidhānataḥ  | | 16.18  | |
tanmadhye vedisaṃsthaṃ taṃ pratiṣṭhāpya hariṃ punaḥ  |
kumbhe ca maṇḍale caiva devamāvāhma pūjayet  | | 16.19  | |
annaṃ caturvidhaṃ caiva nivedya paramātmane  |
kuṇḍasaṃsthitadeveśaṃ samidibhaḥ saptabhiḥ kramāt  | | 16.20  | |
ārādhya caruśeṣaṃ ca svayaṃ prāśyāpi vāgyataḥ  |
aṅkurānarpayitvā ca gatvā devasya saṃnidhim  | | 16.21  | |
pūjayitvā ca deveśaṃ yathāpūrvamatandritaḥ  |
maṇḍape ca samāgatya japastotrādi?bhirniśām  | | 16.22  | |
nītvā jāgaraṇenaiva prabhāte strānapūrvakam  |
vidhārayan sāndhamapi cātmanāpi yatātmavān  | | 16.23  | |
āgatya maṇḍapaṃ tatra dvāratoraṇapūrvakam  |
sabhājanaṃ vidhāyātha kumbhamaṇḍalayorapi  | | 16.24  | |
nivedanāntaṃ sakalaṃ kriyājātāṃ samāpya ca  |
viśeṣayajanaṃ kṛtvā devadevasya vai haraiḥ  | | 16.25  | |
sāyaṃkāle tu saṃprāpte kṛtvā sāyaṃtanaṃ vidhim  |
ārādhya bhagavantaṃ ca vāsudevaṃ sanātanam  | | 16.26  | |
karābje śayanārcāyā bdhvā maṅlakautukam  |
ātmanaśca kare badhvā kautukaṃ svarṇanirmitam  | | 16.27  | |
prārthayitvā ca taṃ devaṃ bahirnirgamanāya ca  |
āropya yānaṃ deveśaṃ dhāma nītvā pradakṣiṇam  | | 16.28  | |
āgatya yāgasadanaṃ sauvarṇe viṣṭharottame  |
maṇḍalābhimukhaṃ devamadhyāsyātha sabhājayet  | | 16.29  | |
paramānnaṃ nivedyaiva kugbhamaṇḍalayorapi  |
hutvā ?cāṣṭottaraśataṃ mūlamantreṇa mantravit  | | 16.30  | |
kumbhāṣṭake ca paritaḥ pūjayitvendrapūrvakān  |
baliṃ ca kumudāsadibhyo lākekapālebhya eva ca  | | 16.31  | |
dikṣvaṣṭāsu ca vinyasya digīśebhyaśca sattama  |
sthāpayitvā ca kalaśān pañcaviṃśātisaṃkhyakān  | | 16.32  | |
strāpayitvā ca deveśaṃ śayane kalipte purā  |
śalīnāṃ pañcabhirbhā raistaṇḍulaiśca tilairapi  | | 16.33  | |
vistīrṇapracchadapaṭaiḥ kalpitaiḥ kambalairapi  |
nānāvarṇairmahārghaiśca sopadhānacatuṣṭayaiḥ  | | 16.34  | |
vayādhracarma samāstīrya tasyordhve kṣaumasaṃjñake  |
śāyayitvā jagannāthaṃ rakṣārthaṃ cāyudhāṣṭakam  | | 16.35  | |
saṃsthāpya pūjayitvātha mukhavarjaṃ ca vāsasā  |
devatācchādya kumbhaṃ ca śiraḥsthāne niveśya ca  | | 16.36  | |
nṛttagītādibhiścaiva vedānāmapi ghoṣaṇaiḥ  |
jāgareṇa nayedrātrimanaśranneva kāśyapa  | | 16.37  | |
brāhme muhūrte cotthāya kṛtvā [caiva prabhātakam] (prābhātikaṃ tathā ?)  |
vidhānaṃ ca svasūtroktaṃ gatvā yāgārthamaṇḍapam  | | 16.38  | |
dvārāvaraṇapūjādi sarvaṃ parisamāpya ca  |
sthāpayitvātha kalaśān pañcaviṃśatisaṃkhyakān  | | 16.39  | |
kumbhamaṇḍalagaṃ devamārādhya ca jaganmayam  |
karakasthaṃ ca ?hetīśaṃ sabhājya ca sudarśanam  | | 16.40  | |
kumbhe cāṣṭārṇamantreṇa hutvā cājyaśatāhutīḥ  |
utthāpya śayanāddevaṃ bhavabhañjakamavyayam  | | 16.41  | |
vedyāmānīyaṃ saṃstrāpya kalaśaiḥ sthāpitairapi  |
pariveṣṭya yathāśobhaṃ viṣṭhare copaveśya ca  | | 16.42  | |
nītvā pradakṣiṇaṃ dhāma kuryāduddhoṣaṇānvitam  |
praveśya bhagavadgrehaṃ sarvaratnavicitrite  | | 16.43  | |
bāla[yoge](?)ca paryaṅke mṛdvāstaraṇasaṃskṛte  |
canagarukarpūradhūpite vāsite'pi ca  | | 16.44  | |
sugandhibhiśca kusumairāmoditadigantare  |
upadhānasamāyukte mahārdhakṣaumasaṃsthite  | | 16.45  | |
śāyayitvātha taṃ devaṃ kṣaumeṇācchāditopa(dya copa ?)ri  |
rakṣāṃ kṛtvātha devasya hetīśaiḥ paritaḥ sithataiḥ  | | 16.46  | |
kumudādigaṇeśāṃśca sthāpayitvā samantataḥ  |
vijñāpya devadevaṃ taṃ gāthayā vakṣyamāṇayā  | | 16.47  | |
lakṣmīnātha jagannātha sarvalokaikasaṃśraya  |
bhaktānāmārtiharaṇe sarvadā dīkṣita prabho  | | 16.48  | |
yoganidrāṃ niṣevasva deva māsacatuṣṭayam  |
sarvalokahitārthāya kṛtārthīkuru māṃ prabho  | | 16.49  | |
iti vijñāpanaṃ kṛtvā praṇamya ca gṛhaṃ vrajet  |
tasmin dine prabhāte ca kalyāṇārcāṃ mahāmate  | | 16.50  | |
nirgamayya sabhāsthāne sarvaśobhāsamanvite  |
viṣṭaraṃ saṃniveśyātha pūjayitvābhiṣicya ca  | | 16.51  | |
mahāhavirnivedyātha sarvavyañcanasaṃyutam  |
mādhavaṃ yānamāropya sarvabhūṣaṇabhūṣitam  | | 16.52  | |
grāmaṃ parītya sarvatra dhāmri (mraḥ ?) sarvāvṛtīrapi  |
pradakṣiṇaṃ paribhramya niveśya ca yathāpuram  | | 16.53  | |
nirgatya svagṛhaṃ gatvā sahito vaiṣṇavairdvijaiḥ  |
bhuktvā yatheṣṭaṃ tebhyāśca dadyācchakyā ca dakṣiṇām  | | 16.54  | |
āramya tasmāddivasācchayānsya madhudviṣaḥ  |
viśeṣārādhanaṃ kuryādutthānāvidhi kāśyapa  | | 16.55  | |
pratimāsaṃ vidhātavyaṃ site pakṣe śriyaḥ pateḥ  |
dvādaśyāṃ devadevasya catuḥsthānasabhājanam  | | 16.56  | |
site pakṣe ca nabhasi dvādaśyāṃ parivartanam  |
pārśvayodevadevasya kārayitvā sabhājayet  | | 16.57  | |
aiṣasya śuddhaddhādaśyāmāsīnaṃ ca prakalpayet  |
ūrje māsi site pakṣe dvādaśyāṃ madhusūdanam  | | 16.58  | |
catuḥskthāneṣu saṃpūjya pūrvoktena vidhānataḥ  |
utthāca yathāpūrvaṃ kalaśairabhiṣicya ca  | | 16.59  | |
alaṃkṛtya nivedyānataṃ sabhājya ca sureśvaram  |
sarvavīthīḥ paribhrāmya mandirāntaḥ(raṃ tu ?) praveśayet  | | 16.60  | |
śyāne jagatāṃ nāthe vratāni śṛṇu kāśyapa  |
kuśodakaṃ pañcagavyaṃ prāpaṇaṃ cānusevanam  | | 16.61  | |
varjyāni śṛṇu viproderdivāsvāpaḥ parāśanam  |
yoṣitsevā tailasevā kāṃsyapātre ca bhojanam  | | 16.62  | |
māṃsāśanamamūnyāhurvajyāṃni dvijpuṃgava  |
vaiṣṇavāṃśca yathāśakti bhojayeddakṣiṇāmapi  | | 16.63  | |
dadyāttebhyaśca tatprītyā pratiḥ syāt puruṣottamaḥ  |
atha kṛṣṇasya vidhivadārādhanavidhiṃ śṛṇu  | | 16.64  | |
śrāvaṇe māsi pakṣe ca kṛṣṇe'ṣṭamyāṃ prajāpateḥ  |
nakṣatre vasudevasya devakyāṃ bhagavān hariḥ  | | 16.65  | |
sarvalokahitārthāya bhūmerbhārāvatāraṇam  |
kartumāvirabhūd bhūmau madhyarātre mahāmate  | | 16.66  | |
tajjanmadivase pūjāṃ saviśeṣaṃ samācaret  |
tasya pūrvaṃ saptame'hi pañcame vāṅkurārpaṇam  | | 16.67  | |
vidhaya maṇḍapaṃ kuryādyathāpūrvamalaṃkṛtam  |
kumbhamaṇḍalasaṃsthānaṃ yathāpūrvaṃ vidhāya ca  | | 16.68  | |
saptamyāmatha sāyāhne samārādhya jagadgurum  |
vijñāpya devadevāya samārādhanakarma tat  | | 16.69  | |
navanīlaghanacchāyaharervā sarvadehinaḥ  |
caturbhujasya vā haste devakītanujanmanaḥ  | | 16.70  | |
svarṇatriniṣkakaṃ sūtraṃ badhvā maṅgalakautukam  |
āroṣya yānaṃ sauvarṇaṃ paribhrāmya pradakṣiṇam  | | 16.71  | |
ānīya maṇḍapaṃ tatra jāmbūnadamaye śubhe  |
siṃhasane samāropya sabhājya ca yathākramam  | | 16.72  | |
dvāratoraṇakumbhānāṃ maṇḍalasya ca pūjanam  |
vidhāyoktavidhānena naivedyānte vidhāya ca  | | 16.73  | |
kuṇḍeśa(ca?) dvādaśārṇena mantreṇa vidhivaccaret  |
sthāpayitvā ca kalaśān pañcaviṃśatisaṃmitān  | | 16.74  | |
strāpayitvā ca tairdevaṃ yathāpūrvaṃ prakalpite  |
śālīnāṃ daśabhāreṇa tatpramāṇaiśca taṇḍulaiḥ  | | 16.75  | |
tilairapi samāstīrṇairnānāvarṇaiśca kambalaiḥ  |
āstṛte śayane devaṃ śāyayitvā jaganmayam  | | 16.76  | |
tatsamīpe samāsīno jitaṃ ta itipūrpakaiḥ  |
dvādaśārṇajapaiva nītanṛttādibhistathā  | | 16.77  | |
rajanīṃ jāgareṇaiva nītvā prātaśca taddine  |
strātaḥ śuklāmbaradharaḥ stragvī ca samalaṃkṛtaḥ  | | 16.78  | |
yāgamaṇḍapamāsādya dvārapūjanapūrvakam  |
kumbhamaṇḍaladevasya naivedyāntaṃ sabhājanam  | | 16.79  | |
kṛtvā śayānaṃ ca vibhuṃ puṇḍarīkāyatekṣaṇam  |
śyāmalaṃ bālapuṣaṃ susmitādharamacyutam  | | 16.80  | |
utthāpya jagatāṃ nāthaṃ maṇḍape viniveśya ca  |
mahāhaviḥpradānāntaṃ catuḥsthānasthitasya ca  | | 16.81  | |
vidhāya yajanaṃ mūlabere'pi saviśeṣataḥ(?)  |
pūrvācca dviguṇaibhogairnaivedyāntaiḥ sabhājanam  | | 16.82  | |
kṛtvā ca divasaṃ sarvaṃ niśāpūrvārdhameva ca  |
gītairnṛttairbahuvidhairdedānāṃ śrāvaṇena ca  | | 16.83  | |
itihāsapurāṇāni(nāṃ?) vācanena kṣapāṃ nayet  |
ardharātre sa saṃprāpte pūjayitvā sureśvaram  | | 16.84  | |
snānavediṃ samānīya gandhatailaṃ susaṃskṛtam  |
upākṛtaṃ śātakumbhadroṇīṣu dvijapuṃgava  | | 16.85  | |
śālitaṇḍulabhārāṇāṃ daśakaiḥ kalpite bhuvi  |
rāśau sunihitaṃ (te?) pātre hemakalṛpte niṣicya ca  | | 16.86  | |
puṇyāhaṃ vācayitvā ca vaiṣṇavaidvijapuṃgavaiḥ  |
saśaṅkhabherīsaṃnādairdevamūdhriṃ ca secayet  | | 16.87  | |
avaśiṣṭaṃ ca sarveṣāṃ vaiṣṇavānāṃ śirassvapi  |
gāyakānāṃ nartakānāṃ tāṇḍavānāṃ ca sarvaśaḥ  | | 16.88  | |
deśānta[ragatānāṃ ca] (rādagatānāṃ ?) veśyānāmapi sarvaśaḥ  |
hanūmadādibhaktaiśca saha māmandireṣvapi  | | 16.89  | |
nikṣipya hemadroṇīṣu niṣiccet paricārakaiḥ  |
ye dhārayanti tattailaṃ śirobhirmanujā bhuji  | | 16.90  | |
nīrogāḥ susamṛddhāśca sarve te yāvadāyuṣam  |
iha? bhogān parān bhuktvā yānti cānte paraṃ padam  | | 16.91  | |
māṣavacūrṇaiśca devasya kalyayitvāṅgamardanām  |
tailāpanayanaṃ kṛtvā gandhāmalakapūrvakaiḥ  | | 16.92  | |
strāpayitvā tadante ca yathāśāstraṃ prakalpite  |
ekaśītighaṭāmbho?bhirabhiṣicya ca tanmayaḥ  | | 16.93  | |
yaśāśebhamalaṃkṛtya kṛṣṇaṃ yādavapuṃgavam  |
balabhadreṇa sahitamathavā kevalaṃ dvija  | | 16.94  | |
kumbhamaṇḍalapūrvādi pūvanaṃ kārayed dvija  |
nivedya pāyasādīni sarvāṇyannottamāni ca  | | 16. 95  | |
navanītaṃ ca subahu hemapātre nivedya ca  |
śuṣṭhīpūrvaṃ (cūrṇaṃ?) śarkaraṇa militaṃ ca nivedayet  | | 16.96  | |
kṣīraṃ ghṛtaṃ ca suvrahu kadalyādiphalāni ca  |
jāmbavāni phalā[nyadya(?) [yattatsaṃpādyate naraiḥ  | | 16.97  | |
tatsarvaṃ devadevāya devakīsūnave dvija  |
nivedayedabhaktiyuktaḥ śraddhāpūtena cetasā  | | 16.98  | |
kṣīraṃ niveditaṃ tasmai tasmin kāle dvijottama  |
tāvatkālamanaśrantyaḥ snātāḥ śuddhāmbarāḥ striyaḥ  | | 16.99  | |
pibanti bhaktittasaṃyuktā vandhyoścattanayān bahūn  |
arogāṃśca surūpāṃśca viduṣo janayanti tāḥ  | | 16.100  | |
devaṃ ca yānamāropya śibikādiṃ svalaṃkṛtam  |
pradakṣiṇaṃ paribhrāmya grāmaṃ mandirameva ca  | | 16.101  | |
sarvamaṅlasaṃyuktaṃ tūryarāvādisaṃyutam  |
praveśayedabhagṛhaṃ yathāsthānaṃ niveśayet  | | 16.102  | |
tataḥ prabhṛti (ta ārabhya?) divasān trīnvā pañcātha sapta vā  |
utsavaṃ kāreyadvidvān yathāśraddhaṃ yathāvasu  | | 16.103  | |
tathā vasantasamaye madhumāse dvijottama  |
site pakṣe navamyāṃ ca tārake'ditidaivate  | | 16.104  | |
prādurbhūtaṃ ca madhyāhe muhūrte'bhijiti dvija  |
devyā ca sītayā sārdhaṃ lakṣmaṇenānujena ca  | | 16.105  | |
ārādhayejjagannāthaṃ sarvadeveśvareśvaram  |
rāmaṃ daśarathājjātaṃ kausalyāyāṃ mahāmate  | | 16.106  | |
rāvaṇasya vadhārthāya(rthaṃ tu?) rākṣaseśasya kāśayapa  |
saṃbhūtaṃ mānuṣe loke bāṇābāḍuṃ dhanurdharam  | | 16.107  | |
bāṇabāṇāsanadharaṃ rāghavaṃ lakṣmaṇāgrajam  |
pūrvoktena vidhānena tasmin kāle samarcayet  | | 16.108  | |
ityevaṃ tu bhidā pūrvādavaśiṣṭaṃ samaṃ dvija  |
tade(thai?)va lakṣmīsahitaṃ nṛsiṃhavapuṣaṃ harim  | | 16.109  | |
[hiraṇyākṣāsuragaṇaṃ dharaṇyā cāṅkasaṃsthayā] (?)  |
[sārdhamuddhṛtayā devyā (?)] yajetsarvārthasiddhaye  | | 16.110  | |
tapasyāśvayuje vāpi site lakṣabhīdharaṃ harim  |
yajeta vidhanā tena pūrvoktavidhinā dvija  | | 16.111  | |
kārtike māsi ca tathā pakṣe śukletare(?)dvija  |
dvādaśyāṃ devadevaṃśaṃ prabodhya puruṣottamam  | | 16.112  | |
arcayecaca yathā devaṃ kumbhādiṣu ca kāśayapa  |
kārtikyāṃ paurṇamāsyāṃ ca sarvadeveśvaraṃ harim  | | 16.113  | |
ārādhya jagatāṃ nāthaṃ saṃcoditavidhānataḥ  |
dīpikā darśayeccaiva pradoṣe śeṣaśāyine  | | 16.114  | |
gopureṣu ca sarveṣu prākāravalayeṣu ca  |
dvāradeśeṣu sarveṣu khātvā(?)stambhān viśeṣataḥ  | | 16.115  | |
maṇḍapeṣu ca sarveṣu dīpānāropayet prabhoḥ  |
kalpayitvā ca kūṭādimadh?ye madhye ca dīpikāḥ  | | 16.116  | |
gavyenājyena cāropya devadevāya darśayet  |
mūlaberasya purataḥ pātraṃ svarṇamayaṃ dvija  | | 16.117  | |
gavyājyāḍhakasaṃpūrṇaṃ [phala(?)]tantudaśāvṛtam  |
dhānyarāśau tu saṃs?thāpya tasmin dīpaṃ ca pūjayet  | | 16.118  | |
upūpādīni sarvāṇi bhakṣyāṇyapi nivedayet  |
māsarkṣeṣu ca sarveṣu viṣuvāyanayostathā  | | 16.119  | |
saṃkrāntiṣu ca sarveṣu(sarvāsu ?) dvādaśīṣu viśeṣataḥ  |
śravaṇe ca punarvasvoḥ phalgunīṣūttareṣu ca  | | 16.120  | |
viśeṣayajanaṃ kāryaṃ viṣṇortiṣṇoḥ suradviṣām  | |

iti pañcarātre mahopaniṣadi viśvāmitrasaṃhitāyāṃ
viśeṣadivasapūjā?[dhidhirnāma]ṣoḍaśo'dhyāyaḥ  | |

<17>
saptadaśo'dhyāyaḥ
kāśyapaḥ
śruto nityotsavavidhirmahotsavavidhermama  |
śuśrūṣā vartate brahman taṃ me vaktuṃ tvamarhasi  | | 17.1  | |
viśvāmitraḥ
mahotsavastridhā prokto nityo naimittikasatathā  |
kānyaśceti mayā proktaḥ proktaḥ prathamaṃ te dvijottama  | | 17.2  | |
śubhe ca tithinakṣatre viṣuvāyanayordvayoḥ  |
saṃkalpitāvabhṛthakaḥ sarvadaivatasevitaḥ  | | 17.3  | |
jñeyau naimittika iti nimitte sati kalpitaḥ  |
anāvṛṣṭau sudīrghāyāṃ sati durbhikṣasaṃbhave  | | 17.4  | |
patitāsu maholkāsu rātrāvindradhanu[rdiśi]  |
darśane vālamīnānāṃ śvetakākasya darśane  | | 17.5  | |
aśanīnāṃ nipāte ca digdāhe bhū?bhikampane  |
devatālayabhede ca patane caityabhūruhām  | | 17.6  | |
devatāpratimānāṃ ca kampane bhedane tathā  |
mahāvāte mahāvṛṣṭau grahayuddhavidhānake  | | 17.7  | |
evamādiṣu sarveṣu mahotpāteṣu suvrata  |
etaddoṣapraśāntyarthaṃ kriyate yo mahotsavaḥ  | | 17.8  | |
sa naimittikanāmā syādutsavo madhuvidviṣaḥ  |
yartkicitkāmamuddiśya kriyate yatka(yaḥ pha ?) larthimiḥ  | | 17.9  | |
sa kāmya iti vijñeyo yatheṣṭaphaladāyakaḥ  |
navāhaḥ saptadivasaḥ pañcāhastridināvaṣiḥ  | | 17.10  | |
yathāvibhavavistāraṃ prakalpyaḥ sa mahotsavaḥ  |
tadarthaṃ manasā pūrvamālocyaiva prakalpayet  | | 17.11  | |
ayane viṣuve vāpi māsarkṣeṣvathavā dvija  |
paurṇamāsyāmamāyāṃ vā rohiṣyāṃ śravaṇe tathā  | | 17.12  | |
pratiṣṭhādivase vāpi kalpayettīthavāsaram  |
grāme vā nagare vāpi pattane vā viśeṣataḥ  | | 17.13  | |
balipradānaṃ rathyāsu kārayejjanasaṃyujate  |
grāmādirahite dhāmri bahirnotsavamācaret  | | 17.14  | |
dhāmro'ntare vā kartavyaṃ sarveṣvāvaraṇeṣu ca  |
grāmādyaṅge svatantre vā tathā hmālayordvayoḥ  | | 17.15  | |
utsavo yaugapadyena na kāryo dvijapuṃgava  |
vāstvaṅgadevatānāṃ ca kalyāṇaṃ doṣavadabhavet  | | 17.16  | |
vartamāne'nyadevānamutsave vaiṣṇavaṃ sata(ha ?)m  |
kuryādvaiṣṇavakalyāṇe nānyatkāryāṃ (?) kadācana  | | 17.17  | |
sarvāsāṃ devatānāṃ hi vāsudevo'dhidaivatam  |
dhvajārohaṇapūrve tu vartamāne mahotsave  | | 17.18  | |
keśavasyādidevasya na kuryānmānuṣaṃ maham  |
adhimāse surācārye śukra cāstamite sati  | | 17.19  | |
notsavaṃ prathamaṃ kuryātkuryāccedrājarāṣṭrayoḥ  |
jāyate sumahatkle ?)śo maraṇe cāpepajāyate  | | 17.20  | |
dvitīyādau na doṣaḥ syādutsave prathamoditaḥ  |
tīrthāṅkurārpaṇadināt pūrvamekonarviśake  | | 17.21  | |
dine dhvajārohaṇaṃ syādaṣṭāviṃśa(daśa?)dine'thavā  |
dvādaśāhe'thavā kuryādvainateyadhvajocchrayam  | | 17.22  | |
dhvajārohaṇanakṣatrānnavame saptame'thavā  |
pañcame vā dine kuryādaṅkurāṇāṃ samarpaṇam  | | 17.23  | |
utsavoktadinātpūrvaṃ kutaścitkāraṇāntarāt  |
dhvajārohaṇavighraścedutpanno dvijapuṃgava  | | 17.24  | |
utsavārambhadivase kuryā?deva dhvajotsavam  |
rātrau saṃtāḍanaṃ kuryādabheryāstu śubhalagrake  | | 17.25  | |
maṅgalāṅguravāpaṃ ca pālikāsu vidhāya ca  |
bherītāḍanataḥ paścād dhvajamāropayettu vā  | | 17.26  | |
kārpāsaṃ vasanaṃ sūkṣmaṃ śuklaṃ ṭṭaḍhamathāharet  |
hastaiḥ ṣoḍaśabhirvāpi nyūnairvāpi samāyatam  | | 17.27  | |
daśahastāyataṃ tasmādardhavistārasaṃyutam  |
yadvā yathoditaṃ kuryādyathāvibhavavistaram  | | 17.28  | |
nirṇijyālipya ca khralīṃ saṃśoṣyāpi vimṛjya ca  |
tasya madhye likhet tārkṣyaṃ pītābhaṃ lakṣaṇānvitam  | | 17.29  | |
navatālasamutsedhaṃ dvihastaṃ ca sakañcukam  |
puṣpāñjalidharaṃ śvetavastravāsitavigraham  | | 17.30  | |
daṃṣṭrākarālavadanaṃ dadhānaṃ bhrukuṭīyuta(ga ?)m  |
jānuno'dhaḥ suvarṇābhamā nābhestuhinaprabham  | | 17.31  | |
kuṅkumāruṇamā marṇādā lalāṭāt sitetaram  |
dadhānaṃ nīlanāsāgraṃ vāmapādaṃ ca kuñcitam  | | 17.32  | |
ṣṭaṣṭhe niviṣṭaṃ bibhrāṇaṃ bṛhatpakṣavirājitam  |
ākāśodgramanodyuktaṃ vṛttaraudrākṣiyugmakam | | 17.33  | |
karaṇḍabhakuṭopetaṃ lalāṭālambitālakam  |
hārādisarvābharaṇabhūṣitaṃ sukhavigraham  | | 17.34  | |
yajñasūtrīkṛtaṃ śoṇaṃ bibhrāṇaṃ vāsukiṃ tathā  |
vāmadordaṇḍakaṭakamanāntaṃ bibhrataṃ sitam  | | 17.35  | |

adhobhāge'mbusaṃpūrṇakumbhaṃ sāṅkurapālikam  | | 17.39  | |
?B vilikhetpārśvayoścaiva dīpayugmaṃ samujjavalam  |

<18>
aṣṭādaśo'dhyāyaḥ  | |
viśvāmitraḥ
utsavārambhadivasāt prāgeva grāmavīthayaḥ  |
saṃśedhanīyāḥ sarvāḥ sruruddhṛtyāvakarānapi  | | 18.1  | |
sarveṣu catveraṣveva kalpyāstoraṇarājayaḥ  |
phalabhārānatāḥ kalpyāḥ kadalyaḥ kramukā api  | | 18.2  | |
vīthīmadhyeṣu sikatāḥ prasāryāḥ samatā yathā  |
dhvajaiśca darbhamālābhiḥ prabhā(pā ?)bhiḥ śobhayedapi  | | 18.3  | |
āpultya gomayāmbhobhiḥ kakubhaśca viśodhayet  |
bhittayo gehārājīnāṃ pañcavarṇavicitritāḥ  | | 18.4  | |
vedikāḥ pūrṇakumbhaiśca sāṅkuraiḥ pālikāgaṇaiḥ  |
sarvato dīpikābhiśca gandhatailāktavartibhiḥ  | | 18.5  | |
bhūṣitāḥ kamanīyāḥ syuḥ kṣaumaśobhitanīvikāḥ  |
utsave devadevasya sevākautukināṃ nṛṇām  | | 18.6  | |
deśāntarādāgatānāmannādyaṃ kalyedapi  |
gandhodakaiḥ susaṃpūrṇāḥkāryāḥ pānīyaśālikāḥ  | | 18.7  | |
samāgateṣu rogārtā bhaveyuryahi tatkṛte  |
sarvaiṣadhīnāṃ saṃbhārāḥ saṃkalpyāḥ syurṭṭaḍhaṃ dvija  | | 18.8  | |

devasya saviśeṣārcāṃ vidadhyāddeśikottamaḥ  |
divānaktaṃ ca sarvatra balidānaṃ samācaret  | | 18.128  | |
tīrthotsavātpūrvadine mṛgayotsavamācaret  |
tatpūrvadivase kāryaṃ jaladroṇyavagāhanam  | | | 18.129  | |
tatpūrvāvasare devaṃ devībhyāṃ saha viṣṭare  |
haime'dhiropya vīthīśca śobhayitvā viśeṣataḥ  | | 18.130  | |
paribhrāmya ca sarvatra nayedudyānavāṭikām  |
tatrāpi ca viśeṣārcāṃ kārayettadvicakṣaṇaḥ  | | 18.131  | |
gītanṛttādikāḥ sarvāḥ krīḍāḥ saṃdarśayettathā  |
evaṃ pratidi?naṃ kuryāt saviśeṣamahotsavam  | | 18.132  | |
āhṛtaṃ grābhṛtaṃ sarvaṃ darśayejjanasaṃsadi  |
devasyānugrahaṃ teṣāṃ svayamājñāpayettathā  | | 18.133  | |
ālokya cānanāmbhojaṃ devasya susamāhitaḥ  |
matvā ca devo'ha?miti prasādādi samācaret  | | 18.134  | |
evamaṣṭāsvatī?teṣu divaseṣu mahotsave  |
jāte ca tīrthanakṣatre navame'hani deśikam  | | 18.135  | |
āropya syandanavaraṃ bhūṣitaṃ sa(ca ?)viśeṣataḥ  |
grāmādivīthayaḥ sarvāḥ parikramya samantataḥ  | | 18.136  | |
praveśayedālaye taṃ maṇḍape cāvatārayet  |
bhadrāsane conaveśya vidhivacca sabhājayet  | | 18.137  | |
tīrthabimbamalaṃkṛtya navakṣaumayugena ca  |
kautukaṃ bandhayitvā ca yānaṃ haimamalaṃkṛtam  | | 18.138  | |
āropya cakrānugataṃ bhakterapi niṣevitam  |
parikramyotsavārcāṃ ca dhāma grāmaṃ pra?dakṣiṇam  | | 18.139  | |
nītvātha saritastīraṃ sarasastaṭameva vā  |
jaladherathavā tīre maṇḍape pūrvakārite  | | 18.140  | |
avatāryāsanavare saṃniveśya ca kautukam  |
cakrādisahitaṃ tatra pūjayitvā vidhānataḥ  | | 18.141  | |
tīrthabimbasya purataḥ kalaśān pañcaviṃśatiḥ  |
saṃsthāpya saptadaśa vā strapanoktavidhānataḥ  | | 18.142  | |
abhiṣiñcecca kalaśairdevaṃ śāstroktavartmanā  |
śiṣṭaiḥ sudarśanaṃ devamabhiṣiñcecca vāribhiḥ  | | 18.143  | |
abhāve saridādīnāṃ teṣāmāvāhanaṃ budhaḥ  |
kārayitvā tīrthabimbaṃ cakraṃ bhaktāṃśca deśikaḥ  | | 18.144  | |
nimajjayeccatuṣkṛtvā taṭamuttārya cāsane  |
niveśya cāpyalaṃkṛtya dhāma caiva praveśayet  | | 18.145  | |
tatkāle saha devena ye nimajjanti mānavāḥ  |
te'nubhūya śubhaṃ sarvaṃ sarvajanmasamārjitam  | | 18.146  | |
suduṣprāpaṃ paraṃ yānti padamācandratārakam  |
devaṃ maṇḍapamānīya sabhājya ca yathāvidhi  | | 18.147  | |
mahāhavirnivedyātha prātaḥ kāle samāhitaḥ  |
bhaktairjanairbahuvidhaiḥ sevite maṇḍapottame  | | 18.148  | |
vidhāya maṇḍapaṃ(laṃ ?) cakrapahmamāvaraṇānvitam  |
puṣpaistkālasaṃbhūtaiḥ kṛṣṇaiśca tulasīdalaiḥ  | | 18.149  | |
varṇaiḥ sitādibhiḥ savaiścitrayitvā ca madhyataḥ  |
siṃhapādāṅkite bhadrapīṭhe ca prabhayā yute  | | 18.150  | |
autsavaṃ beramādāya kramādardhyādibhiryajet  |
dviṣaṭkamanuvarṇāni dvādaśāni(?) gururdvijaḥ  | | 18.151  | |
pṛthakpṛthagudāhṛtya jedardhyādibhiḥ kramāt  |
samastairapi deveśaṃ yajetpūṣpairanantaram  | | 18.152  | |
aṅgāni ca hṛdādīni saṃprocya kusumairyajet  |
bhūṣāṇāni kramādiṣṭvā cakrādyānyāyudhānyapi  | | 18.153  | |
devānāvaraṇasthāṃśca samabhyarcya svanāmabhiḥ  |
matsyādīnarcayitvātha caturviśatimūrtayaḥ  | | 18.154  | |
pūjākrameṇa puṃsūktamuccārya pratyṛktaṃ tathā  |
nārāyaṇānuvākaṃ ca viṣṇusūktaṃ ca kāśyapa  | | 18.155  | |
devapādāmbujadvandve vikṣipetkumumāñjalim  |
pañcopaniṣadairmantraiḥ kṣipetkusumasaṃcayam  | | 18.156  | |
caturdikṣu caturvedapaṭhanaṃ kārayed dvija  |
koṇeṣvekāyanī śākhāṃ tanmayaiḥ pāṭhayedapi  | | 18.157  | |
gītairnṛttairbahuvidhaistoṣayecca prabhuṃ dvija  |
puṣpaudhaiḥ pañcavarṇāḍhyairdeveśamabhiṣecayet  | | 18.158  | |
annaṃ caturvidhaṃ caiva devāya vinivedayet  |
juhuyāt samidādyaiśca catubhirmūlavidyayā  | | 18.149  | |
pṛthakṛ pṛthak cāṣṭhaśataṃ deśiko mantravittamaḥ  |
devaṃ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya daṇḍavat praṇipatya ca  | | 18.160  | |
yāne ca devamāropya prathamāvaraṇasthitam  |
brahmādīnāṃ ca devānāṃ bhaktānāṃ cāpi cotsavam  | | 18.161  | |
prāptānāmanusaṃdhātuṃ gamanāyālayaṃ prati  |
anujñāṃ devadevena kārayitvā pradikṣaṇam  | | 18.162  | |
parītya sevakairnityaiḥ sārdhamālayamāvrajet  |
tadratryāḥ prathame yāme nītvā strapanaṇḍapam  | | 18.163  | |
kalaśaiḥ strāpayitvā taṃ nivedya ca mahāhāviḥ  |
āsādya yāgadānaṃ kṛtvā homaṃ yathāvidhi  | | 18.164  | |
śāntihomaṃ vidhāyātha prāyaścittārthamapyatha  |
sahastraṃ vā śataṃ vārān pañcopaniṣadā pṛthak  | | 18.165  | |
hutvā pūrṇāhutiṃ kṛtvā paścādagri visarjayet  |
toraṇādisthitān devān visṛjya tadanantaram  | | 18.166  | |
gatvā dhvajasamīpaṃ ca sabhājya patageśvaram  |
nivedya ca hivaḥ kṛtvā marutmantaṃ pra?dakṣiṇam  | | 18.167  | |
udvāsayet paṭasthaṃ taṃ khagaṃ saṃhṛtiyogataḥ  |
dhvajāvarohaṇaṃ kṛtvā madhyarātre ca pāṭhayet  | | 18.168  | |
daṇḍaṃ dhvajapaṭaṃ caiva svīkuryāddeśikottamaḥ  |
garuḍasyātimahati(tī ?) tṛptiḥ syād grahaṇe hi saḥ (tayoḥ ?)  | | 18.169  | |
pūrvamāvāhitān devān tattatsthānepuṃ saṃsthitān  |
saṃpradāya baliṃ tūryaṃ ghoṣayitvā pṛthak pṛthak  | | 18.170  | |
brahmaṇamapi codvāsya mūkaiḥ parijanaiḥ saha  |
aṣṭākṣarajapaṃ kuryāt guru ma?(rma ?)nvādikaṃ hareḥ(?)  | | 18.171  | |
vidhāya devaṃ yānsthāmautsavaṃ garbhamandiram  |
praveśya vijñāpya punaḥ praṇamya svagṛhaṃ yajet  | | 18.172  | |
prabhātāyāṃ tu śarvaryāṃ yajamānastu deśikam  |
vastrābharaṇagandhādyairalaṃkṛtsaya sabhājya ca  | | 18.173  | |
datvottamasvarṇaśataniṣkayuktāṃ tu dakṣiṇām  |
tadardhaṃ vā yathāśakti datvā vaca praṇabhedgrurum  | | 18.174  | |
ācāryāṇāṃ tathānyeṣāṃ daśaniṣkapramāṇataḥ  |
ṛtvijāṃ ca tadardhaṃ syādanyeṣāṃ ca tadardhakam  | | 18.175  | |
krameṇa dakṣiṇāṃ datvā gāyakādyān janānapi  |
svārṇāmbarādyaiḥ saṃtopya tattatkarmānurūpataḥ  | | 18.176  | |
draṣṭumutsavamāyātān janān bhāgavatānīti  |
pūjayedīptisatairdravyaistattatsaṃtoṣakāraṇāt  | | 18.177  | |
dakṣiṇā cāpi dātavyā sarvebhyo yāgakarmaṇi  |
[karmabhyo dakṣiṇāhīno](dakṣiṇāhīkarmabhyo?) yāgo niṣphala eva ca  | | 18.178  | |
pratyuta pratyavāyāya yajamānasya kalpate  |
vibhavānuguṇaṃ tasmāddātavyā dakṣiṇā budhaiḥ  | | 18.179  | |
kārayedutsavaṃ yastu devadevasya śārṅgiṇaḥ  |
śraddhayā dakṣiṇāyuktaṃ sāṅgopāṅga mahāmanāḥ  | | 18.180  | |
kulaṃ tārayate sarvaṃ yāvadābhapūtasaṃplavam  |
aśvamedhasahastreṇā yo yajeta samāhitaḥ  | | 18.181  | |
yajeta yo bhagavato yāgena na samaṃ phalam  |
atiricyeta bhagavādyagajaṃ phalamuttamam  | | 18.182  | |
puṣpayāgadinādanvagdivase tu yathāpuram  |
nityārcanaṃ vidhāyātha sāyāhne deśikottamaḥ  | | 18.183  | |
devaṃ bahirnirgamayya maṇḍape viṣṭharottame  |
muktāvitānasyādhastānniveśya ca sabhājya ca  | | 18.184  | |
viṣvaksenaṃ ca devasya purataḥ saṃniveśya ca  |
havirnivedanāntaṃ ca kārayeddevapūjanam  | | 18.185  | |
nivedyānte(niveditaṃ ?) dine tasmin yadyat pāyasapūrvakam  |
caturvidyaṃ vānnajātaṃ pṛthukāpūpasaktavaḥ  | | 18.186  | |
tāmbūlaṃ caitadakhilaṃ viṣvaksenāya dāpayet  |
tatsarvaṃ purato nyasya viṣvaksenasya deśikaḥ  | | 18.187  | |
tasmai nivedya sakalamagādhe'mbhasi nikṣipet  |
paścātseneśasahitaṃ devaṃ sthāne praveśayet  | | 18.188  | |
eṣa te bhagavadyagaḥ saṃkṣipto'bhihito mayā  |
avadhāryemamitthaṃ tvaṃ kṛtī syāḥ kaś?yapātmaja  | | 18.189  | |

iti pañcarātre mahopaniṣadi viśvāmitrasaṃhitāyām
itsavavidhirnāma aṣṭādaśo'dhyāyaḥ  | |

<19>
ekonaviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ
kāśyapaḥ
bahuśaḥ strapaṃ kāryamiti prāktiṃ tvayā guro  |
tasya prakāraṃ bhagavan saṃkṣipya tvaṃ pracakṣva me  | | 19.1  | |
viśvāmitraḥ
rūpanasya vidhiṃ brahman pravakṣyāmi samāhitaḥ  |
śrotumarhasi caitat tvaṃ sāvadhānena cetasā  | | 19.2  | |
viṣuvāyanayoryugme grahaṇe candrasūryayoḥ  |
vyatīṣāte ca rohiṇyāṃ punarvasvordvijottama  | | 19.3  | |
utpāteṣu ca ṭṭaṣṭeṣu durbhikṣe vyādhipīḍane  |
yajamānasya janmarkṣe śāntike pauṣṭike tathā  | | 19.4  | |
atsavādyantayoḥ snānaṃ strapanaṃ kārayed budhaḥ  |
strapanaṃ tripidhaṃ proktamuttamaṃ madhyamaṃ tathā  | | 19.5  | |
adhamaṃ ceti śāstrajñairyathāvibhavavistaram  |
samayānuguṇaṃ caiva triṣvekaṃ kārayed budhaḥ  | | 19.6  | |
pūrvasmin divase kuryādaṅkurāṇāṃ samarpaṇam  |
badhvā ca kautukaṃ paścādadhivāsanamācaret  | | 19.7  | |
utsavoktena vidhinā śayanādi prakalpayet  |
sadyo vā sakalaṃ kuryādbimbe strapanūrvake  | | 19.8  | |
alābhe pūrvabimbasya tairthikaṃ vādhivāsayet  |
autsavaṃ bimbamathavā balyarcāmathavā dvija  | | 19.9  | |
karmārcāmathavā tatra [na teṣvapi ca kūrcakam (?)]  |
kuryāt prāsādakasyāgre maṇḍapaṃ strapanārthakam  | | 19.10  | |
viṃśaddhastasamāyāmaṃ tadardhenaiva vistṛtam  |
dvāraṃ caturṣu dikṣu syāt pratidvāraṃ ca toraṇam  | | 19.11  | |
vinyaset kumbhayugalaṃ saṃpūrṇaṃ dvārapārśvayoḥ  |
prācyāṃ vā maṇḍapaṃ kuryādavakāśodaye sati  | | 19.12  | |
tridhā vibhakte tatrāpi paścirme'śe prakalpayet  |
hastotsedhāṃ caturhastāṃ vedikāṃ strānakāraṇāt  | | 19.13  | |
tanmadhye caturaśraṃ vā vṛttaṃ vā caturaṅgulam  |
samunnataṃ tritālena vistārāyāmayoḥ samam  | | 19.14  | |
aśvabhrabhūmikulyāṃ ca vedikopari kalpayet  |
berāntarāṃsaṃbhave tu ?bahirvediṃ na kalpayet  | | 19.15  | |
maṇḍapaṃ sarvatodiklaṃ(kṣa ?) kṣaumavastrairvitānayet  |
saṃveṣṭaya darbhamālabhilaimbayemau?ktikastrajaḥ  | | 19.16  | |
ālipya gomayajalaiścūrṇaiḥ saudhaiśca rekhayet  |
sāṅkurā pālikā nyasya sthāpayeccāpi dīpikāḥ  | | 19.17  | |
kaṭāhaṃ ca jaladroṇīṃ sthāpayedīśakoṇake  |
pūrayed‌grālitaiḥ svacchaiḥ salilaiśca sugagandhibhiḥ  | | 19. 18  | |

<20>
viṃśo'dhyāyaḥ
viśvāmitraḥ
kṛtvā vṛtrasya nidha(hana ?)naṃ devendro brahmahatyayā  |
abhibhūtaḥ paribhraṣṭaḥ padāt svasmāt saha śriyā  | | 20.1  | |
ārādhya jagatāmīśaṃ nārāyaṇamananyadhīḥ  |
sahastrakalaśastrānavidhinā svapadaṃ punaḥ  | | 20.2  | |
labghavām̐stadvijaśreṣṭha vidhānaṃ kathayāmyaham  |
anye'pi bahavo devaṃ samārādhya śriyaḥ patim  | | 20.3  | |
anena vidhinā kāmān durlabhāpapi lebhire  |
ekārdhikaiḥ sahastrestu kalaśairabhiṣecanam  | | 20.4  | |
saṃprīṇanaṃ bhagavataḥ sarvapāpānodanam  |
jayapradaṃ puṣṭikaraṃ putrasaṃpatpradāyakam  | | 20.5  | |
āyāsena catuḥpaṣṭihastayuktaṃ dvijottama  |
tadardhena tu vistīrṇaṃ maṇḍapaṃ kalpayenmune  | | 20.6  | |
dvāratoraṇakumbhādipraklṛptiḥ pūrvavad bhavet  |
pratiṣṭhoktaprakāreṇa maṅgalāṅkuraropaṇam  | | 20.7  | |
badhvā ca kautukaṃ śayyāṃ kṛtvā tatrādhivāsayet  |
kalaśāspadabhūbhāgaṃ pañcaviṃśatibhedataḥ  | | 20.8  | |
vibhajet sūtrapātena brahmasathāne tu madhyame  |
ekottarāśītighaṭasthānaṃ saṃkalpayettataḥ  | | 20.9  | |
tadbrāhyaye daivike bhāge dikṣvaṣṭasu pṛthak pṛthak  |
daśā saptottarān kumbhān sthāpayitvā vicakṣaṇaḥ  | | 20.10  | |
bāhme tu mānuṣe bhāge kṛte ṣoḍaśa vā punaḥ  |
ghaṭānekonapañcāśat pratibhāgaṃ niveśayet  | | 20.11  | |
vīthīḥ prakalpayedetadanukūlaṃ vicakṣaṇaḥ  |
madhyame brahmabhāge tu madhyame navake dvija  | | 20.12  | |
ghṛtoṣṇedakaratnāmbhaḥ phalalohāmbumārjanam  |
gandhākṣatayavāmbūni vinyasya tadanantaram  | | 20.13  | |
navakeṣvaṣṭasu prācyādīśānānteṣu ca kramāt  |
pādyārdhyācamanīrayāni pañcagavyaṃ caturdiśam  | | 20.14  | |
madhyakumbheṣu vinyasya koṇeṣu ca payo dadhi  |
kṣaudraṃ kaṣāyaṃ kramaśo madhyakumbheṣu vinyaset  | | 20.15  | |
śoṣān kumbhām̐ścatuḥ ṣaṣṭi śuddhāmbhobhiḥ prapūrayet  |
aindrādisaptadaśake catuṣke madhyame ghaṭe  | | 20.16  | |
malayodabhavakāśīrakarpūrośīrakardamam  |
koṇasthasaptadaśake catuṣke vinyaset kramāt  | | 20.17  | |
tilāmalakasiddhārthatailāni ghanasārajam  |
tainaṃ ca śeṣān kalaśānaṣṭāviṃśacchataṃ budhaḥ  | | 20.18  | |
pūrayecchuddhapayasā candanādyavidaivatam  |
puruṣaṃ satyamaupendraṃ naranārāyaṇāvapi  | | 20.19  | |
acyutaṃ cāniruddhaṃ ca harikṛṣṇau kramādyajet  |
mānuṣyaikonapañcāśadghaṭapuñceṣu madhyame  | | 20.20  | |
aindrādiṣu catuṣkasthaṃ gula(ḍa ?)mikṣurasaṃ tathā  |
nālikerajalaṃ śāntivāri kumbhe tu vinyaset  | | 20.21  | |
vāraho nārāsiṃhaśca śrīgharo hayaśīrṣakaḥ  |
gulā(ḍā ?)dīnāṃ kramādetānaghidevān samabhājayet  | | 20.22  | |
caturṣu koṇakumbheṣu maṅgalāmbhaḥ prapūrayet  |
tatraiva vāsudevādyā mūrtayasteṣu devatāḥ  | | 20.23  | |
indragrimadhyabhāgeṣu kumbhāsaṃgheṣu ca kramāt  |
himatoyaṃ tilāmbhaśca taṇḍulaṃ tilajaṃ jalam  | | 20.24  | |
vṛṣṭhitoyaṃ kuśāmbhāśca tulasyambhaḥ samudrajam  |
kramaśo vinyasenmadhyakumbheṣu dvijasattama  | | 20.25  | |
keśavaṃ māghavaṃ caiva govindaṃ viṣṇumeva ca  |
madhusūdanaṃ vānaṃ va pahmanābhamanantaram  | | 20.26  | |
dāmodaramimān devān kumbheṣvaṣṭasu pūjayet  |
śeṣān saptadaśa(śatā?)n ṣaṣṭikumbhānaṣṭādhikān dvija  | | 20.27  | |
gālitairgandhasaṃyuktaiḥ śuddhāmbhobhiḥ prapūrayet  |
brāhme madhyasthanavake śrīvatsaṃ vanamālayā(likām?)  | | 20.28  | |
śaṅgaṃ cakraṃ gadāṃ pahmaṃ khaṅgaṃ śārṅgaṃ śriyaṃ tathā  |
drarvyairhemādibhiḥ kṛtvā kramānnikṣipya teṣvapi  | | 20.29  | |
etāneva yajetteṣu kambheṣu dvijapuṃgava  |
aindrādimadhyamāntaṃ tu kumbhānāmuddhṛtibharpavet  | | 20.30  | |
sahastrakalaśasnāne deśiko vṛṣṇuyādbrahūn  |
caturo dviguṇān vāpi ṛtvijo dviguṇām̐stathā  | | 20.31  | |
puṇyāhavācanaṃ pūrvaṃ kṛtvānvagadhidevatāḥ  |
iṣṭavā pañcopaniṣadaiḥ sahastraṃ sarpiṣāhutīḥ  | | 20.32  | |
mūlamantreṇa śāntyarthaṃ sabhidādyairhune(?)ttataḥ  |
pūrvoktena krameṇaiva kalaśānadhivāsya ca  | | 20.33  | |

<21>
ekaviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ
kāśpaḥ
devālasya nirmāṇaṃ devasya ca vinirmita(ti ?)m  |
śrotumicchāmi bhagavan kiṃcit yaṃkṣipya [vakṣya] (cocya ?)tām  | | 21.1  | |
viśvāmitraḥ
devadhāmro vinirmāṇaprakāraṃ śṇu kāśyapa  |
yasya śravaṇamātreṇa nirmāṇaphalamaśrute  | | 21.2  | |
aśvamedhādibhiryajñairiṣṭvā yatphalamāpruyāt  |
devadhāmavinirmāṇasaṃlpena labheta tat  | | 21.3  | |
śileṣṭakākāṣṭhakṛtamuttamādhamamadhyamam  |
śilā vai cottamaṃ madhyamiṣṭakāracitaṃ dvija  | | 21.4  | |
adhamaṃ kāṣṭharacitaṃ kṣudraṃ mṛṇmayamucyate  |
yajamānānukūlarkṣe śubhe caivottarāyaṇe  | | 21.5  | |
śubhe yoge śubhe lagre bhūmibhāgasya saṃgraham  |?
vidhāya tatra deveśaṃ varāhatanumacyutam  | | 21.6  | |
praṇamya khātayedyāvajjalasyod‌gramanaṃ bhavet  |
śalyādīnapi coddhṛtya bahiḥ prakṣepayed dvija  | | 21.7  | |
śubhasaṃdarśane siddhiraśubhe śāntimācaret  |
saudarśanena mantreṇa hune(?)daṣṭasahastrakam  | | 21.8  | |
vāstudeśasthitān devān pūjayedavaṭe tataḥ  |
pāṣāṇavālukādyaistaṃ pūrayedavaṭaṃ tataḥ  | | 21.9  | |
dakṣiṇenonnataṃ kṛtvā nimramutarato'pi ca  |
taṃ deśaṃ gomayāmbhobhiḥ samīkṛtya viśodhya ca  | | 21.10  | |
juhuyānmūlamantreṇa sabhidājyacarūn pṛthak  |
aṣṭottaraśataṃ mantrī puṇḍarīkākṣamantrakam  | | 21.11  | |
japitvā vācayetpaścāt puṇyāhaṃ brāhmaṇottamaiḥ  |
sathāpatisthāpakayuto matimān gaṇakaiḥ saha  | | 21.12  | |
tanmadhye sthāpayecchuṅkumṛjuṃ granthivivarjitam  |
śaṅkudviguṇavistāraṃ tadbrahijarmaṇḍalaṃ likhet  | | 21.13  | |
prātaḥ sāyaṃ ca gacchāyā maṇḍalānte dvijottama  |
tatra bindudvayaṃ kṛtvā prākpratyak sūtrayugmakam  | | 21.14  | |
kṣiptvā bindvantare bhrāmyet kacchapānanapucchavat  |
prāgudagdimdvayaṃ jñātvā samīkṛtya ca tatpunaḥ  | | 21.15  | |
uttarārdhaparaṃ sūtraṃ dvihastaṃ bhrāmayettataḥ  |
trihastaṃ pañcahastaṃ vā saptahastamathāpi vā  | | 21.16  | |
adhikaṃ vā yatheṣṭaṃ tatsūtrāṇi sphālayet pṛthak  |
diksūtraṃ bhrāmayitvā ca lāñchayet koṇasūtrakam  | | 21.17  | |
koṇadiksaṃprasiddhyarthaṃ koṇasūtraṃ kṣipettataḥ  |
sūtrasya vṛttabhramaṇāt samatāṃ parikalpya ca  | | 21.18  | |
sūtradvayaṃ samālambya koṭiṃ niṇīyaṃ ca dvija  |
krameṇaiva diśaścāṣṭhau nirṇayecca pṛthak pūthak  | | 21.19  | |
takṣava(vā ?)stuvighijñena caturaśraṃ vidhāya ca  |
ninādayedaṣṭhaśaṅkūn nimittāni ca lakṣayet  | | 21.20  | |
nimitte tu śubhe kāryamaśubhe śāntimācaret  |
saudarśanena juhuyānmantreṇāṣṭhasahastrakam  | | 21.21  | |
vahvayādiyāmkoṇāntamaṣṭau devāḥ sthitāḥ pade  |
mārutādīśaparyantaṃ tathaivāṣṭau pade sthitāḥ  | | 21.22  | |
abādyāḥ pṛthviparyantāḥ sthitāḥ śiṣṭāḥ padeṣvapi  |
uktaiḥ saṃkhyaiḥ (?) samārādhya yathānyāyaṃ gururdvija  | | 21.23  | |
pūrayeccaiva taṃ deśaṃ bālukābhiryathā samam  |
ārabheta tataḥ prājña adhamaṃ [dāruṇā ca yam]  | | 21.24  | |
gṛhītasthānakaṃ prācyāṃ dakṣiṇe cottare'pi vā  |
yathaivābhimatasthānaṃ kuryāt kṛtrimamālayam  | | 21.25  | |
prākāraṃ paritaḥ kṛtvā kuryādagre ca maṇḍapam  |
haimaṃ vā rūpyamathavā tarthodumbarameva vā  | | 21.26  | |
hasvakāyadharaṃ devaṃ caturbāhudharaṃ harim  |
vighāya jalavāsādi kramāt kṛtvātha maṇḍapam  | | 21.27  | |
avivāsya tathā kṛtvā mūrtihomaṃ ca śāntikam  |
suprabhāte sulagre ca vimale ca tathāmbare  | | 21.28  | |
pradakṣiṇaṃ ghāma nītvā garbhagehaṃ praveśayet  |
ekahastasamāyāmavistāre tatsamocchrayam(ye ?)  | | 21.29  | |
vedimadhye ca saṃs?thāpya mūlamantreṇa deśikaḥ  |
niścalaṃ sthāpayitvā va tatra devaṃ sanātanam  | | 21.30  | |
ādityamaṇḍalāntaḥsthaṃ nārāṇamananyadhīḥ  |
āvāhma ca jitaṃ tena bimbamadhye niveśayet  | | 21.31  | |
saṃpūjya cathāśāstraṃ tato vijñāpayedidam  |
yāvaddhāmasamāptiḥsyāt tāvatkālaṃ surottama  | | 21.32  | |
alpagehe'tra vastavyamasmatprītyā janārdana  |
iti vijñāpya deveśaṃ daṇḍavat praṇipatya ca  | | 21.33  | |
prāpte caturthadivase strāpayitvottamena tu  |
utsavaṃ kārayitvātha tīrthayātrāṃ ca kārayet  | | 21.34  | |
yajamānānukūle tu śubhe'hani samāhitaḥ  |
iṣṭakā dvādaśāṣṭhau vā catastro vā śivāḥ śilāḥ  | | 21.35  | |
gṛhītvā śikha(takṣa ?)yitvā ca takṣakaiḥ śi(ta ?)kṣita(tā?)tataḥ  |
pañcagavyaiḥ kṣālayitvā maṇḍape cādhivāsayet  | | 21.36  | |
iṣṭhakānāṃ dakṣiṇataḥ kalaśān dhānyarāśiṣu  |
sthāpayitvā vidhānena digīśān pūjayedatha  | | 21.37  | |
kum?bhaṃ madhye ca saṃsthāpya prāguktavidhinā dvija  |
tanmadhye pūjayitvātha brahmaṇāṃ kamalāsanam  | | 21.38  | |
kṣaumaiḥ śuddhairnavaiḥ kumbhaiḥ(mbhaṃ ?) kalaśāniṣṭakā api  |
saṃveṣṭayottarato vahnimādhāya vidhinā dvija  | | 21.39  | |
samīkṛtya carūn hutvā pṛthagaṣṭottaraṃ śatam  |
deśiko mūlamantreṇa jagareṇa nayenniśām  | | 21.40  | |
udite vimale sūrye sumuhūrte samāgate  |
dvāradeśaṃ tu saṃkalpya dakṣiṇena samāhitaḥ  | | 21.41  | |
kumbhodakenābhiṣicya brāhmaṇānumateṣṭakāḥ  |
manunā lokapālānāṃ kalaśairaṣṭabhiḥ kramāt  | | 21.42  | |
prāsādamadhyago bhūtvā japedaṣṭākṣaraṃ manum  |
naranārāyaṇaṃ devaṃ śriyā sārdhaṃ jagadgurum  | | 21.43  | |
praṇāmaṃ daṇḍavatkṛtvā praṇavaṃ manumuccaran  |
dvāradakṣiṇabhāge tu syāpayet prathameṣṭakām  | | 21.44  | |
cahnikoṇaṃ samārabhya prādakṣiṇyena vinyaset  |
aindrāgri(nta?)miṣṭakāścāṣṭau rakṣārthaṃ mandirasya ca  | | 21.45  | |
japetsaudarśanaṃ mantramaṣṭottaraśataṃ sudhīḥ  |
aṣṭākṣaraṃ ca mantreśaṃ vārāhaṃ nārasiṃha?kam  | | 21.46  | |
japedaṣṭottaraśataṃ pṛthak pṛthagudāradhīḥ  |
prīṇayitvā ca vipragryān śilpisaṃghaṃ ca toṣayet  | | 21.47  | |
vāsobhirmūṣaṇaiścaiva sāghanairdhānyarāśibhiḥ  |
ācāryāya ca ṛtvigbhyaḥ kramāddatvā ca dakṣiṇām  | | 21.48  | |
bherīkāhalaśaṅkhādivādakānapi toṣayet  |
śvabhramadibhaśca saṃpūrya ratnairdhānyeśca deśikaḥ  | | 21.49  | |
gandhākṣataṃ ca nikṣipya praṇavaṃ mantramuccaran  |
parīkṣeta jalāvartaṃ śubhaṃ cejjāyate śubham  | | 21.50  | |
aśubhe juhuyādāmnau manunāṣṭākṣareṇa tu  |
pūrayitvāvaṭaṃ tacca vālukābhiḥ samantataḥ  | | 21.51  | |
tataḥ prabhṛti taṃ deśaṃ rakṣeccaikāgramānasaḥ  |
hastatrayaṃ samārabhyā navahastāvadhi dvica  | | 21.52  | |
hastairayugmaiḥ kuvīṃta kramādekatalaṃ dvija  |
kuryāttataśca dvitalaṃ yat trayodaśahastakam  | | 21.53  | |
hastairdaśādhikairvāpi pañcordhvairvāstubhūmikām  |

saptadhā vihite caiva vistārād dvāśāṅgulam  |

evamā dvādaśatalāgutsedhe vihite sati  |

catuṣpadaṃ garbhagehaṃ madhyaṃ syādbrāhmatastataḥ  |
bhittyarthaṃ dvādaśapadānyanyāni parikalnayet  | | 21.58  | |
prācyodīcyaiśca ṣaṭsūtraiḥ pañcaviṃśatisaṃkhyayā  |
?B padāni syurnavanadaṃ garbhagehāntaraṃ bhavet  | | 21.59  | |
padaiḥ ṣoḍaśābhiḥ kuryādibhattīḥ sarvāḥ samantataḥ  |
prāsādastrividhaḥ proktastvākṛtīnāṃ vibhedataḥ  | | 21.60  | |
nāgaraṃ caturaśraṃ syādāsthūpyantaṃ dvitottama  |
prāsādaṃ kaṇṭheśāntamaṣṭāśraṃ drāvilaṃ(ḍaṃ?) bhavet  | | 21.61  | |
vṛttamākaṇṭhadeśāntaṃ tadvesaramudāhṛtam  |
āyāme caturaśraṃ ca vyāyāme vartulaṃ tathā  | | 21.62  | |

kṛtvā tu dhāmno viṣkambhaṃ pañcabhāgavibhājitam  | | 21.63  | |
teṣu triṣu vibhāgeṣu saptadhā tribhireva vā  |
athavā navabhāgeṣu caturbhirvā prakalpayet  | | 21.64  | |

kṛtvā tridhaikabhāgena tadardhaikena vā punaḥ  | | 21.65  | |
pañcasvekena vā karyād dvāradeśatya vistṛtiḥ(tim?)  |
vistārād dviguṇaṃ kuryādutsedhaṃ bhūsurottama  | | 21.66  | |
prāsādeṣvevamevātha vihitaṃ syād dvijottama  |
mūtīnāṃ sthāpanaṃ vakṣye samāhitamanāḥ śṇu  | | 21.67  | |
ādye tale tūpariṣṭāt sthāpayecca caturdiśam  |
vārāhaṃ nārāsiṃhaṃ ca śrīdharaṃ hayavavatrakam  | | 21.68  | |
caturṣu koṇadeśeṣu garuḍaṃ tannibhānanam  |
āsīnaṃ vā sthitaṃ vāpi kuryāttattaddigānanam  | | 21.69  | |
athavā keṇadeśeṣu kalpayet siṃhameva vā  |
karṇakūṭeṣu śālāsu tathā dvārāntareṣvapi  | | 21.70  | |
kumudādīnupendrādīn sagaṇān ?parikalpayet  |
siddhavidyādharādīṃśca nṛtyato gāyato'pi vā  | | 21.71  | |
vādyavādanakārāṃśca paśyato'nyonyameva tān  |
kalpayedibhattibhāgeṣu śilpiśāstraviśāradaḥ  | | 21.72  | |
dvitīye tu tale brahman śṛṇu devān. pṛthak pṛthak  |
naraṃ nārāyaṇaṃ caiva hariṃ kṛṣṇaṃ yathākramam  | | 21.73  | |
kalpayeccaturdikṣu pūrvādyuttarapaścimam  |
anyeṣu cāvakāśeṣu keśavādīn saśaktikān  | | 21.74  | |

puruṣaṃ satyamanu ca tathācyutamanantaram  |

indrādīṃścaiva lokeśānādityān dvādaśāpi ca  |

pūrve vā vāmanaṃ kuryāddakṣiṇasyāṃ trivikramam  |
paścimāyāṃ jāmadagri(gnaya ?)suttarasyāmapi dvija  | | 21.79  | |
baladevaṃ vidadhyācca śeṣeṣvapi samantataḥ  |
vaibhavaṃ devasaṃghātaṃ pahmanābhādikaṃ dvija  | | 21.80  | |
pañcame'pi tale kuryāccaturdikṣvapi kāśyapa  |
sudarśanaṃ pāñcajanyaṃ gadāṃ pahmaṃ tathaiva ca  | | 21.81  | |
mūrdhabhiḥ svāyudhadharān kṛtāñjalipuṭān kramāt  |
avaśiṣṭeṣu deśeṣu kṛṣṇasyāpi viceṣṭitam  | | 21.82  | |
saptame'pi tale kuryāccaturdikṣu yathākramam  |
vāsudevādikān devām̐ścaturo'pi mahāmune  | | 21.83  | |
karṇakūṭeṣu dharmādīn pañjareṣu yathākramam  |
[mahām̐śca](?) caturo vedān kṛtatretādikānapi  | | 21.84  | |
divyāyudhāni kurvīta [hārāṇā(?)]mantareṣu ca  |

siṃhāneva tathā kuryādvīrasenaṃ suṣeṇakam  |
vīranāthaṃ subhadraṃ ca bhujaiḥ prāsādadhāriṇau  | | 21.86  | |
kuryāccaturdiśaṃ vipra caṇḍādīn dvārapārśvataḥ  |
śaṅkhapahmanidhīn(dhī ?) kuryādevaṃ prāsādevatāḥ  | | 21.87  | |
kārayed dvijavaryāsya prāsādasya caturdiśam  |
saṃbhavetyuryadi purā prathamāditaleṣvapi  | | 21.88  | |
tṛtīyāditaleṣūktadevatā eva kalpayet  |
gopureṣu kṛteṣvevaṃ devatānyāsamācaret  | | 21.89  | |
[prate](?)rūrdhavapradeśe tu bhūtamālāṃ vidhāpayet  |

kārayet strapanārthaṃ ca tadbrahirmaṇṭapaṃ dvija  |
pānīyaśalāṃ kuvīṃta viśālāṃ śītakāriṇīm  | | 21.93  | |
vāruṇe vāhanasthānaṃ mārute vāsumandiram  |
śayanasyottare sthānamīśe kusumamaṇḍapam  | | 21.94  | |
tṛtīyāvaraṇe kuryādagreye nṛttamaṇḍapam  |
yāmye gānālayaṃ kuryāt sabhāṃ niṛrtikoṇake  | | 21.95  | |

evaṃ vidadhyātprāsādaṃ garbhanyāsamatho śṛṇu  | | 21.98  | |

aṣṭāṅgulaṃ vā kuvīṃta yatheṣṭaṃ dvijapuṃgava  |

athavā gartanavakaṃ vidadhīta vicakṣaṇaḥ  |

śālibhirmaṇḍalaṃ kuryādaṣṭabhārapramāṇakaiḥ  |
tadardhataṇḍulayutaṃ vastrairācchādya cāpi tat  | | 21.103  | |

pañcāyudhaṃ svarṇamayaṃ kumbhamamye ca vinyaset  | | 21.104  | |
arcayed‌grandhapuṣpādyairbrahmabījena deśikaḥ  |
paritastasya kalaśān nidhāyāṣṭau samabhājayet  | | 21.105  | |
indrādīśānaparyantaṃ kalaśeṣvaṣṭakeṣu ca  |

adrermṛdādi kharṇaṃ ca nyasetpātrāvaṭeṣvatha  | | 21.108  | |

aṣṭākṣaraṃ japan mantraṃ prasṛtena spṛśecca tat  | | 21.109  | |
daiśikaḥ śīlasahito janān sarvān vilambaya ca  |

tṛtīyabhāge vinyatya viśeṣeṇorumūlake  | | 21.111  | |
paṣṭikāyāṃ tu devānāṃ garbhanyāsaḥ praraśasyate  |
dvijānāṃ paṭṭikādhastānnṛpāṇāṃ kumudopari  | | 21.112  | |
vaiśyānāṃ kumudādhastātturyāṇāṃ [jagatopari] (?)  |
gāyatryā prokṣaṇaṃ kṛtvā vāruṇe homamācaret  | | 21.113  | |
svanāmrā keśavādīṃśca ghṛtena juhuyāttataḥ  |
indrādīn brahmaparyantān pṛthagaśṭāhutīrhunet (?)  | | 21.114  | |
hutvā vyāhṛtibhiścānte śāstracoditavartmanā  |
vaiṣṇavaṃ śilpaśastrajñaṃ kriyāsu kuśalaṃ guruḥ  | | 21.115  | |
evaṃguṇasamāyuktaṃ [śṛṇuyād]dvijapuṃgava (?)  |
saṃpātājyena siktvātha mūlavidyāṃ japan sudhīḥ  | | 21.116  | |
apidhāya ca tatpātraṃ praṇavena samāhitaḥ  |
dakṣiṇāṅguṣṭhaśirasā spṛśannaṣṭākṣaraṃ japet  | | 21.117  | |
prāṅmukhaḥ secane tatra smaran bhūbhiṃ kṛtāñjaliḥ  |

sarvabhūtaghare kānte parvastanamaṇḍite  |
samudraparidhānīye devi garbhaṃ samāśraya  | | 21.119  | |
anena vidhinā rātrau garbhanyāsaṃ samācaret  |
mūrdheṣṭakāṃ vidhāyātha śilpaśāstroktavartmanā  | | 21.120  | |
sthūpikāṃ saṃniveśyātha sthāpayitvā śikhāghaṭam  |
varṇakairlepayitvā ca prāsādaṃ cāpi sarvataḥ  | | 21.121  | |
pūjayitvā guruṃ pūrvaṃ pūjayecchilpinaṃ budhaḥ  |
prāsādaṃ yo vidhānena vāsudevasya kārayet  | | 21.122  | |
sarva kulaṃ samuddhṛtya viṣṇuloke supūjyate  | |

iti pañcarātre mahopaniṣadi viśvāmitrasaṃhitāyāṃ
prāsādanirmāṇaṃ(ṇavidhiḥ?)nāma ekaviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ  | |

<22>
dvāviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ
viśvāmitraḥ
bimbasya lakṣaṇaṃ vakṣye dravyasaṃgrahasaṃyutam  |
maṇijaṃ lohajaṃ śailaṃ trividhaṃ bimbamuttamam  | | 22.1  | |
vajraṃ ca pahmarāgaṃ ca [vālavāyo](?)dabhavaṃ tathā  |
tāmrajaṃ paittalaṃ caiva lohānyetāni suvrata  | | 22.2  | |
śilā caturvighā jñeyā sarṇayogānmahāmate  |
vāruṇī ca tathāgreyī māhendrī vāyavīti ca  | | 22.3  | |
tāsu vai dvividhā grahmā māhendrī vāruṇī tathā  |
āgreyyākhyā śilā caiva vāyavyā[khya]śilā tathā  | | 22.4  | |
varjyā syād dvividhā brahman garbhādīnapi varjayet  |
ṭṭaṣṭvātha śailaṃ vidhivadadhivāsanamācaret  | | 22.5  | |
śailasya paścime bhāge gomayena vilepayet  |
purastasya śailasya ṣaṅbhāraiḥ sthaṇḍilaṃ dvija  | | 22.6  | |
kārayitvā ca tanmadhye nyasya kumbhaṃ salakṣaṇam  |
kalaśānaṣṭa parito dikṣvaṣṭāsu praklapya ca  | | 22.7  | |
yathāpuraṃ kumbhamadhye hemaratrādikaṃ nyaset  |
kṣaumābhyāṃ veṣṭayitvā ca tatra brahmaṇamarcayet  | | 22.8  | |
kalaśeṣu tathendrādīnarcayitvacā vicakṣaṇaḥ  |
krūrabhūtapraśāntyarthaṃ sarvadikṣu baliṃ haret  | | 22.9  | |
caturdiśaṃ vidadhyācca homamaṣṭaśatāhutīḥ  |
juhuyānmūlamantreṇa samidājyacarūnapi  | | 22.10  | |
kārayitvā ca puṇyāhavācanaṃ dvijapuṃgavaiḥ  |
paścādavahito bhūtvā mantrametamudīrayet  | | 22.11  | |
o namaḥ sarvalokāya viṣṇave prabhaviṣṇave  |
viśvāya viśvarūpāya svaprādhipataye namaḥ  | | 22.12  | |
utsārya ca janān sarvān mantraṃ codīrayedimam  |
yakṣarakṣaḥpiśācāśca ye'tra tiṣṭhanti nityaśaḥ  | | 22.13  | |
te sarve'pyapagacchantu saṃnidhattāṃ sadā hariḥ  |
abhidhāyottamākāraṃ svamraṃ paśyettathā niśi  | | 22.14  | |
yajamānena sahito deśiko munipuṃgava  |
yadi paśyecchubhaṃ svagraṃ śilāṃ gṛhṇīta susvarām  | | 22.15  | |
varjayedaśubhe ṭṭaṣṭe prabhāte cotthito dvija  |
aṭṭaṣṭe'pi ca gṛhṇīyāditi śāstrasya niśvayaḥ  | | 22.16  | |
ācāryaḥ śilpibhiḥ sārdhaṃ gatvā bhūbhiṃ vilokayet  |
aindrādyagrā śilā grāhmā tathordhvāgrāpi cottamā  | | 22.17  | |
koṇadikchiraso varjyā grāhyā lakṣaṇasaṃyutāḥ  |
utpāṭya śailaṃ matimān kumbhaiḥ samabhiṣicya ca  | | 22.18  | |
kalaśaiśca tataḥ strāpya vastrairāveṣṭya tatra ca  |
abhyarcya mūlamantreṇa gandhapuṣpādibhiḥ kramāt  | | 22.19  | |
gṛhītvā lakṣaṇopetāṃ śilāṃ homaṃ prakalpayet  |
puṃstrīnapuṃsakākhyā ca śilā ca trividhā matā  | | 22.20  | |
puṃliṅgena tathā beraṃ striyā vai pīṭhikā bhavet  |
ityevaṃ bimbaklṛptiḥ syādyatnairapi ca kārayet  | | 22.21  | |
pratimāyāḥ pravakṣyāmi pramāṇaṃ saviśeṣataḥ  |
gehaṃ ca bimbānuguṇaṃ vidadhyānmantravittamaḥ  | | 22.22  | |
yajamānurūpaṃ syāt pramāṇaṃ tasya kāśyapa  |
caturaṅgulamārabhya caturaṅgulavṛddhitaḥ  | | 22.23  | |
yāvadaṣṭottaraśamaṅgulīnāṃ bhaved dvija  |
bimbaṃ sauvarṇakaṃ kāryamata ūrdhvaṃ na kārayet  | | 22.24  | |
rūpyatāmraśilābimbaṃ yattaddhāmāśrayaṃ bhavet (?)  |
garbhagehapramāṇā yā pratimā sottamā bhavet  | | 22.25  | |
navabhāgādekahīnā madhyamā pratimā dvija  |
pañcabhāgādekahīnā adhamā viddhi kāśyapa  | | 22.26  | |
garbhavistāramāne tu sarvadā(ptadhā?) vijitendriyaḥ  |
gṛhītvā pañcabhāge(gaṃ?)tu pañcabhāgagāt tribhāgakam  | | 22.27  | |
tribhāgebhyo dvibhāgaṃ vā śreṣṭhaṃ madhyādhamaṃ viduḥ  |
dvārapramāṇamuditaṃ pādardhādhikameva vā  | | 22.28  | |
uttamādhamamadhyānāṃ pramāṇamiti kīrtitam  |
śuddhadvārasamānaṃ vā pādādhikamathāpi vā  | | 22.29  | |
adhyardhadvāradeśaṃ syāduttamādhamamadhyam  |
śuddhadvārapramāṇe tu dvuyaṣṭabhāgavibhājite  | | 22.30  | |
uttamaṃ tvekabhāgaṃ syād dvibhāgaṃ madhyamaṃ bhavet  |
athavā stambhamānena kautukaṃ kārayed budhaḥ  | | 22.31  | |
stambhādadhyardhamānaṃ vā bimbaṃ kuryānmahāmate  |
sthānakasyocchuyaṃ viddhi dvāramāsana(nasa?)mānakam  | | 22.32  | |
navabhāgaikahīnaṃ vaca pañcabhāgaikahīnakam  |
uttamādhamamadhyaṃ syāt sthānakocchrāyamityapi  | | 22.33  | |
āsanasya dvijaśreṣṭha viddhi mānaṃ mahāmate  |
dvārārdhakadhikaṃ pādādadhikaṃ vā tathāpi ca  | | 22.34  | |
nibodha śayanaṃ vipra dvārād dviguṇeme va ca  |
adhyardhaṃ vāpi kuvītaṃ nyūnādhikyaṃ na kārayet  | | 22.35  | |
yathākāmaṃ prakuvīṃta sthānayānāsanādikam  |
mūlaberasyānuguṇaṃ karmārcā vidadhīta ca  | | 22.36  | |
dvādaśāṅgulamānaṃ vā nyūnaṃ vā caturaṅgulaiḥ  |
karmaberaṃ vidadhyācca baliberaṃ tathaiva ca  | | 22.37  | |
tatpramāṇaṃ vidadhyācca yātrāyā dvijasattama  |
kṛtrimālayabimbaṃ syādaṣṭāṅṛgulamiti dvija  | | 22.38  | |
yāgaberacaturbhāgaṃ tribhāgaṃ vāpi kārayet  |
kalpayet strānabimbaṃ ca dvitālocchrāyasaṃyutam  | | 22.39  | |
śaṅkhacakragadāpahmacaturlakṣalakṣitam  |
yathālakṣaṇasaṃyuktaṃ pīṭhikopari vinyaset  | | 22.40  | |
mūtīṃnāmeva sarvāsāṃ karmārcā syāccaturbhut  |
bimbaṃ suśobhanaṃ caiva prasannaṃ pūjyameva ca  | | 22.41  | |
dhruvaberasamucchrāyaṃ tridhā bhaktyāthavā dvija  |
caturdhā vā muniśreṣṭha teṣvekocchrāya(yi?)kā bhavet  | | 22.42  | |
kalyāṇakautukasyaivamutsavārcāpramāṇataḥ  |
tṛtīyāṃśena vā kuryāccaturthāśena vā punaḥ  | | 22.43  | |
karmārcā bahuberasya kramo'yaṃ parikīrtitaḥ  |
ekabere tadardhā tu tatturyāṃśādhikāpi vā  | | 22.44  | |
utsavārcā tṛtīyāṃśā(śa?)samucchrāyā tridhā bhavet  |
śayanāsanasthānavidhau dhruvārcā vihitā yathā  | | 22.45  | |
tathāvidhāni kāryāṇi karmārcāprabhṛtīnyapi  |
śayāne dhruvabere tu sthitaṃ vāsīnameva vā  | | 22.46  | |
karmādikautukaṃ kuryātrityakalyāṇakautukam  |
sarvatraiva sthitaṃ kuryāt pīṭhaklṛptiṃ mune śṛṇu  | | 22.47  | |
bimbāṃyāme tridhā klṛpte kāryamekena pīṭhakam  |
upānahādibhiryuktaṃ pañcāṃśairvartulaṃ śubham  | | 22.48  | |
visatāratulitāyāmaṃ caturaśraṃ manoharam  |
tṛtīyāṃśe susaṃklṛptaprabhāśaṅkudvayaṃ dvija  | | 22.49  | |
pīṭhocchrāyaṃ tridhā kṛtvā kuryādekena pahkajam  |
yuktaṃ dalairdvādaśabhiraṣṭhābhirvā yathecchayā(kam ?)  | | 22.50  | |
taducchrāyadalaṃ kuryānmadhyame śubhakarṇikam  |
upacchadayutaṃ caiva phullapaṅkajasaṃnibham  | | 22.51  | |
aṅghrimānaṃ dalopetaṃ kevalaṃ vātha paṅkajam  |
pīṭhaṃ vā pahmamathavā [suṣi]yuktaṃ(?) tu kārayet  | | 22.52  | |
sthitārcāpīṭhasaṃkalṛptiruditeyaṃ dvijottama  |
caturaśrāyataṃ vāpi syādvṛttāyamathāpi vā  | | 22.53  | |
pratimāṃśocchrayaṃ vāpi tattṛtīyāṃśameva vā  |
āsīnasya bhavetpahmaṃ dalairbahubhinvitam  | | 22.54  | |
manojñayā [tayā](?)yuktaṃ vipulaṃ vāthavā dvija  |
prasavyāṅdhriṃ tu saṃkucya lambayitvetaraṃ punaḥ  | | 22.55  | |
yathoktamāsanaṃ devaṃ samadhyāsitasusthitam  |
ādhāralambitasyāgre kalpayet paṅkajaṃ dvija  | | 22.56  | |
devasya dikṣaṇe pārśve lakṣmīṃ vāme vasundharām  |
āsīne kalpayeddevyāvāsīnasya dvijottama  | | 22.57  | |
vimbaṣaṭkasya karmādeḥ śrībhūmyau pārśvage sadā  |
ekāsanasthitasyāgre(?)devena pṛthageva vā  | | 22.58  | |
lakṣmīṃ sthitāṃ vā saṃkalpya vāmāṅdhriṃ lambayeta vā  |
lakṣmīṃ sthitāṃ vā saṃkalpya vāmāṅdhri lambayeta vā  |
athāsīnāṃ tathā pṛthvīṃ tisthatasyobhe abhisthite  | | 22.59  | |
bimbānusārato vṛttā prabhāṃ vṛttāyatāṃ tu vā  |
manoharāṃ vidadhyācca tejorāśibhiranvitām  | | 22.60  | |
pratimāmānamadhunā śuṇu kāśpa suvrata  |
ekāṅgulaṃ syāduṣṇīṣaṃ keśāntaṃ syāt triyaṅgulam  | | 22.61  | |
tataḥ prabhṛti ṣaṭtriṃśadyavamānaṃ bhaved dvija  |
ṭṭaksūtrāntaṃ tu tadvatsyānnāsāpuṭayugāntimam  | | 22.62  | |
cubukāntaṃ ca tadvat syāt keśāntādi dvijottama  |
cubukāntaṃ vaktramānamaṣṭottaraśataṃ yavam  | | 22.63  | |
ādyāgulaṃ syāt tristhānamūrumānaṃ dvijottama  |
ṣaṅviṃśatyaṅgulocchrāyatadvajjaṅdyo bhavet  | | 22.64  | |
avaśiṣṭāṃśamānaṃ syāttattaduktamatha śṛṇu  |
daśatāle'dhame vaktraṃ mataṃ śatayavaṃ dvija  | | 22.65  | |
tadvadeva bhavet sthānatrayamūrū śatadvayam  |
yavānāṃ kathitaṃ jaṅdyā tāvaducchrāyasaṃyutā  | | 22.66  | |
galaṃ(lo ?) jānu ca gulkaṃ ca pratyekaṃ catuṅgulam  |
ucchrāyamānaṃ bimbasya kathitaṃ dvijapuṃgava  | | 22.67  | |
anirvā(?)hatvāt sūkṣmaṃ te mayā cābhihitaṃ dvija  | |

iti pañjarātre mahokapaniṣadi viśvāmitrasaṃhitāyāṃ
bimbamānaṃ(navidhiḥ?)nāma dvāviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ | |

<23>
trayoviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ
kāśyapaḥ
prāsādanāṃ ca vimbānāṃ jīrṇānāṃ gādhinandana  |
uddhāraṃ bhagavan mahmaṃ vaktumarhasi sāṃpratam  | | 23.1  | |
viśvāmitraḥ
[svayābhuvaṃ ca](vīnāṃ ?) divyānāmārṣīṇāṃ caiva kāśyapa  |
mānuṣīṇāṃ bhagavataḥ pratimānāṃ dvijottama  | | 23.2  | |
maṇijānāṃ ca bimbānāṃ hāniraṅgeṣu ced dvija  |
yadyadaṅgaṃ vihīnaṃ syāttaddhemrā ca mahāmate  | | 23.3  | |
samādhātavyameva syānna tyājyaṃ dvijapuṃgava  |
śailajānāmapi tathā samādhānaṃ vidhīyate  | | 23.4  | |
samādhāne'ṅgātrasya sṛṣṭau sākalyato'pi vā  |
bimbamadhyasthitāṃ śāktiṃ yathāpūrvaṃ vidhāya ca  | | 23.5  | |
adhivāsādikarmādi pratiśṭhāvat samācaret  |
bimbāni saṃsthitānyeva śakyante yadi kāśyapa  | | 23.6  | |
samādhānaṃ tataḥ kuryānna tyajet sarvathā dvija  |
kautukaṃ lohajaṃ jīrṇaṃ drāvayitvā punaḥ sṛjet  | | 23.7  | |
punaḥsṛṣṭau tu jātāyāṃ jīrṇaveṣasya kāśpa  |
prācīnā yadi vidyante devayastābhiśca taṃ prabhum  | | 23.8  | |
[saṃyojane](yet ?) punarnānyā devyaḥ kāryā dvijottama  |
avicchedo yadi bhavet tāsāṃ pūjanakarmaṇi  | | 23.9  | |
udvāhaṃ na punaḥ kuryāditi śāstravidāṃ matam  |
vaikalyaṃ yadi saṃjātaṃ prabhāpahmāsanādiṣu  | | 23.10  | |
[hetu](ti ?)ṣvapi na yaṣṭavyamasamādhāya tatpunaḥ  |
ghaṭe vā mūlabere vā śaktiṃ tadvimbasaṃsthitām  | | 23.11  | |
udvāsya kārayetteṣāṃ samādhānaṃ dvijottama  |
saṃprokṣya ca vidhātavyaṃ pūjanaṃ syādyathāpuram  | | 23.12  | |
śailajaṃ vastujaṃ bimbaṃ jīrṇaṃ yadi bhavenmune  |
bālājayaṃ kalpayitvā śaktiṃ vinyasya tatra ca  | | 23.13  | |
ārādhayecca tattyaktaṃ khaneddhūmau jale'thavā  |
nikṣiped dvijavaryāgre prāsāde ca vidhiṃ śṛṇu  | | 23.14  | |
śaithilye yadi(sati ?) saṃjāte prāsādasya māhāmate  |
bālālaye sthāpayitvā devaṃ saparivārakam  | | 23.15  | |
tatraiva pūjayedyāvaddhāmasaṃpādanaṃ bhavet  |
dhāmri jīrṇe na jīrṇe ca dhruve taddhāmasaṃsthitam  | | 23.16  | |
nānyatrodvāsayejjīrṇaṃ samādadhyāt punardvita  |
dhāmāderaṅgahānau ca na jātāyāṃ na kālpayet  | | 23.17  | |
anīrṇe ca panuḥ sthairyabuddhyā tat kalpayedyadi  |
yajamānasya tasyāśu sānvayasya ca nāśanam  | | 23.18  | |
purā pramādāducchrāyaṃ prāsādādi kṛtaṃ yadi(?)  |
punarlakṣaṇasaṃyuktaṃ kalpayed dvijapuṃgava  | | 23.19  | |
punaḥprakalpanavidhau bahuberaikaberayoḥ  |
yathāpuraṃ kalpayeta vyatyāsaṃ naiva kalpayet  | | 23.20  | |
pūrvaṃ devīvinābhūtaṃ bhūyaḥkalṛptau kṛtaṃ yadi  |
bahuberaṃ tathā tasya devībhyo saha vā vinā  | | 23.21  | |
yathābuddhi vidhātavyaṃ yajamānasya suvrata  |
prāsādaṃ(do?) bimbamathavā svayaṃvyaktamamānuṣam  | | 23.22  | |
pūrvaṃ lakṣaṇahīnaṃ ca(cet?)jīrṇeddhāre'pi taṃ(tat?)tathā  |
anyathā naiva kartavyaṃ kṛte doṣo mahān bhavet  | | 23.23  | |
sthitayānaśayāneṣu bimbeṣu ca yathāpuram  |
amānuṣeṣu kartavyamanaghairevameva hi  | | 23.24  | |
mānuṣeṣu yathecchātaḥ punaḥsṛṣṭau na doṣ?ābhāk  |
yadvastu yacca yanmānaṃ yāṭṭaśaṃ devanirmitam  | | 23.25  | |
vimānaṃ tāṭṭagevātha jīrṇoddhāre'pi nānyatha  |
mohāt prakalpayetta[cca a](cceda?)nyathā rājarāṣṭrayoḥ  | | 23.26  | |
doṣāya jāyate vipra yathāpūrvaṃ prakalpayet  |
aiṣṭakaṃ mṛṇmayaṃ vā cenmānuṣaṃ vārṣameva vā  | | 23.27  | |
dhāma jīrṇaṃ sadi bhavet punastacchilayāpi vā  |
athaveṣṭakayā kuryāt tatra doṣo na vidyate  | | 23.28  | |
rakṣārthamupapīṭhādi kalpayan vardhayedbrahiḥ  |
tatrasthān parivārāṃśca sthāpayet kalpayetpunaḥ  | | 23.29  | |
tatra doṣaśca na bhavettatsaṃrakṣaṇārthinaḥ  |
jīrṇaṃ bhavedyadi punargopuraṃ pīṭhameva vā  | | 23.30  | |
nadyādīnāṃ jalarayairnaṣṭaṃ vā calitaṃ tu vā  |
dhāma bimbādi tatsthānāt punaranyatra (ttu?) kalpayet  | | 23.31  | |
yena mantreṇa yā mūrtiryāṭṭaśenādhikāriṇā  |
kalpitā tāṭṭaśīmeva mūtīnāṃ(rtiṃ tu ?)parikalpayet  | | 23.32  | |
mūrtistantraṃ ca mantraṃ ca tāṭṭaśaṃ caiva deśikaḥ  |
jīrṇoddhāre pūrvavidhau phalaṃ tulyaṃ dvayorbhavet  | | 23.33  | |
vyatyaye kalpyamāne tu [mūrtinātrā(tīnāma?) dhikāriṇā]  |
kṣayo bhavedatimāhan rājarāṣṭranṛṇāmani  | | 23.34  | |
jīrṇoddhāre kalpyamāne bahubere pratiṣṭhite  |
strāpayet karmabimbaṃ ca vedikāyāṃ ca śāyet  | | 23.35  | |
na kāryo jalasaṃvāso nayanonmīlanaṃ vinā  |
saṃhārasṛṣṭī ca vinā hmanyatsarvaṃ samācaret  | | 23.36  | |
āsādya dhruvabereṇa sarvaṃ vipra prakalpayet  |
bālālayaṃ vidhāyātha tadrātryāṃ prārthya sādaram  | | 23.37  | |
āvahma tadgratāṃ śaktiṃ kumbhe taddhānyamastake  |
sthāpayitvā samabhyarcya prāpte kāle ca deśikaḥ  | | 23.38  | |
niveṣya mūlabere tāṃ śaktiṃ śāstroktavartmanā  |
[kumbhapātau](?) viśeṣeṇa parivārān prakalpayet  | | 23.39  | |
tadante cotsarva kuryāt sa hi śāntividhiḥ smṛtaḥ  |
prāsāde pratimāyāṃ ca prabhāpīṭhādike'pi ca  | | 23.40  | |
aṅgabhaṅgādidoṣāṇaṃ kṛte saṃdhānakarmaṇi  |
jātāyāmapi vicchittau pūjāyāścaiva kāśyapa  | | 23.41  | |
aspṛśyasparśane jāte viṇmūtrarudhirādhirādibhiḥ  |
sparśanād dūṣaṇe jāte dhāmro bimbasya vā dvija  | | 23.42  | |
saṃprokṣaṇaṃ vidhātavyamiti śastrasya niścayaḥ  |
doṣāṇaṃ tāratamyena prokṣaṇaṃ strapanaṃ hareḥ  | | 23.43  | |
homaṃ ca kārayedvidvān śāntyarthaṃ sarvakarmaṇām  | |

iti pañcarātre mahopaniṣadi viśvāmitrasaṃhitāyāṃ
jīrṇoddhāravidhirnāma trayorviśo'dhyāyaḥ  | |

<24>
caturviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ
kāśyapaḥ
bhagavan vaiṣṇave yāge kriyamāṇe'pi nityaśaḥ  |
rājarāṣṭrādidoṣeṇa vighraścejjāyate guro  | | 24.1  | |
taddoṣapravighātāya kiṃ kāryaṃ mānavairbhuvi  |
mayyanugrahabuddhiścettanme vaktuṃ tvamarhasi  | | 24.2  | |
viśvāmitraḥ
ārādhane bhamavataḥ kriyamāṇe dvijottamaiḥ  |
mantralope kriyālope śāntyarthaṃ śrūyatāṃ vidhiḥ  | | 24.3  | |
sāṃvatsarikadoṣaughaprāyaścittaṃ vadāmyaham  |
pavitrāroṇaṃ nāma sarvapāpapraṇāśanam  | | 24.4  | |
varṣe varṣe tu kartavyamārādhanamidaṃ param  |
śrāvaṇe māsi vā kāryaṃ yadvā bhādrapade'pi vā  | | 24.5  | |
[aiṣvā(ṣā ?)dimāsāḥ sarvatra pavitrāropaṇaṃ na ca(?)]  |
nirmitān viprakanyābhistantūn kārpāsajānapi  | | 24.6  | |
kauśeyānathavā krītān samān doṣavivarjitān  |
mudramāṣādibhiścūrṇaiḥ saṃmiśraṃ kṣālayejjalaiḥ  | | 24.7  | |
śucibhiśca prasannaiśca śuddheṣu phalakādiṣu  |
nidhāya śoṣayedvidvānātape śodhi(ṣi ?)tāṃstataḥ  | | 24.8  | |
parīkṣya tṛṇaromāṇi nirasya ca samāhitaḥ  |
triguṇīkṛtya ca punastriguṇānapi kārayet  | | 24.9  | |
caturguṇīkṛtya ca tān mātuluṅgaphalopamān  |
vidhāya sthāpayet pūrvaṃ mṛtpātreṣu naveṣu ca  | | 24.10  | |
gomayālepite śuddhe bhūtale susame śubhe  |
pramāṇānuguṇaṃ śaṅkudvayaṃ jajñadrumodabhavam  | | 24.11  | |
nighāya śaṅkorāropya tadvatkuryātpravarta(vitra?)kam  |
strātaiḥ śuklāmbaradharairvedavidibhaḥ samāhitaiḥ  | | 24.12  | |
dhṛtordhvapuṇḍraiḥ kuśalairvaiṣṇavairavikatthanaiḥ  |
udaṅ‌mukhaiḥ prāṅmukhairvā kārayettu pavitrakam  | | 24.13  | |
sūtrairaṣṭhottaraśataṃ(?) dvātriṃśadgunthisaṃyutam  |
mūlaberādikānāṃ syādadhivāsārthamīdṛśām  | | 24.14  | |
maṇḍale pahmāmānaṃ syāt kumbhe tatkumbhāmānakam  |
mekhalāmānakaṃ kuṇḍe pavitramaghivāsakam  | | 24.15  | |
cakrabjamaṇḍalastheṣu pahmanābhyaranemiṣu  |
tatsnāni ca kuvīṃta pavitrāṇi vahirvṛte(tau?)  | | 24.16  | |
tatsamānaṃ pavitraṃ syād grannthitantuvidhiṃ śṛṇu  |
tantūṃścatuḥśataṃ viddhi granthayaḥ saptaviṃśatiḥ  | | 24.17  | |
pādonā tantusaṃkhyā syād diguṇā granthayo dvija  |
tantusaṃkhyā śatayugaṃ granthayastriguṇātma(tmi?)kāḥ  | | 24.18  | |
bāhmavṛtte(hmavṛtau?) tatstāśca catuḥśatamitāstathā  |
granthayaḥ pañcaguṇitāḥ kumṛbhasyāṣṭottaraṃ śatam  | | 24.19  | |
tantavo granthayaśca syuścaturśiṃśatisaṃkhyakāḥ  |
karake tanvaḥ kāryā ekāśītipramāṇakāḥ  | | 24.20  | |
saptaviṃśatisaṃkhyākā granthayaḥ karake'pi ca  |
kautukānāṃ pavitrāṇi daśa tāni kramācchṛṇu  | | 24.21  | |
pramāṇagandhālaṃkāravitratrayamuttamam  |
madhyamaṃ cādhamamiti trīṇi paścāt kirīṭakam  | | 24.22  | |
śrīvatsaṃ kaustubhaṃ caiva vanamālā trayaṃ tathā  |
itthaṃ daśapavitrāṇi tantugranthikramaṃ śṛṇu  | | 24.23  | |
nābhyantaṃ prathamaṃ sūtramaṣṭottaraśataṃ viduḥ  |
dvātriṃśat syād granthisaṃkhyā gandhākhyaṃ hṛdayāvadhi  | | 24.24  | |
aśītitantavastasya granthayaḥ pañcaviṃśatiḥ  |
alaṃkārapavitraṃ tu jaṅghāmadhyoktamāyatam(?)  | | 24.25  | |
tantavo'ṣṭottaraśataṃ dvātriṃśad granthayo matāḥ  |
uttamākhyāpavitrasya sūtrasaṃkhyā catuḥśatam  | | 24.26  | |
granthayoṣṭottaraśataṃ jānumadhyāntamāyatam(?)  |
madhyamasya ca tantūnāṃ śatāni trīṇi kāśyapa  | | 24.27  | |
aśītigranthayo jñeyā ūmadhyaṃ tadāyatiḥ(?)  |
adhamasya śate dve tu tantavaḥ parikīrtitāḥ  | | 24.28  | |
granthayastasya ṣaṣṭiḥ syurnābhimadhyaṃ tadāyatiḥ(?)  |
kirīṭākhyapavitrasya tantavaḥ syuścatuḥśatam  | | 24.29  | |
kirīṭameva mānaṃ syād dvātriṃśadgranyo'sya ca  |
śrīvatsākhyapavitrasya tantavaḥ syuścatuḥśatam  | | 24.30  | |
granthayaḥ saptaviṃśat (?) syādabhā(syurhā ?)rāntaṃ tatprakalpanam  |
kaustubhasya śate dve tu tanmūnāṃ parikīrtite  | | 24.31  | |
dvātriṃśad granthayaḥ kāryāḥ stanamadhyaṃ tadāyatiḥ(?)  |
vanamālāhvayasya syustanvo'ṣṭasahastrakam  | | 24.32  | |
pramāṇaṃ caraṇāyāmaṃ granthisaṃkhyā na vidyate  |
pīṭhasya ca pavitraṃ syāt pañcaviṃśatisūtrakam  | | 24.33  | |
daśabhirgranthibhiryuktaṃ tadāyāmasamanvitam  |
urdhyāpātre dhūpadīpapātre ghaṭākṣamālayoḥ  | | 24.34  | |
sarveṣvaṣṭottaraśataṃ sūtrāṇi granthayo'pi ca  |
yathāśobhaṃ prakalpyāḥ syurāyāmo dvādaśāṅgulaḥ  | | 24.35  | |
viṣvaksanenasya tārkṣyasya dikpālānāṃ ca kāśyapa  |
digdevatānāṃ ca tathā durgāvighreśayorapi  | | 24.36  | |
brahmaśaṃkarayoścāpi tantavo'ṣṭottaraṃ śatam  |
dvātriṃśad granthayaḥ proktā hastāyāmo(?) dvijottama  | | 24.37  | |
caṇḍādīnāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ saptaviṃśatisūtrakam  |
hastamānaṃ prakartavyaṃ yaśāśobhamapi dvija  | | 24.28  | |
dvāratoraṇakumbhānāṃ tadvadeva pavitrakam  |
karṇikāyāmasaṭṭaśaṃ baliṭhapavitrakam  | | 24.39  | |
ācāryāṇāṃ yatīnāṃ ca nābhyantaparilambitam  |
aṣṭottaraśataiḥ sūtrairbahubhirgranthibhiryutam  | | 24.40  | |
ṛtvijāṃ pūjakānāṃ ca vaiṣṇavānāṃ tathaiva ca  |
sūtrairekottarāśītimitairnābhyantalambitam  | | 24.41  | |
rājñāmaṣṭottaraśatai racitaṃ tantubhirdvija  |
nirmiṃtaṃ tūlagarbhaiśca granthibhiḥ śobhitaṃ yathā  | | 24.42  | |
nābhyantalambitaṃ kāryamanyeṣāmanujīvinām  |
ekaśītipramāṇaiśca sūtraiḥ kāryaṃ dvijottama  | | 24.43  | |
vaiśyadīnāṃ ca kurvīta caturviṃśatintubhiḥ  |
vibhave sati sarveṣāṃ pavitrāṇāṃ tu vistṛtiḥ(?)  | | 24.44  | |
maṇimuktāpravālādyailohaiḥ svarṇādibhistathā  |
tadabhāve ca tūlairvāgranthimadhyaṃ prapūrya ca  | | 24.45  | |
sūtrairgranthayantarālāni yathāśobhaṃ tu ra ñjayet  |
vibhave sati saṃkoco naiva kāryo vijānatā  | | 24.46  | |
pavitreṣu kṛteṣvevaṃ śāstracoditavartmanā  |
nikṣipya hemabhāṇḍeṣu mahatsu bahuṣu dvija  | | 24.47  | |
athavā mṛṇmayeṣveva śubheṣu śaṃciṣu dvija  |
vastrakhaṇḍaiśca vaktrāṇi sūtrairbadhvā ṭṭaḍhairapi  | | 24.48  | |
pūrvapakṣe daśamyāṃ ca sāyāhne mūrtipaiḥ saha  |
sahaiva yajamānena praviśya sadanaṃ hareḥ  | | 24.49  | |
ārādhayitvā deveśaṃ bhaktyā vijñāpayet svayam  |
praṇataḥ prāñjalirmūtvā gāthāmetāmudīrayet  | | 24.50  | |
bhagavan nṛhare tasmin nityapūjāvidhau tava  |
nyūnātirekajanitaduritotsāraṇakṣamam  | | 24.51  | |
sarvāsāṃ saṃpadāṃ caiva samutpādanahetukam  |
pavitrāroha(pa?)ṇaṃ nāma samārādhanamuttamam  | | 24.52  | |
cikīrṣābhi jagannātha vidhinā coditena tat  |
kārayitvā tvavighrena juṣasva dayayā mayi  | | 24.53  | |
itthaṃ vijñāpanaṃ kṛtvā praṇamyālayamadhyataḥ  |
nirgatya maṇṭapavaraṃ svanuliptaṃ khalaṃkṛtam  | | 24.54  | |
vitānadhvamālābhirdarbhamālāpariṣkṛtamam  |
dvāraparśveṣu(rśvasu?)vinyastapūrṇakumbhayugaṃ dvija  | | 24.55  | |
cakrābjamaṇḍalaṃ tatra kārayitvā vidhānataḥ  |
maṇḍalasya samīpe tu darbhānāstīrya deśikaḥ  | | 24.56  | |
āsīnaḥ prāṅmukhastiṣṭhan saṃkalpyovasettataḥ  |
jāgradeva nayettatra rātriṃ japaparastadā  | | 24.57  | |
ekādaśyāṃ niśādau tu kārayedadhivāsanam  |
pratiṣṭhoktena vidhinā vidhāyāṅkuraropaṇam  | | 14.58  | |
bandhanaṃ kautukasyaiva kṛtvā kalyāṇakautukam (ke?)  |
torāṇādyarcanaṃ kṛtvā maṇṭapaṃ digvidikṣu ca  | | 24.59  | |
ṛgādivedāṃścaturo dvijāgryairvevittamaiḥ  |
śakhāmekāyanīṃ cāpi vi?dvadibharabhighoṣya ca  | | 24.60  | |
bherīpaṭahakāṃsyādi ghoṣayitvā samantataḥ  |
kārayitvā ca gītādīn sarvamaṅgalasaṃyutam  | | 24.61  | |
śīlīnāṃ daśabhirbhāraiḥ kṛtvā māhendramaṇḍalam  |
pavitrabhāṇḍamakhilaṃ tatra saṃsthāpya deśikaḥ  | | 24.62  | |
puṇyāhadhoṣaṇaṃ kṛtvā dahanāpyāyanādikaiḥ  |
viśodhya dhūpayitvā tu kālāgarusamudabhavaiḥ  | | 24.63  | |
kalaśaiścandanādyaiśca ghanasāraiśca sarvataḥ  |
sarvato gandhapuṣpaiśca suśubhairabhiṣicya ca  | | 24.64  | |
kṣaumairaminavaistāni pātraṇyāveṣṭaya kāśyapa  |
cakramudrāṃ darśayitvā sabhājyāstreṇa maṇṭapam  | | 24.65  | |
saṃveṣṭye sūtrairīśānīṃ diśāmārabhya sarvataḥ  |
upariṣṭānmaṇṭapasya dhyātvāstreśaṃ sudarśanam  | | 24.66  | |
bhūtale sagadaṃ pahmaṃ pāñcajanyaṃ diśāsvapi  |
chidānveṣaṇadakṣāṇāṃ bhūtānāṃ śāntaye punaḥ  | | 24.67  | |
baliṃ māṣaudanenaiva vikīrya ca samantataḥ  |
strāpayitvautsavaṃ bimbamupavītāvasānakam  | | 24.68  | |
sabhājya maṇṭape caiva samānīya dvijottama  |
nivedya ca havistatra samāpyārādhanaṃ hareḥ  | | 24.69  | |
karkarīsahite kumbhe havirantaṃ sabhājya ca  |
havyavāhagataṃ devaṃ tarpayitvā hutena ca  | | 24.70  | |
pavitrabhūṣaṇaṃ sarvaṃ catuḥsthānasthitasya ca  |
pṛthak pṛthak pātragataṃ purato viniveśya ca  | | 24.71  | |
japannidaṃ viṣṇuritimantraṃ maṅgalasaṃyutam  |
āsādya mūlabimbaṃ tu pavitraṃ mūlamantrataḥ  | | 24.72  | |
tasmin vinyasya sarvatra kramādarcāsu deśikaḥ  |
pavitrabhūṣaṇanyāsaṃ vidhāya tadantaram  | | 24.73  | |
sūtraiśca pañcavarṇādyaiḥ(ḍhyaiḥ?)prāsādaṃ cāpi veṣṭayet  |
tāṃ rātriṃ sakalāṃ nītvā jāgradeva japādikaiḥ  | | 24.74  | |
prabhāte maṇḍapāgrasthaṃ nītvā strānārthavedikām  |
strāpayitvā ca vidhinā bhūṣayitvā tataḥ param  | | 24.75  | |
nītvā ca maṇḍalaṃ tatra pūjayeddīpapāścimam  |
kumbhādīnapi cābhyarcya tivedyānnaṃ caturvidham  | | 24.76  | |
hutvā ca pāyasādīni puṃsūktena pṛthak pṛthak  |
bahubhiḥ satilairlārdhūpairagarubhistataḥ  | | 24.77  | |
samidibharājyaihuyāt pṛthagaṣṭaśatāhutīḥ  |
sāṃvatsarokte subhage muhūrte deśikottamaḥ  | | 24.78  | |
maṇḍalaṃ bhūṣayetpūrvaṃ pavitrairmūṣāṇottamaiḥ  |
maṇḍalāṅgāni ca tathā pahmādīni yathātatham  | | 24.79  | |
kumbhādīnapi cābhyarcya nivedyānnaṃ caturviṇdham  |
kumbhasthe(mbhe ca ?) karake deve kuṇḍe vā toraṇādiṣu  | | 24.80  | |
tatkumbheṣu ca sarveṣu bhūṣaṇāni yathākramam (?)  |
maṇḍalasyaṃ jagannāthaṃ parītyātha pradakṣiṇam  | | 24.81  | |
sarvamaṅgalasaṃyuktaṃ mandirāntaḥ praveśya ca  |
strapanādinivedyānte vidhāya ca sabhājanam  | | 24.82  | |
mūlārcādīni sarvāṇi bimbānyapi pavitrakaiḥ  |
alaṃkṛtyātha nirgatya caṇḍādīnāmapi dvija  | | 24.83  | |
parivāragaṇānāṃ ca pavitrāṇi nidhāya ca  |
brahmādīnāpi tathā senānītārkṣyayorapi  | | 24.84  | |
balipīṭhāvasānānāṃ sarveṣāmapi kāśyapa  |
viṣṇugāyatriyā yadvā tathedaṃ viṣṇurityapi  | | 24.85  | |
aṣṭākṣareṇa vā yadvā dvādakṣaravidyayā  |
pavitrāṇi pradāyātha deśiko mūrtipairyutaḥ  | | 24.86  | |
praviśya viṣṇoḥ sadanaṃ yajamānaḥ samāhitaḥ  |
añjalau ratnanicayaṃ haimaṃ vā puṣpasaṃcayam  | | 24.87  | |
niveśya stotrapaṭhanaṃ kurvannaṃva padābjayoḥ  |
vikīrya tāni ratnāni haimāni kusumānyapi  | | 24.88  | |
manujānāmapi tathā pavitrāṇi kṛtāni ca  |
vastrāṇyani ca vijñāpya devaṃ gṛhṇīta deśikaḥ  | | 24.89  | |
ṛtvigbhyaḥ pūjakebhyaśca vedavidbhyaśca dāpayet  |
vihitāni pavitrāṇi ṭṭaṣṭvā devamukhāmbujam  | | 24.90  | |
vijñāpayedimāpaṃ gāthāṃ sarvadevakṣamāpiṇīm  |
devadeva jagannātha yadāmbhodhe mayā kṛtam  | | 24.91  | |
gṛhāṇārādhanamidaṃ sarvabhogaprapūraṇam  |
svātan?tryeṇa mayā naitadvihitaṃ jagatāṃ nidhe  | | 24.92  | |
kiṃ tu tvatprerimatiḥ kimu bānyat kṣamasva me  |
itthaṃ vijñāpya deveśaṃ praṇipatya kṣamāpayet  | | 24.93  | |
yajamānaśca gurave tasmin kāle paśūn bhuvaḥ  |
gṛhaṃ ca dhanadhānyāḍhyaṃ dadyāt prītena cesā  | | 24.94  | |
anyebhyaḥ sahakāribhyastattatkarmānurūpataḥ  |
deyā ca dakṣiṇā teṣāṃ manaḥprītiryathā bhavet  | | 24. 95  | |
aparāhṇe ca deveśāṃ nītvā dhāma pradakṣiṇam  |
sarvamaṅgalasaṃyuktaṃ mandirāntaḥ praveśayet  | | 24.96  | |
sapta trīn pañca divasān samatītyāvarohaṇam  |
pauruṣaṃ sūktamuccārya tathā sāma rathantaram  | | 24.97  | |
trisuparṇaṃ mūlamatha pavitrāṇyavaropayet  |
gurave tāni deyāni prīṇanārthaṃ madhudviṣaḥ  | | 24.98  | |
kumbhasthaṃ maṇḍalasthaṃ ca vahisthaṃ ca hariṃ punaḥ  |
visṛjya devasya tataḥ strapanaṃ cāpi kārayet  | | 24.99  | |
anena vidhinā vipra pavitrāroha(pa?)ṇaṃ hareḥ  |
kurvat phalānyanantāni prāprotyeva na saṃśayaḥ  | | 24.100  | |
anena vidhinā vipra kaṅlārakusumānyapi  |
tathā ?damanyāḥ patrāṇi devasyāropayed dvija  | | 24.101  | |
sāṃvatsarikabhogānāṃ pūraṇārthaṃ śriyaḥ pateḥ  | |

pa?vitrāropaṇavidhirnāma caturviśo'dhyāyaḥ  | |

<25>
pañcaviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ
kāśyapaḥ
bhagavan karuṇāvāsa kaṅlārakusubhārcanam  |
damanīdalapūjāṃ ca vaktumarhasi me guro  | | 25.1  | |
viśvāmitraḥ
kaṅlārakusumaiḥ pūjāṃ lakṣmībharturvadāmyaham  |
sāvadhānena manasayā tacchuṇu dvijapuṃgava  | | 25.2  | |
purāmbarīṣo nāmābhūdrājarṣirdhārmikottamaḥ  |
cakrakatīṃśvara[ścakre](?) sadvīpāṃ sāgārāmbarām  | | 25.3  | |
vijitya sakalāmuvīṃ sa rājā dharmataḥ prajāḥ  |
rañcayāmasa sakalāḥ prajā svā iva bhūpatiḥ  | | 25.4  | |
adarśanena kāryāṇāmanyeṣāṃ yajñapūruṣam  |
toṣayāmāsa bahubhiryajñaiḥ saṃpūrṇadikṣiṇaiḥ  | | 25.5  | |
ekonamaśvedhānāṃ śataṃ cakre mahāmanāḥ  |
kartuṃ śatatamaṃ yajñaṃ turaṃgaṃ savavisṛṣṭavān  | | 26.6  | |
khairaṃ carantamaśvaṃ tamātyairbahubhirvṛtam  |
paśyatāṃ rakṣiṇāṃ śakro gṛḍhamūrtirjahāra ha  | | 25.7  | |
rakṣiṇo'nviṣya sarvatra turagādirśanena [tam] (tu?)  |
viṣaṇṇavadanā bhūtvā bhūmipālaṃ vyajijñapan  | | 25.8  | |
paśyatā sāvadhānena(nā ?)a(pya ?)smākaṃ te (tu ?) turaṃgamaḥ  |
adarśanaṃ gato deva karaṇīyaṃ vada prabho  | | 25.9  | |
iti śutvā giraṃ teṣāmambarīṣo mahīpatiḥ  |
ṛtvikpurohitācāryānāhūyedaṃ caco'bravīt  | | 25.10  | |
madhye turaṃgamo naṣṭaḥ kiṃ kṛtyaṃ vadata dijāḥ  |
ityuktāste giraṃ cemācurdvijavarā dvija  | | 25.11  | |
anviṣyatāṃ dvijasutaḥ kaścit tena yajāmahe  |
ityukto bhūmipālastu dvijasūnuṃ svakairdhanaiḥ  | | 25.12  | |
[vikrayā(?)]nītavān mārge mayā nītaḥ kumārakaḥ  |
mahmaṃ nivedayāmāsa yāthṛttaṃ mayāpi saḥ  | | 25.13  | |
pālitaḥ śiṣyatāṃ prāptaḥ śunaḥśepaḥ sa vai dvijaḥ  |

śaṅkhakāhalatūryādinādayuktaṃ pṛthak pṛthak  |

ardhyādimudhaparkāntaṃ devaṃ ca strānavedikām  |

mahā?havaśca daśābhistaṇḍulānāṃ kṛtaṃ tathā  |
