vāhane devadeveśaṃ rathādāvadhiropya tam  | | 25.169  | |

tvaddattairbhoganicayaiḥsaṃpūrṇāṃ sarvakāmadām  | | 25.172  | |
iti vijñāpya deveśaṃ praṇipatya kṣamāpayet  |
tasmin kāle ca gurave yajamānastu dakṣiṇām  | | 25.173  | |
[dadyānniṣkaśataṃ svarṇakataṃ](?) yadvā tadardhakam  |
paśūn gṛhāṇi grāmāṃśca vastrāṇi vividhānyapi  | | 25.174  | |

bherīkāhalaśaṅkhādivādyadhoṣapuraḥsaram  |

praveśayet svakaṃ gaṃhaṃ sarvaṅgalasaṃyutam  | | 25.186  | |

iti pañcarātre mahopaniṣadi viśvāmitrasaṃhitāyāṃ
kahlārakusumāropaṇaṃ (ṇavidhiḥ?) nāma pañcaviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ  | |

ṣaḍviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ
viśvāmitraḥ
damanāropaṇavidhiṃ vakṣyāmi dvijasattama  |
yadanuṣṭhānato vipra aihikāmuṣmikaṃ phalam  | | 26.1  | |
avāpya cānte paramaṃ padamāproti mānavaḥ  |
caiva māse tu vaiśākhe sitapakṣe viśeṣataḥ  | | 26.2  | |
dvādaśyāṃ paurṇamāsyāṃ ca damanīdalapūjanam  |
kartavyaṃ tad dvijaśreṣṭha tatprakāro'dya vakṣyate  | | 26.3  | |
saṃkalpitadinātpūrvaṃ navame saptame'pi vā  |
pañcame vā tṛtīye vā divase'ṅkuraropaṇam  | | 26.4  | |
kārayitvā ca pūrvoktavidhānena mahāmune  |
ekādaśyāṃ prabhāte'tha sāndhaṃ karma samāpya ca  | | 26.5  | |
strātaḥ śuklāmbaradharaḥ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitaḥ  |
pavitritakaraḥ śuklavilepanavibhūṣitaḥ  | | 26.6  | |
gatvodyānaṃ sahartvigbhiryajamānena saṃyutaḥ  |
gatvā damanavāṭīṃ tu tasyāḥ paścimato mune  | | 26.7  | |
catuḥstambhāṃ prapāṃ kṛtvā vitānena pinadhya(hma ?) ca  |
saṃveṣṭayu darbhamālāṃ ca tasyādho vedikāṃ tataḥ  | | 26.8  | |
caturaśraṃ kalpayitvā gomayenāpalipya ca  |
tasyopari vinikṣipya vrīhibhāracatuṣṭayam  | | 26.9  | |
āstīrya tasyopari ca paṭamāstīrya taṇḍulān  |
tadardhānatha vistīrya tasyoparyambarordhvataḥ  | | 26.10  | |
tilāṃstadardhān vinyasya tadūrdhvaṃ(rdhve ?) vinyased ghaṭam  |
tantuveṣṭitasarvāṅgaṃ vinyastanavaratnaknam  | | 26.11  | |
kṣaumayugmena saṃveṣṭya kuśakūrcaṃ tu vinyaset  |
tāṭṭagvidhaṃ ca karakaṃ tasya dakṣiṇato nyaset  | | 26.12  | |
kumbhāṣṭakaṃ ca vinyasya tasya dikṣvaṣṭasu kramāt  |
kumbhe pradyumramabhyarcya karake ca sudarśanam  | | 26.13  | |
kumbhāṣṭake ca ?paritaḥ śakrādīnarcayet kramāt  |
pāyasānnaṃ nivedyātha cottare havyavāhanam  | | 26.14  | |
ādhāyājyena hutvātha cāṣṭottaraśatāhutīḥ  |
pradyumrenaiva mantreṇa paścāt kṣetreśamarcayet  | | 26.15  | |
pāyasānnena ca baliṃ dikṣavaṣṭasu vinikṣipet  |
ācāryo mūrtipaiḥ sārdhaṃ manuṃ pradyumramuccaran  | | 26.16  | |
lṛtvā damanapatrāṇi svarṇapātreṣu nikṣipet  |
tāni patrāṇi kumbhasya puna(raḥ) saṃsthāpya bhūtale  | | 26.17  | |
tadambhasā ca saṃprokṣya vāhayitvā dvijottamaiḥ  |
uccarañchākunaṃ sūktaṃ vahvṛcairdvijapuṃgava  | | 26.18  | |
anyairapi ca viprāgryaiḥ svaśākhāmukharānanaiḥ  |
saṃyutastūryaghoṣeṇa badhirīkṛtya digvadhūḥ  | | 26.19  | |
chatracāmaraketūnāṃ saṃghairācchādya cāmbaram  |
sarvamaṅgalasaṃyuktaṃ praviśya sadanaṃ hareḥ  | | 26.20  | |
devasya(vāya ?) darśayitvā ca puṣpamaṇḍapamānayet  |
śucimirdvijaryaiśca nipuṇairmālikādidhau  | | 26.21  | |
kahlāravatpramāṇena kalpenmālikāgaṇam  |
uttamādikrameṇaiva tattadbrerānusārataḥ  | | 26.22  | |
kumbhamaṇḍalakuṇḍānāṃ sthaulyaṃ hastapramāṇakam  |
caṇḍādīnāmapi tathā tārkṣyaseneśayorapi  | | 26.23  | |
kahlāradāmavaddairdhyaṃ sthaulyaṃ hastapramāṇakam  |
toraṇānāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ [damanīdalamālayā](?)  | | 26.24  | |

vitānaṃ parito mālāṃ lambayeddamanīṃ śubhām  | | 26.25  | |
mandiraṃ maṇḍapaṃ caiva veṣṭayeddamanītrajā  |
damanīdāmabhiḥ kuryādvimānamapi maṇḍapam  | | 26.26  | |
sthūṇādamanālabhirveṣṭayettāsu lambayet (?)  |
maṇḍapaṃ paritaḥ kuryādudyānamiva pūrvavat  | | 26.27  | |

tasyaiśānye ca digbhāge kugbhaṃ dhānyasya saṃcaye  | | 26.28  | |

satantukaṃ sakauśeyaṃ saratnaṃ ca sapallavam  |

nālikerī(?)mātuluṅgapūgīphalayutānyapi  |

maṇḍalottaradigbhāge vahlikuṇḍaṃ vidhāya ca  |

satantubhiśca sakṣaubherantarvinyastaratnakaiḥ  |

caturṣu dvādeśeṣu maṇḍapasya pārśvayoḥ  |
ghaṭānāṣṭau yathāpūrvaṃ tantubhiḥ saṃvṛtānapi  | | 26.42  | |
pratyekaṃ vastrayugmena veṣṭayitvā saratnakān  |
sthāpayet kalaśān samyak pūrvasyāṃ diśa kāśyā  | | 26.43  | |
pratyekaṃ dhānyapīṭheṣu śatamaṣṭottaraṃ tu vā  |
ekaśītimathaikonapañcāśatamathāpi vā  | | 26.44  | |
pūritān gandhatoyena sakūrcan sāpidhānākan  |

ātape śoṣayitvā ca viniyuñjyād dvijottama  |

sugandhinā candanena viliptāṅgraḥ subhūṣitaḥ  |

āropya śibikāṃ dhāma paribhrāmya pradakṣiṇam  |
ānīya maṇḍapaṃ devamāropyottamaviṣṭare  | | 26.53  | |

krameṇānena badhrīyāt kautukānāṃ catuṣṭayam  |
dvāradaivatapūrvaṃ ca pūjāṃ nirvartya diśikaḥ  | | 26.56  | |
kalaśānarcayitvā ca niśācūrṇaghaṭaṃ tataḥ  |
kalaśottaradigbhāge tāḍuladroṇamūrdhani  | | 26.57  | |
nihitaṃ kūrcasahitaṃ madhyaveṣṭitavastrakam  |

kumbhāntaḥsthaṃ jagannāthaṃ naivedyāntaṃ samarcya ca  |

devaṃ sabhājya tadanu mantrāsanavidhānataḥ  |
nivedya madhuparkaṃ ca vediṃ samadhiropya ca  | | 26.61  | |
tatrāpyārādhya kalaśaiḥ strāpayitvā vidhānataḥ  |
alaṃkṛtya jagannātha nivedyānnaṃ caturvidham  | | 26.62  | |
mukhavāsasamāyuktaṃ tāmbūlaṃ saṃnivedya ca  |
vahlimadhyagataṃ devamabhyarcya vidhinā tataḥ  | | 26.63  | |
samidibhaḥ saptabhirhutvā damanaṃ ghṛtasaṃplatam  |
aṣṭottarasahastraṃ ca hutvā pūrṇāṃ vidhāya ca  | | 26.64  | |
deveśamarcayitvā ca havanaṃ vidhinā kṛtam  |
indrādīnāṃ ca purato baliṃ ?datyāṣṭadikṣvapi  | | 26.65  | |
adhivāsanāmālāṃ ca kumbhamaṇḍalayo?rapi  |
saśaktikasya devasya mūlaberādikasya ca  | | 26.66  | |
ekaikāṃ saṃniveśyātha śayane ca jagadgurum  |
śayayitvā ca saṃprāśya hutaśeṣaṃ caruṃ tataḥ  | | 26.67  | |
tadādibhirvinodaiśca svadhyāyapaṭhanaiḥ saha  |
geyanṛttādibhiścaiva jāgareṇa niśāṃ nayet  | | 26.68  | |
tataḥ prabhāte sustrātaḥ paridhāyāṃśukaṃ navam  |
yathāpūrvamalaṃkṛtya maṇḍapāntaḥ praviśya ca  | | 26.69  | |
kṛtvā nyāsādisakalamardhyādi parikalpya ca  |
dvāreṣu pūjanaṃ kṛtvā tiṣṭhannāsīna eva vā  | | 26.70  | |
arcayitvā ca sakalān kumbhamāṇḍalagaṃ vibhum  |
ārādhya ca nivedyāntamagrisaṃsthaṃ jagadgurum  | | 26.71  | |
sabhājayitvā devaṃ ca saṃpūjya strāpayet kramāt  |
alaṃkṛtya tato devaṃ nipedya ca māhaviḥ  | | 26.72  | |
devyo?rapi nivedyānnaṃ pāyasādi caturvidham  |
mūlaberādibinbeṣu saviśeṣaṃ sabhājanam  | | 26.73  | |
vidhāya ca nivedyānte hutvā saptasamitadgraṇam  |
muhūrte śobhane prāpte yajamānasukhāvahe  | | 26.74  | |
kumbhe ca maṇḍale caiva kuṇḍe cāryāsvapi dvija  |
mūladiṣu samasteṣu devyorapi pṛthak pṛthak  | | 26.65  | |
pradāya damanīmālāṃ devaṃ vijñāpya deśikaḥ  |
kumbhamaṇḍalavahlisthaṃ devamuddhāsya vai dvija  | | 26.66  | |
dhānyapātraṇi sarvāṇi maṅgalāṣṭakameva ca  |
phalāni ca samastani darśyāyitvā ca viṣṇave  | | 26.67  | |
ācāryāya pradādyācca dhānyasaṃcayameva ca  |
kṣaumāṇāṃ nicayaṃ sarvamanyaccānītamatr?ā ca  | | 26.68  | |
tatsarvaṃ deśikaḥ kṛtvā cātmasāt svagṛhaṃ nayet  |
deva(bera ?)mutsavasaṃjñaṃ ca vāhane viniveśya ca  | | 26.79  | |
sarvālaṃkārasaṃyuktaṃ paribhrāmya pradakṣiṇam  |
praveśayedālayāntaḥ svasthāne viniveśayet  | | 26.80  | |
yajamānena sahitaḥ ṛtvigbhirapi deśikaḥ  |
praṇamya devadevasya pādabjayugalaṃ dvija  | | 26.81  | |
gṛhītvā pāṇiyugmena devaṃ vijñāpayedidam  |
atsavaṃ devadeveśa damanīdalakalpitam  | | 26.82  | |
asmāsu dayayā deva gṛhāṇa parameśvara  |
iti vijñāpanaṃ kṛtvā sarvadevādhidaivatam  | | 26.83  | |
prasādya devadeveśaṃ vardhasayedenamāśiṣā  |
yajamatāno'pi gurave dakṣiṇāṃ pratidāpayet  | | 26.84  | |
śataniṣkaṃ tadardhaṃ vā vāsāṃsi vividhāni ca  |
ratnormikāḥ paśūn kāmān gṛhāṇi sumāhantyapi  | | 26.85  | |
kṣamāpayet saṃpradāya ṛtvimagbhaśca tadardhakam  |
parivāraka?viprāṇāmanyeṣāṃ sahakāriṇām  | | 26.86  | |
sadasyānāṃ sevakānāṃ gītanṛttaratātmanām  |
bheryādivādakānāṃ ca tattatkarmānurūpataḥ  | | 26.87  | |
pradāpayeddhanagaṇaṃ sastraudhaṃ dhānyasaṃcayam  |
vitīrya bhojayedvidvān yathāśakti mahāmate  | | 26.88  | |
kārayet prativarṣaṃ yo damanīpatrapūjanam  |
devasya vāsavudevasya śraddhayā parayā yutaḥ  | | 26.89  | |
tasya vidhyanti sakalāḥ saṃpadastatprasādataḥ  |
iha prāpya paramṛddhiṃ vitṝnuttāryaṃ sarvataḥ  | | 26.89  | |
pūrpānapyapa(va ?)rān sapta svadehasya samantataḥ  |
prāpruyādvaiṣṇavaṃ dhāma sarvayogīndradurlabham  | | 26.90  | |

iti pañcarātre mahopaniṣadi viśvāmitrasaṃhitāyāṃ
damanāropaṇavidhirnāma ṣaṅviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ  | |

<27>
saptaviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ
viśvāmitraḥ
śṛṇu kāśyapa sarveṣāṃ prāyaścittaṃ ca karmaṇām  |
yāgakarmasu sarveṣu vihīneṣu dvijarṣabha  | | 27.1  | |
prāyaśrittaṃ vidhātavyamiti śāstrasya niśrayaḥ  |
prāsadanāśe saṃjāte kṛtvā bālālayaṃ budhaḥ  | | 27.2  | |
tatra vinyasya karmārcāṃ kārayitvā yathāpuram  |
ālayaṃ tatra devasya pratiṣṭhāṃ saṃvidhāya ca  | | 27.3  | |
puṇyāhaṃ kārayitvātha saṃprokṣya ca jagadgurum  |
snāpayitvoktamārgeṇa brāhmaṇān bhojayedapi  | | 27.4  | |
pīṭhaviśleṣaṇe jāte lākṣāsajjarasaṃ tathā  |
gugmuluṃ kuruvindaṃ ca gulaṃ(ḍaṃ?) tailaṃ ca gairikam  | | 27.5  | |
aṣṭabhāgābhivṛddhaistu kramādebhiḥ suveṣṭitaiḥ  |
saṃśleṣya suṭṭaḍhaṃ kṛtvā vālukābhiśca pūrayet  | | 27.6  | |
sarvāṅgaṣu ca devasya candanai[rnavapantakaiḥ ](?)  |
karpūragārukāśmīrahimāmbhomiśritairdvija  | | 27.7  | |
saṃlipya veṣṭayitvā ca navakṣaumayute(ge?)na ca  |
puṇyāhavācanaṃ kṛtvā vidhāya snapanaṃ hareḥ  | | 27.8  | |
svatantre ?ceddiśāhomaṃ kṛtvā homaṃ sabhājya ca  |
bhojayitvā dvijāgryāṃśca dakṣiṇāṃ dāpayedapi  | | 27.9  | |
vinṣṭe maṇṭape kṛtvā maṇṭapaṃ tadyathāpūram  |
kārayitvā ca puṇyāhaṃ strāpayitvā hariṃ tataḥ  | | 27.10  | |
hutvā ca mūlamantreṇa śatamaṣṭādhikaṃ tathā  |
bhojayitvā dvijāṃstatra dadyāttebhyaśca dakṣiṇām  | | 27.11  | |
prāsādadvārabhaṅge vā saṃbhave'yaṃ (?) vidhirbhavet  |
salmīkadarśanenai(cai?)va prāsādasyāntare dvija  | | 27.12  | |
āmūlāt khanayitvā taṃ pradeśaṃ pūrayedapi  |
valmīkamṛdibhaḥ śuddhābhiḥ kārayet strapanādikam  | | 27.13  | |
ālaye'śanipāte ca vahlidāhe ca pūrvavat  |
kārayitvā pradeśaṃ taṃ saṃprokṣya strapanaṃ tathā  | | 27.14  | |
kārayitvā vaiṣṇavānāṃ bhojanādīni dāpayet  |
nadīstretaḥsamāvegāt patite dhāmri kāśyapa  | | 27.15  | |
varjayitvā tu taṃ deśaṃ śubhe deśe ca mandiram  |
kārayitvā pratiṣṭhāpya tatra devaṃ śriyaḥ patim  | | 27.16  | |
pratiṣṭhoktena vidhinā śeṣamanyat samācaret  |
[pratiṣṭhāhīberasya sthāpanaṃ sārvakālikam] (?)  | | 27.17  | |
[tatpūrvaṃ brāhmaṇavidhiṃ gṛhītvā sthāpayedapi] (?)  |
kālatipātaśāntyarthaṃ hutvā cāṣṭasahastrakam  | | 27.18  | |
nayanonmīlanādanyat kṛtvā saṃsthāpayecca tam  |
pratimāyāstu patane jāte sadyaḥ samācaret  | | 27.19  | |
kuṇḍamaṇḍalahīne tu kārayet punareva tat  |
hutvā jitaṃ ta(te?)mantreṇa śatamaṣṭādhikaṃ caret  | | 27.20  | |
mahādhvajavihīne tu cihlo vā patite'pi vā  |
patitaṃ samyagādāya cihlaṃ saṃtyajya deśikaḥ  | | 27.21  | |
punaranyat sthāpayitvā huneda(?)ṣṭamahasrakam  |
strapanādi yathāpūrvaṃ śeṣamanyat samācaret  | | 27.22  | |
dhvajatoraṇahānau tu panuḥ kṛtvā tu taddvikam  |
puṇyāhavācanastrānaprabhṛtyanyat samācaret  | | 27.23  | |
śūlasthāpanahīnaṃ cecdūlaṃ kuryātpunaḥ sthiram  |
satnanyāsavihīnaṃ cet kṛtvā ratnādhivāsanam  | | 27.24  | |
kṛtvā ca pīṭhamūle tu nikṣipya suṭṭaḍhaṃ yathā  |
kārayitvā ca sudhayā strapanādi samācaret  | | 27.25  | |
aṅkurārpaṇahīnaṃ cedaṅkurānarpayet punaḥ  |
aprarūḍhaṃ yadi tyājyaṃ raktaṃ śyāme ca vāpayet  | | 27.26  | |
sarvatra strapanādīni kārayecca vicakṣaṇaḥ  |
aṣṭamaṅgalahīnaṃ cet kārayitvā punardvija  | | 27.27  | |
puṇyāhavācanaṃ kṛtvā prokṣayet kuśayariṇā  |
samārādhanīhīne tu kālameṃkaṃ madhudviṣaḥ  | | 27.28  | |
dviguṇaṃ kārayeddhīmān naivedyāvāhanādikam  |
dvikālahānau strapanaṃ navakumbhairvidhāya ca  | | 27.29  | |
dviguṇaṃ pūjayeccaiva havirādi ca kārayet  |
ekapūjanahānau ca daśabhiḥ saptabhiśśa(śca ?)tam  | | 27.30  | |
kalaśaiḥ strāpayitvā ca havanādi māmācaret  |
tadūrdhvaṃ pañcadivasaparyantaṃ pañcaviṃśatim  | | 27.31  | |
kalaśaṃ sthāpayitvā ca strapanādīn samācaret  |
punardaśāhaparyantaṃ pañcāśadibhardhaṭairdvija  | | 27.32  | |
deveśaṃ strāpayitvā ca havanādi samācaret  |
tadūrdhvaṃ māsaparyantamekāśītighaṭairdvija  | | 27.33  | |
vidhāya strapanaṃ śeṣaṃ yathāpūrvaṃ samācaret  |
māsaṣaṭkādhike hīne samārādhanakarmaṇi  | | 27.34  | |
saṃprokṣaṇaṃ vidhātavyamiti śāstrasya niścayaḥ  |
abdatridvayahīne ca pratiṣṭhatāṃ kārayed dvija  | | 27.35  | |
agrikārye punahīne punaḥ saṃskāramācaret  |
hutvā cāṣṭottaraśataṃ vyāhṛtībhirdhaṭairapi  | | 27.36  | |
navabhiḥ strāpayeddevaṃ śeṣaṃ pūrvavadācaret  |
utsavāgrau virāhite sandhātikramaṇe'pi ca  | | 27.37  | |
pañcaviṃśaddhaṭajalaiḥ(?) strāpayitvā hariṃ prabhum  |
puṇyāhavācanādīni yathāpūvāṃ samācaret  | | 27.38  | |
sandhyātikramaṇe'pyeṣa vidhiḥ syād dvijapuṃgava  |
avinīte gurau sarvaṃ karma tena na kārayet  | | 27.39  | |
anyamānīya ca guraṃ mīcīnaṃ samācaret  |
ajñānādyatkṛtaṃ karma tadguruśca samācaret  | | 27.40  | |
balipradānahānau ca baliṃ kuryādyathāpuram  |
[aṣṭottaraśatairhomai[rmūlamantreṇa kārayet  | | 27.41  | |
ekakālaṃ balerhānau dviguṇaṃ ca baliṃ haret  |
kuryācca pūrvavaccheṣamiti santaḥ pracakṣate  | | 27.42  | |
vihīne struk?struve(?)caiva strukstruvaṃ(vau ?) pravidhāya ca  |
homastrapanamukhyāni yathāpūrvaṃ tu kārayet  | | 27.43  | |
raktastrīdarśane dhāmri [khātvā(?)taṃ deśamāvaseḥ](?)  |
sikatābhiśca saṃpūrya pañcagavyaiḥ samurakṣya ca  | | 27.44  | |
ekāśītighaṭastrānaṃ dināni trīṇi pañca ca  |
puṇyāhaṃ ca samādhoṣya bhojayed brāhmaṇānati  | | 27.45  | |
dine dine ca juhuyādaṣṭottarasahastrakam  |
caṇḍālaprabhṛtīnāṃ ca hīnānāṃ darśane sati  | | 27.46  | |
śuddhāmbhasā kuśāgreṇa prokṣayitvā prabhuṃ dvija  |
huneda(?)ṣṭottaraśatamaṣṭārṇena dvijottama  | | 27.47  | |
sūtikāśaucayuktaiścāpyantyajaiḥ sparśane sati  |
ekāśītighaṭaḥ strāpya śeṣaṃ pūrvavadācaret  | | 27.48  | |
corādibhirgalairvāpi bimboddhāre kṛte sati  |
pūrvavatkārayitvā ca pratiṣṭhāmapi sattama  | | 27.49  | |
diśāhomaṃ ca mantrasya strānaṃ tyaktvā samācaret  |
pratiṣṭhoktakramāccaiva(merṇave ?) strapanādi samacaret  | | 27.50  | |
[saṃprokṣaṇe vidhirayaṃ prokto dvijavarātmaja (?)]  |
ya itthamācaredyastu(?) prāyaścittaṃ samāhitaḥ  | | 27.51  | |
sarvān kāmānihāvāpya prāprotyante paraṃ padam  |
iti te sarvamākhyātaṃ yanmayā brahmaṇo mukhāt  | | 27.52  | |
śrutaṃ tasmāt samuddhṛtya sāraṃ saṃkṣepato mune  |
pañcarātrāmṛtāmbhodhiṃ mathitvā sāsaṃgrahaḥ  | | 27.53  | |
prokto mayāyaṃ tasmāttvametacchrutvāvadhārya ca  |
anena vidhinā devaṃ prapannārtiharaṃ harim  | | 27.54  | |
samārādhaya devaṃ tvaṃ prāyaścittātmanaḥ padam  |
guhmād guhmatamaṃ ma(ta ?)ntraṃ sārātsāratīraṃ mune  | | 27.55  | |
saptaviṃśatibhiḥ proktamadhyāyaiḥ kaśyapātmaja  |
viścāmitrīyanāmraitanmayoktatvāt prathāṃ gatam  | | 27.56  | |
nāśiṣyāya pradātavyaṃ na saṃsāranivāsine  |
vaiṣṇavāya pradātavyamupasannāya dhīmate  | | 27.57  | |
iti śrutvā muniśveṣṭhaḥ kāśyapaḥ suprasannadhīḥ  |
praṇamya bhagavantaṃ taṃ viśvāmitraṃ taponidhim  | | 27.58  | |
kṛtāñjalipuṭo bhūtvā dhyāyan nārāyaṇaṃ prabhum  |
khamāśramaṃ samāsādya bhaktiyuktena cetasā  | | 27.59  | |
tyaktanyasarvavyāsaṃgaḥ vāṅmanaḥkarmabhiḥ sadā  |
pañcakālarato bhūtvā samārādhya śriyaḥ patim  | | 27.60  | |
avāpya (pa?) paramaṃ dhāma yato nāvartate punaḥ  | |

iti pañcarātre mahopani?ṣadi viśvāmitrasaṃhitāyāṃ
prāyaścittavidhirnāma saptaviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ  | |
